逐项课程来源 · 真实实验记录

架构：Transformer → ViT → Swin

生成：VAE → VQ-VAE / VQGAN → DDPM → DDIM → CFG → Latent Diffusion → DiT

表征：DINO → iBOT → DINOv2 → Registers → DINOv3；与MAE对照阅读

注意：自监督DINO与目标检测DINO不同；MAE不是VAE。 完整指南 · 前置知识教程 · PyTorch小实验 · CSV清单 · BibTeX · 翻译阅读指南

31 · 2024-02 · 原表 #01 综述与理论

大型视觉语言模型幻觉综述

A Survey on Hallucination in Large Vision-Language Models

Hanchao Liu, Wenyuan Xue, Yifei Chen et al.
arXiv 预印本（元数据未标注录用）

界定 LVLM 幻觉概念与表现形式，汇总专用评测基准与缓解方法，并讨论其相较于纯文本幻觉的独有挑战。

展开中英文摘要与表格信息

中文摘要（原表译文）

大型视觉语言模型（LVLMs）的近期发展因其落地应用潜力而在人工智能领域受到越来越多的关注。然而，"幻觉"——更准确地说，是事实性视觉内容与相应文本生成之间的失配——构成了使用 LVLMs 的重大挑战。在本篇综述中，我们剖析与 LVLM 相关的幻觉，以建立整体认识并推动未来的缓解研究。我们的考察首先厘清 LVLMs 中幻觉的概念，呈现多种幻觉症状，并强调 LVLM 幻觉所固有的独特挑战。随后，我们梳理了专为评测 LVLMs 特有幻觉而设计的基准与方法论。此外，我们深入探究这些幻觉的根源，涵盖来自训练数据与模型组件的洞见。我们还对现有的幻觉缓解方法进行了批判性评述。本文最后讨论了与 LVLMs 幻觉相关的开放问题与未来方向。

English abstract

Recent development of Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs) has attracted growing attention within the AI landscape for its practical implementation potential. However, ``hallucination'', or more specifically, the misalignment between factual visual content and corresponding textual generation, poses a significant challenge of utilizing LVLMs. In this comprehensive survey, we dissect LVLM-related hallucinations in an attempt to establish an overview and facilitate future mitigation. Our scrutiny starts with a clarification of the concept of hallucinations in LVLMs, presenting a variety of hallucination symptoms and highlighting the unique challenges inherent in LVLM hallucinations. Subsequently, we outline the benchmarks and methodologies tailored specifically for evaluating hallucinations unique to LVLMs. Additionally, we delve into an investigation of the root causes of these hallucinations, encompassing insights from the training data and model components. We also critically review existing methods for mitigating hallucinations. The open questions and future directions pertaining to hallucinations within LVLMs are discussed to conclude this survey.

完整作者
Hanchao Liu, Wenyuan Xue, Yifei Chen, Dapeng Chen, Xiutian Zhao, Ke Wang, Liping Hou, Rongjun Li, Wei Peng

32 · 2024-03 · 原表 #02 综述与理论

视觉幻觉：定义、量化与处方性纠正

Visual Hallucination: Definition, Quantification, and Prescriptive Remediations

Anku Rani, Vipula Rawte, Harshad Sharma et al.
arXiv 预印本（元数据未标注录用）

面向图像描述与 VQA 归纳出八类视觉幻觉倾向（上下文猜测、身份错配、视觉错觉等），并提出可操作的量化与纠正方案。

展开中英文摘要与表格信息

中文摘要（原表译文）

幻觉令人不安的增长或许是负责任人工智能进步的最大障碍。近来，大量研究集中于检测与缓解大型语言模型（LLMs）中的幻觉。然而值得注意的是，幻觉在视觉语言模型（VLMs）中同样相当普遍。本文基于两个任务——i) 图像描述，ii) 视觉问答（VQA）——对 VLM 幻觉进行了细粒度刻画。我们划分出视觉幻觉的八种细粒度倾向：i) 上下文猜测、ii) 身份错配、iii) 地理错误、iv) 视觉错觉、v) 性别异常、vi) VLM 充当分类器、vii) 误读、viii) 数值偏差。我们构建了 Visual HallucInation eLiciTation（VHILT）数据集，公开可用，包含由八个 VLM 在两个任务（图像描述与 VQA）上生成的 2000 个样本，并附有上述类别的标注。

English abstract

The troubling rise of hallucination presents perhaps the most significant impediment to the advancement of responsible AI. In recent times, considerable research has focused on detecting and mitigating hallucination in Large Language Models (LLMs). However, it's worth noting that hallucination is also quite prevalent in Vision-Language models (VLMs). In this paper, we offer a fine-grained discourse on profiling VLM hallucination based on two tasks: i) image captioning, and ii) Visual Question Answering (VQA). We delineate eight fine-grained orientations of visual hallucination: i) Contextual Guessing, ii) Identity Incongruity, iii) Geographical Erratum, iv) Visual Illusion, v) Gender Anomaly, vi) VLM as Classifier, vii) Wrong Reading, and viii) Numeric Discrepancy. We curate Visual HallucInation eLiciTation (VHILT), a publicly available dataset comprising 2,000 samples generated using eight VLMs across two tasks of captioning and VQA along with human annotations for the categories as mentioned earlier.

完整作者
Anku Rani, Vipula Rawte, Harshad Sharma, Neeraj Anand, Krishnav Rajbangshi, Amit Sheth, Amitava Das
发表时间

33 · 2024-04 · 原表 #03 综述与理论

申博清单 #01 · 综述 / 问题定义

多模态大模型幻觉综述

Hallucination of Multimodal Large Language Models: A Survey

Zechen Bai, Pichao Wang, Tianjun Xiao et al.
arXiv 预印本（元数据未标注录用）

系统梳理 MLLM 幻觉的成因、评测方法与缓解手段，指出幻觉是制约其落地部署的主要障碍。

展开中英文摘要与表格信息

中文摘要（原表译文）

本综述对多模态大模型（MLLMs，亦称大型视觉语言模型 LVLMs）中的幻觉现象进行了全面分析。这类模型在多模态任务上已取得显著进展与出色能力。尽管发展势头良好，MLLMs 仍经常生成与视觉内容不一致的输出，即所谓"幻觉"，这给其实际部署带来重大障碍，也引发人们对其真实场景可靠性的担忧。该问题已引起越来越多的关注，推动了检测与缓解此类不准确输出的研究。我们回顾了在识别、评测与缓解幻觉方面的最新进展，详细梳理了幻觉的成因、评测基准、指标以及为应对该问题而发展的各种策略。此外，我们分析了当前的挑战与局限，并提出勾勒未来研究可能路径的开放性问题。通过对幻觉成因、评测基准与缓解方法进行细粒度分类与全景式梳理，本综述旨在加深对 MLLMs 幻觉的理解，并推动该领域的进一步发展。通过这一深入细致的综述，我们为提升 MLLMs 鲁棒性与可靠性的持续讨论做出贡献，并为研究者与实践者提供有价值的见解与资源。资源见：https://github.com/showlab/Awesome-MLLM-Hallucination 。

English abstract

This survey presents a comprehensive analysis of the phenomenon of hallucination in multimodal large language models (MLLMs), also known as Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs), which have demonstrated significant advancements and remarkable abilities in multimodal tasks. Despite these promising developments, MLLMs often generate outputs that are inconsistent with the visual content, a challenge known as hallucination, which poses substantial obstacles to their practical deployment and raises concerns regarding their reliability in real-world applications. This problem has attracted increasing attention, prompting efforts to detect and mitigate such inaccuracies. We review recent advances in identifying, evaluating, and mitigating these hallucinations, offering a detailed overview of the underlying causes, evaluation benchmarks, metrics, and strategies developed to address this issue. Additionally, we analyze the current challenges and limitations, formulating open questions that delineate potential pathways for future research. By drawing the granular classification and landscapes of hallucination causes, evaluation benchmarks, and mitigation methods, this survey aims to deepen the understanding of hallucinations in MLLMs and inspire further advancements in the field. Through our thorough and in-depth review, we contribute to the ongoing dialogue on enhancing the robustness and reliability of MLLMs, providing valuable insights and resources for researchers and practitioners alike. Resources are available at: https://github.com/showlab/Awesome-MLLM-Hallucination.

完整作者
Zechen Bai, Pichao Wang, Tianjun Xiao, Tong He, Zongbo Han, Zheng Zhang, Mike Zheng Shou

34 · 2024-05 · 原表 #04 综述与理论

语言/图像/视频/音频基础模型幻觉综合综述

A Comprehensive Survey of Hallucination in Large Language, Image, Video and Audio Foundation Models

Pranab Sahoo, Prabhash Meharia, Akash Ghosh et al.
EMNLP 2024 (Findings)

跨文本、图像、视频、音频四类基础模型综合梳理幻觉识别与缓解的最新进展。

展开中英文摘要与表格信息

中文摘要（原表译文）

基础模型（FMs）在语言、图像、音频与视频等领域的快速进展，已在多样任务上展现出非凡能力。然而，FMs 的普及也带来一个关键挑战：可能产生幻觉输出，在高风险应用中尤为突出。基础模型生成幻觉内容的倾向，可以说是其在真实场景中被广泛采用的最大障碍，在可靠性与准确性至上的领域尤其如此。本综述全面梳理了旨在识别与缓解 FMs 幻觉问题的最新进展，涵盖文本、图像、视频与音频模态。通过综合各模态在幻觉检测与缓解方面的最新成果，本文旨在为研究者、开发者与实践者提供有价值的见解。实质上，它为多模态基础模型中的幻觉问题建立了一个涵盖定义、分类体系与检测策略的清晰框架，为这一关键领域的后续研究奠定了基础。

English abstract

The rapid advancement of foundation models (FMs) across language, image, audio, and video domains has shown remarkable capabilities in diverse tasks. However, the proliferation of FMs brings forth a critical challenge: the potential to generate hallucinated outputs, particularly in high-stakes applications. The tendency of foundation models to produce hallucinated content arguably represents the biggest hindrance to their widespread adoption in real-world scenarios, especially in domains where reliability and accuracy are paramount. This survey paper presents a comprehensive overview of recent developments that aim to identify and mitigate the problem of hallucination in FMs, spanning text, image, video, and audio modalities. By synthesizing recent advancements in detecting and mitigating hallucination across various modalities, the paper aims to provide valuable insights for researchers, developers, and practitioners. Essentially, it establishes a clear framework encompassing definition, taxonomy, and detection strategies for addressing hallucination in multimodal foundation models, laying the foundation for future research in this pivotal area.

完整作者
Pranab Sahoo, Prabhash Meharia, Akash Ghosh, Sriparna Saha, Vinija Jain, Aman Chadha

35 · 2024-10 · 原表 #05 综述与理论

申博清单 #02 · 综述 / 问题定义

多模态基础模型幻觉：成因、纠正与评测综述

Hallucination in Multimodal Foundation Models: A Survey on Causes, Corrections, and Evaluations

Yinghao Guo, Wei Lan, Wenyi Chen et al.
arXiv 预印本（元数据未标注录用）

从模型架构与数据质量两方面归类幻觉成因，并构建纠正与评测方法的完整分类体系。

展开中英文摘要与表格信息

中文摘要（原表译文）

多模态基础模型代表了人工智能的一次重大飞跃。其中，大型视觉语言模型（LVLMs）是这类基础模型的典型代表，它把视觉模态直接集成到大型语言模型（LLMs）之中，在信息处理与生成方面展现出强大能力。然而，幻觉的存在限制了 LVLM 在各领域的潜力与实际效果。尽管已有大量工作致力于幻觉的缓解与纠正，但对其进行总结的综述仍然很少。为填补这一空白，本综述对 LVLMs 中的幻觉图景进行了系统梳理。我们将与模型架构和数据质量相关的成因加以归类，并构建了现有缓解策略的完整分类体系。此外，我们从判别式与生成式两个视角批判性地评估了当前的幻觉评测基准，指出了现有指标的局限。本文最后讨论了开放挑战与未来研究方向，以提升 LVLMs 的可靠性与可信度。

English abstract

Multimodal Foundation Models represent a significant leap in artificial intelligence. Among them, Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs) serve as the typical representative of these foundation models, which integrate visual modality directly into Large Language Models (LLMs). They have demonstrated strong capabilities in information processing and generation. However, the existence of hallucinations has limited the potential and practical effectiveness of LVLM in various fields. Although lots of work has been devoted to hallucination mitigation and correction, there are few reviews to summarize them. To address this gap, this survey provides a systematic review of the hallucination landscape in LVLMs. We categorize the causes related to model architecture and data quality, and construct a comprehensive taxonomy of existing mitigation strategies. Furthermore, we critically assess current hallucination evaluation benchmarks from both discriminative and generative perspectives, highlighting the limitations of existing metrics. This survey concludes by discussing open challenges and future research directions to advance the reliability and trustworthiness of LVLMs.

完整作者
Yinghao Guo, Wei Lan, Wenyi Chen, Qingfeng Chen, Shichao Zhang, Shirui Pan, Huiyu Zhou, Yi Pan

36 · 2026-04 · 原表 #06 综述与理论

失真还是捏造？视频大模型幻觉综述

Distorted or Fabricated? A Survey on Hallucination in Video LLMs

Yiyang Huang, Yitian Zhang, Yizhou Wang et al.
ACL 2026 (Findings)

提出「动态失真／内容捏造」二分分类体系，系统评述视频 LLM 幻觉的基准、指标与干预策略，并归因于时序表征能力不足。

展开中英文摘要与表格信息

中文摘要（原表译文）

尽管视频-语言建模取得了显著进展，幻觉仍是视频大语言模型（Vid-LLMs）中持续存在的挑战，指那些看似合理却与输入视频内容相矛盾的输出。本综述对 Vid-LLMs 中的幻觉进行了全面分析，并提出一套系统分类体系，将其划分为两个核心类型：动态失真与内容捏造，每类各含两个子类型并附代表性案例。基于该分类，我们评述了幻觉评测与缓解的最新进展，涵盖关键基准、指标与干预策略。我们进一步分析了动态失真与内容捏造的根源，它们往往源于时序表征能力有限与视觉接地不足。这些洞见引出了若干有前景的未来方向，包括开发运动感知的视觉编码器以及引入反事实学习技术。本综述整合了分散的进展，以促进对 Vid-LLMs 幻觉的系统理解，为构建鲁棒可靠的视频-语言系统奠定基础。相关工作的最新整理列表维护于 https://github.com/hukcc/Awesome-Video-Hallucination 。

English abstract

Despite significant progress in video-language modeling, hallucinations remain a persistent challenge in Video Large Language Models (Vid-LLMs), referring to outputs that appear plausible yet contradict the content of the input video. This survey presents a comprehensive analysis of hallucinations in Vid-LLMs and introduces a systematic taxonomy that categorizes them into two core types: dynamic distortion and content fabrication, each comprising two subtypes with representative cases. Building on this taxonomy, we review recent advances in the evaluation and mitigation of hallucinations, covering key benchmarks, metrics, and intervention strategies. We further analyze the root causes of dynamic distortion and content fabrication, which often result from limited capacity for temporal representation and insufficient visual grounding. These insights inform several promising directions for future work, including the development of motion-aware visual encoders and the integration of counterfactual learning techniques. This survey consolidates scattered progress to foster a systematic understanding of hallucinations in Vid-LLMs, laying the groundwork for building robust and reliable video-language systems. An up-to-date curated list of related works is maintained at https://github.com/hukcc/Awesome-Video-Hallucination .

完整作者
Yiyang Huang, Yitian Zhang, Yizhou Wang, Mingyuan Zhang, Liang Shi, Huimin Zeng, Yun Fu

37 · 2023-10 · 原表 #07 幻觉机理与检测

申博清单 #19 · 缓解方法 · 训练期

分析并缓解 LVLM 的物体幻觉（LURE）

Analyzing and Mitigating Object Hallucination in Large Vision-Language Models

Yiyang Zhou, Chenhang Cui, Jaehong Yoon et al.
ICLR 2024

统计分析指出共现、不确定性、物体位置是物体幻觉三大成因，并提出后置修正器 LURE 重写低幻觉描述。

展开中英文摘要与表格信息

中文摘要（原表译文）

大型视觉语言模型（LVLMs）在结合人类语言理解视觉信息方面展现出卓越能力。然而 LVLMs 仍受物体幻觉困扰，即生成包含图像中实际并不存在的物体的描述。这会负面影响视觉摘要与推理等诸多视觉语言任务。为解决该问题，我们提出一种简单而强大的算法 LVLM Hallucination Revisor（LURE），通过重建幻觉更少的描述来事后纠正 LVLMs 的物体幻觉。LURE 建立在对物体幻觉背后关键因素的严谨统计分析之上，包括共现（某些物体常与其他物体同时出现于图像中）、不确定性（在 LVLM 解码过程中不确定性较高的物体）与物体位置（幻觉常出现在生成文本的靠后部分）。LURE 可无缝集成到任意 LVLMs 中。我们在六个开源 LVLMs 上评测 LURE，在通用物体幻觉评测指标上较此前最佳方法提升 23%。在 GPT 评测与人工评测中，LURE 均稳定名列前茅。数据与代码见 https://github.com/YiyangZhou/LURE 。

English abstract

Large vision-language models (LVLMs) have shown remarkable abilities in understanding visual information with human languages. However, LVLMs still suffer from object hallucination, which is the problem of generating descriptions that include objects that do not actually exist in the images. This can negatively impact many vision-language tasks, such as visual summarization and reasoning. To address this issue, we propose a simple yet powerful algorithm, LVLM Hallucination Revisor (LURE), to post-hoc rectify object hallucination in LVLMs by reconstructing less hallucinatory descriptions. LURE is grounded in a rigorous statistical analysis of the key factors underlying object hallucination, including co-occurrence (the frequent appearance of certain objects alongside others in images), uncertainty (objects with higher uncertainty during LVLM decoding), and object position (hallucination often appears in the later part of the generated text). LURE can also be seamlessly integrated with any LVLMs. We evaluate LURE on six open-source LVLMs, achieving a 23% improvement in general object hallucination evaluation metrics over the previous best approach. In both GPT and human evaluations, LURE consistently ranks at the top. Our data and code are available at https://github.com/YiyangZhou/LURE.

完整作者
Yiyang Zhou, Chenhang Cui, Jaehong Yoon, Linjun Zhang, Zhun Deng, Chelsea Finn, Mohit Bansal, Huaxiu Yao

38 · 2024-02 · 原表 #08 幻觉机理与检测

逻辑闭环：用模型自身揭示物体幻觉

Logical Closed Loop: Uncovering Object Hallucinations in Large Vision-Language Models

Junfei Wu, Qiang Liu, Ding Wang et al.
ACL 2024

利用「真实物体回答逻辑自洽、幻觉物体逻辑矛盾」的特性，无需外部检测器即可检测物体幻觉。

展开中英文摘要与表格信息

中文摘要（原表译文）

物体幻觉一直是阻碍大型视觉语言模型（LVLMs）更广泛应用的关键弱点。物体幻觉指 LVLMs 声称图像中存在并不存在的物体。为缓解物体幻觉，已有指令微调与基于外部模型的检测方法，但它们要么需要大规模计算资源，要么依赖外部模型的检测结果。然而，利用 LVLM 自身来缓解物体幻觉仍是一个探索不足的方向。本工作采用这样一个直觉：LVLM 对存在的物体倾向于给出逻辑一致的回应，而对幻觉物体则不一致。因此我们提出基于逻辑闭环的物体幻觉检测与缓解框架 LogicCheckGPT。具体地，我们设计了逻辑一致性探查，提出具有逻辑关联的问题，从物体询问其属性、反之亦然。二者的回答能否构成逻辑闭环，可作为物体幻觉的指示器。作为一种即插即用的方法，它可无缝应用于所有现有 LVLMs。在四个 LVLMs、三个基准上的全面实验表明我们的方法带来显著提升，体现了其有效性与通用性。

English abstract

Object hallucination has been an Achilles' heel which hinders the broader applications of large vision-language models (LVLMs). Object hallucination refers to the phenomenon that the LVLMs claim non-existent objects in the image. To mitigate the object hallucinations, instruction tuning and external model-based detection methods have been proposed, which either require large-scare computational resources or depend on the detection result of external models. However, there remains an under-explored field to utilize the LVLM itself to alleviate object hallucinations. In this work, we adopt the intuition that the LVLM tends to respond logically consistently for existent objects but inconsistently for hallucinated objects. Therefore, we propose a Logical Closed Loop-based framework for Object Hallucination Detection and Mitigation, namely LogicCheckGPT. In specific, we devise logical consistency probing to raise questions with logical correlations, inquiring about attributes from objects and vice versa. Whether their responses can form a logical closed loop serves as an indicator of object hallucination. As a plug-and-play method, it can be seamlessly applied to all existing LVLMs. Comprehensive experiments conducted on three benchmarks across four LVLMs have demonstrated significant improvements brought by our method, indicating its effectiveness and generality.

完整作者
Junfei Wu, Qiang Liu, Ding Wang, Jinghao Zhang, Shu Wu, Liang Wang, Tieniu Tan

39 · 2024-06 · 原表 #09 幻觉机理与检测

物体定位真能减少 LVLM 的幻觉吗？

Does Object Grounding Really Reduce Hallucination of Large Vision-Language Models?

Gregor Geigle, Radu Timofte, Goran Glavaš
arXiv 预印本（元数据未标注录用）

指出此前「加入 grounding 目标可降低幻觉」的结论建立在有缺陷的评测协议之上，严格再评测后对该论断提出质疑。

展开中英文摘要与表格信息

中文摘要（原表译文）

大型视觉语言模型（LVLMs）近期大幅推进了图像描述与诸多图像理解任务（如视觉问答）的技术水平。然而 LVLMs 经常产生幻觉，输出的描述会提及图像中找不到的概念。这些幻觉侵蚀了 LVLMs 的可信度，可以说是其被广泛采用的主要障碍之一。近期工作提出，加入接地目标——即显式对齐图像区域或物体与文本片段的目标——可减少 LVLM 幻觉的数量。这一说法虽符合直觉，却缺乏实证依据：我们认为，此前确立的"幻觉减少"效果是建立在有缺陷的评测协议之上，这些协议 (i) 依赖在 LVLM 训练中被大量使用的数据（即 MSCOCO），且 (ii) 通过问答而非开放式描述生成来衡量幻觉。与上述工作相反，本工作在更能真实反映开放生成中 LVLM 幻觉的评测协议下，首次系统分析了细粒度物体接地对 LVLM 幻觉的影响。在三类主干 LLM 上的大量实验表明，接地目标对开放描述生成中的物体幻觉几乎没有影响。

English abstract

Large vision-language models (LVLMs) have recently dramatically pushed the state of the art in image captioning and many image understanding tasks (e.g., visual question answering). LVLMs, however, often \textit{hallucinate} and produce captions that mention concepts that cannot be found in the image. These hallucinations erode the trustworthiness of LVLMs and are arguably among the main obstacles to their ubiquitous adoption. Recent work suggests that addition of grounding objectives -- those that explicitly align image regions or objects to text spans -- reduces the amount of LVLM hallucination. Although intuitive, this claim is not empirically justified as the reduction effects have been established, we argue, with flawed evaluation protocols that (i) rely on data (i.e., MSCOCO) that has been extensively used in LVLM training and (ii) measure hallucination via question answering rather than open-ended caption generation. In this work, in contrast, we offer the first systematic analysis of the effect of fine-grained object grounding on LVLM hallucination under an evaluation protocol that more realistically captures LVLM hallucination in open generation. Our extensive experiments over three backbone LLMs reveal that grounding objectives have little to no effect on object hallucination in open caption generation.

完整作者
Gregor Geigle, Radu Timofte, Goran Glavaš

2024-06

40 · 2024-06 · 原表 #10 幻觉机理与检测

多模态幻觉「雪球效应」的研究与缓解（MMHalSnowball）

Investigating and Mitigating the Multimodal Hallucination Snowballing in Large Vision-Language Models

Weihong Zhong, Xiaocheng Feng, Liang Zhao et al.
ACL 2024

提出 MMHalSnowball 框架，证明先前生成的幻觉会持续误导后续回答（即便真实视觉信息仍在），并给出缓解方法。

展开中英文摘要与表格信息

中文摘要（原表译文）

尽管在结合人类语言理解视觉信息方面较为先进，大型视觉语言模型（LVLMs）仍受多模态幻觉困扰。一个自然的忧虑是：在多模态交互过程中，已生成的幻觉可能影响 LVLMs 后续的生成。因此我们提出一个问题：当面对与先前生成的幻觉相关的查询时，即便真实的视觉信息就在眼前，LVLMs 是否会被误导而作出错误回答？为回答该问题，我们提出 MMHalSnowball 框架，用于评测 LVLMs 在遭遇已生成幻觉时的行为：LVLMs 需要在一段精心构造的含幻觉对话中回答特定的视觉问题。关键在于，我们的实验显示开源 LVLMs 的性能至少下降 31%，表明 LVLMs 倾向于接受已生成的幻觉，并作出若不受干扰本不会支持的错误陈述。我们将该现象称为多模态幻觉雪球效应。为缓解此问题，我们进一步提出一种免训练方法——残差视觉解码，用由残差视觉输入导出的分布来修正 LVLMs 的输出分布，使模型能够直接获取视觉信息。实验表明，我们的方法可缓解超过 24% 的雪球式多模态幻觉，同时保持原有能力。

English abstract

Though advanced in understanding visual information with human languages, Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs) still suffer from multimodal hallucinations. A natural concern is that during multimodal interaction, the generated hallucinations could influence the LVLMs' subsequent generation. Thus, we raise a question: When presented with a query relevant to the previously generated hallucination, will LVLMs be misled and respond incorrectly, even though the ground visual information exists? To answer this, we propose a framework called MMHalSnowball to evaluate LVLMs' behaviors when encountering generated hallucinations, where LVLMs are required to answer specific visual questions within a curated hallucinatory conversation. Crucially, our experiment shows that the performance of open-source LVLMs drops by at least $31\%$, indicating that LVLMs are prone to accept the generated hallucinations and make false claims that they would not have supported without distractions. We term this phenomenon Multimodal Hallucination Snowballing. To mitigate this, we further propose a training-free method called Residual Visual Decoding, where we revise the output distribution of LVLMs with the one derived from the residual visual input, providing models with direct access to the visual information. Experiments show that our method can mitigate more than $24\%$ of the snowballed multimodal hallucination while maintaining capabilities.

完整作者
Weihong Zhong, Xiaocheng Feng, Liang Zhao, Qiming Li, Lei Huang, Yuxuan Gu, Weitao Ma, Yuan Xu, Bing Qin

41 · 2025-04 · 原表 #11 幻觉机理与检测

性能提升的幻象：对比解码为何没能真正缓解物体幻觉

The Mirage of Performance Gains: Why Contrastive Decoding Fails to Mitigate Object Hallucinations in MLLMs?

Hao Yin, Guangzong Si, Zilei Wang
arXiv 预印本（元数据未标注录用）

揭示对比解码在 POPE 上的提升主要来自输出分布的单向粗暴调整与采样退化为贪心两个误导因素，并非真正减少幻觉。

展开中英文摘要与表格信息

中文摘要（原表译文）

对比解码策略被广泛用于减少多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）中的物体幻觉。这类方法通过构造对比样本来诱发幻觉，然后在输出分布中对其加以抑制。然而本文证明，此类方法无法有效缓解幻觉问题。在 POPE 基准上观察到的性能提升主要由两个误导性因素驱动：(1) 对模型输出分布的粗糙、单向调整；(2) 自适应合理性约束，它使采样策略退化为贪心搜索。为进一步说明这些问题，我们提出一系列伪造提升的方法，并与对比解码技术进行性能对比。实验结果显示，对比解码中观察到的性能增益与其缓解幻觉的预期目标完全无关。我们的发现挑战了关于对比解码策略有效性的常见假设，并为开发真正有效的 MLLMs 幻觉解决方案铺路。

English abstract

Contrastive decoding strategies are widely used to reduce object hallucinations in multimodal large language models (MLLMs). These methods work by constructing contrastive samples to induce hallucinations and then suppressing them in the output distribution. However, this paper demonstrates that such approaches fail to effectively mitigate the hallucination problem. The performance improvements observed on POPE Benchmark are largely driven by two misleading factors: (1) crude, unidirectional adjustments to the model's output distribution and (2) the adaptive plausibility constraint, which reduces the sampling strategy to greedy search. To further illustrate these issues, we introduce a series of spurious improvement methods and evaluate their performance against contrastive decoding techniques. Experimental results reveal that the observed performance gains in contrastive decoding are entirely unrelated to its intended goal of mitigating hallucinations. Our findings challenge common assumptions about the effectiveness of contrastive decoding strategies and pave the way for developing genuinely effective solutions to hallucinations in MLLMs.

完整作者
Hao Yin, Guangzong Si, Zilei Wang

42 · 2025-05 · 原表 #12 幻觉机理与检测

LVLM 视觉物体幻觉的全面剖析

A Comprehensive Analysis for Visual Object Hallucination in Large Vision-Language Models

Liqiang Jing, Guiming Hardy Chen, Ehsan Aghazadeh et al.
arXiv 预印本（元数据未标注录用）

逐一拆解 LLaVA 类模型的 LLM 主干、视觉骨干与投影器，定位各自幻觉来源并分别给出缓解手段。

展开中英文摘要与表格信息

中文摘要（原表译文）

大型视觉语言模型（LVLMs）在多模态任务上展现出卓越能力，但视觉物体幻觉仍是一个持续存在的问题。它指模型根据查询输入生成不准确的视觉物体相关信息，可能导致错误信息并引发对安全性与可靠性的担忧。此前的工作侧重视觉幻觉的评测与缓解，但其深层成因尚未得到全面研究。本文分析 LLaVA 类 LVLMs 的各个组件——大型语言模型、视觉骨干与投影器——以识别潜在的误差来源及其影响。基于我们的观察，我们针对每个存在问题的组件提出了缓解幻觉的方法。此外，我们构建了两个幻觉基准：侧重属性与关系幻觉的 QA-VisualGenome，以及侧重基于认知的幻觉的 QA-FB15k。

English abstract

Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs) demonstrate remarkable capabilities in multimodal tasks, but visual object hallucination remains a persistent issue. It refers to scenarios where models generate inaccurate visual object-related information based on the query input, potentially leading to misinformation and concerns about safety and reliability. Previous works focus on the evaluation and mitigation of visual hallucinations, but the underlying causes have not been comprehensively investigated. In this paper, we analyze each component of LLaVA-like LVLMs -- the large language model, the vision backbone, and the projector -- to identify potential sources of error and their impact. Based on our observations, we propose methods to mitigate hallucination for each problematic component. Additionally, we developed two hallucination benchmarks: QA-VisualGenome, which emphasizes attribute and relation hallucinations, and QA-FB15k, which focuses on cognition-based hallucinations.

完整作者
Liqiang Jing, Guiming Hardy Chen, Ehsan Aghazadeh, Xin Eric Wang, Xinya Du

43 · 2025-07 · 原表 #13 幻觉机理与检测

从认知心理学视角解读 VLM 幻觉

Investigating VLM Hallucination from a Cognitive Psychology Perspective: A First Step Toward Interpretation with Intriguing Observations

Xiangrui Liu, Man Luo, Agneet Chatterjee et al.
arXiv 预印本（元数据未标注录用）

提出心理学式分类（谄媚、逻辑不一致、新发现的「诉诸权威」），把 VLM 幻觉与人类认知偏差相类比。

展开中英文摘要与表格信息

中文摘要（原表译文）

幻觉是视觉语言模型（VLMs）中一个长期被积极研究的问题。现有研究通常将幻觉归因于技术局限或谄媚偏差，后者指模型倾向于生成迎合用户期望的错误答案。然而这些解释主要聚焦于技术性或外部驱动因素，可能忽视了这样一种可能：幻觉行为或许映射出人类心理学中观察到的认知偏差。本工作提出一个心理学分类体系，对导致 VLMs 幻觉的认知偏差进行归类，包括谄媚、逻辑不一致，以及一个新识别出的 VLM 行为：诉诸权威。为系统分析这些行为，我们设计了 AIpsych——一个可扩展的基准，用于揭示模型回答模式中的心理倾向。借助该基准，我们研究了模型架构与参数规模的变化如何影响模型在回应策略性操纵问题时的行为。实验表明，随着模型规模增大，VLMs 表现出更强的谄媚倾向但权威偏差减弱，说明能力在提升，而回答的诚实性可能被侵蚀。一项人类被试研究进一步验证了我们的假设，并凸显了 VLMs 与人类受访者之间的关键行为差异。本工作为理解 VLMs 幻觉提供了新视角，并强调将心理学原理纳入模型评测的重要性。

English abstract

Hallucination is a long-standing problem that has been actively investigated in Vision-Language Models (VLMs). Existing research commonly attributes hallucinations to technical limitations or sycophancy bias, where the latter means the models tend to generate incorrect answers to align with user expectations. However, these explanations primarily focus on technical or externally driven factors, and may have neglected the possibility that hallucination behaviours might mirror cognitive biases observed in human psychology. In this work, we introduce a psychological taxonomy, categorizing VLMs' cognitive biases that lead to hallucinations, including sycophancy, logical inconsistency, and a newly identified VLMs behaviour: appeal to authority. To systematically analyze these behaviours, we design AIpsych, a scalable benchmark that reveals psychological tendencies in model response patterns. Leveraging this benchmark, we investigate how variations in model architecture and parameter size influence model behaviour when responding to strategically manipulated questions. Our experiments reveal that as model size increases, VLMs exhibit stronger sycophantic tendencies but reduced authority bias, suggesting increasing competence but a potential erosion of response integrity. A human subject study further validates our hypotheses and highlights key behavioural differences between VLMs and human respondents. This work suggests a new perspective for understanding hallucination in VLMs and highlights the importance of integrating psychological principles into model evaluation.

完整作者
Xiangrui Liu, Man Luo, Agneet Chatterjee, Hua Wei, Chitta Baral, Yezhou Yang

44 · 2026-09 · 原表 #14 幻觉机理与检测

幻觉揭示了什么？用对比解码探针诊断视觉定位失效（SAFE）

What Do Hallucinations Reveal About Multimodal Reasoning? Diagnosing Visual Grounding Failures via Contrastive Decoding Probes

Zhipeng Zhao, Wenxu Wang, Peishun Liu et al.
EMNLP 2026

提出免训练 SAFE 框架，对比「视觉接地／视觉消融」两条生成路径得到 token 级接地分数，既检测视觉未接地的 token 又用于解码惩罚。

展开中英文摘要与表格信息

中文摘要（原表译文）

当强大多模态模型广泛可用时，进展需要超越基准分数的新科学方法论——把模型当作理解行为的工具。为此我们提出：能否将大型视觉语言模型（LVLMs）作为实验工具来研究其自身的失败动力学？围绕视觉幻觉，我们提出 SAFE——一个免训练的解码框架，它对比"视觉接地"与"视觉消融"两条生成路径，产出 token 级的对比接地分数，用以识别模型何时更倾向语言先验而非视觉证据。该信号具有双重作用：既可作为检测视觉未接地 token 的实用代理，又可作为解码期惩罚的依据。我们的分析得出三点实证观察：视觉依赖性随生成过程衰减；幻觉在时间上成簇共现；提前干预可减少成簇现象，而不会显著损害流畅性。在 MMHalBench 上，SAFE 大幅优于所有对比基线；在其他数据集上结果则较为混杂。我们认为，设计对比探针体现了一项更宏大的使命：把模型用作科学理解的工具。代码：https://github.com/zhaozhipeng1997/SAFE_public 。

English abstract

When strong multimodal models are widely available, progress requires new scientific methodologies beyond benchmark scores---using models as instruments for understanding behavior. We address this by asking: can we use large vision-language models (LVLMs) as experimental instruments for studying their own failure dynamics? Focusing on visual hallucination, we introduce SAFE, a training-free decoding framework that contrasts visually-grounded and vision-ablated generation paths to produce a token-level contrastive grounding score that identifies when the model favors linguistic priors over visual evidence. This signal serves dual roles: as a practical proxy for detecting visually-ungrounded tokens, and as the basis for decoding-time penalties. Our analysis yields three empirical observations: visual dependency decays over generation, hallucinations co-occur in temporal clusters, and early intervention reduces clustering without substantially degrading fluency. On MMHalBench, SAFE substantially outperforms all compared baselines; results elsewhere are more mixed. We argue that designing contrastive probes exemplifies a broader mission: using models as instruments for scientific understanding. Code: https://github.com/zhaozhipeng1997/SAFE_public.

完整作者
Zhipeng Zhao, Wenxu Wang, Peishun Liu, Ruichun Tang

45 · 2023-10 · 原表 #15 图像幻觉基准与评测

申博清单 #06 · 图像侧评测基准

HallusionBench：语言幻觉与视觉错觉耦合诊断套件

HallusionBench: An Advanced Diagnostic Suite for Entangled Language Hallucination and Visual Illusion in Large Vision-Language Models

Tianrui Guan, Fuxiao Liu, Xiyang Wu et al.
CVPR 2024

用 346 图 1129 问的对照式题目量化评测 LVLM 的图文推理与失败模式，GPT-4V 等先进模型题目对准确率仅 31.42%。

展开中英文摘要与表格信息

中文摘要（原表译文）

我们提出 HallusionBench，一个用于评测图像-上下文推理的综合基准。该基准通过强调对视觉数据的细致理解与解读，对 GPT-4V(Vision)、Gemini Pro Vision、Claude 3、LLaVA-1.5 等先进大型视觉语言模型（LVLMs）构成重大挑战。基准包含 346 张图像与 1129 个问题，全部由人类专家精心设计。我们为这些视觉问题引入了一种旨在建立对照组的新颖结构，该结构使我们能够定量分析模型的回答倾向、逻辑一致性以及各类失败模式。在 HallusionBench 上我们评测了 15 个不同模型，其中表现最好的 GPT-4V 仅取得 31.42% 的题目对准确率；值得注意的是，其他所有被评测模型的准确率均低于 16%。我们的分析不仅揭示了语言幻觉与视觉错觉等被观察到的失败模式，也加深了对这些陷阱的理解。HallusionBench 中详尽的案例研究揭示了 LVLMs 中幻觉与错觉所带来的挑战，并基于这些洞见提出了未来改进的可能路径。基准与代码见 https://github.com/tianyi-lab/HallusionBench 。

English abstract

We introduce HallusionBench, a comprehensive benchmark designed for the evaluation of image-context reasoning. This benchmark presents significant challenges to advanced large visual-language models (LVLMs), such as GPT-4V(Vision), Gemini Pro Vision, Claude 3, and LLaVA-1.5, by emphasizing nuanced understanding and interpretation of visual data. The benchmark comprises 346 images paired with 1129 questions, all meticulously crafted by human experts. We introduce a novel structure for these visual questions designed to establish control groups. This structure enables us to conduct a quantitative analysis of the models' response tendencies, logical consistency, and various failure modes. In our evaluation on HallusionBench, we benchmarked 15 different models, highlighting a 31.42% question-pair accuracy achieved by the state-of-the-art GPT-4V. Notably, all other evaluated models achieve accuracy below 16%. Moreover, our analysis not only highlights the observed failure modes, including language hallucination and visual illusion, but also deepens an understanding of these pitfalls. Our comprehensive case studies within HallusionBench shed light on the challenges of hallucination and illusion in LVLMs. Based on these insights, we suggest potential pathways for their future improvement. The benchmark and codebase can be accessed at https://github.com/tianyi-lab/HallusionBench.

完整作者
Tianrui Guan, Fuxiao Liu, Xiyang Wu, Ruiqi Xian, Zongxia Li, Xiaoyu Liu, Xijun Wang, Lichang Chen, Furong Huang, Yaser Yacoob, Dinesh Manocha, Tianyi Zhou

46 · 2023-11 · 原表 #16 图像幻觉基准与评测

申博清单 #07 · 图像侧评测基准

AMBER：无需大模型的多维幻觉评测基准

AMBER: An LLM-free Multi-dimensional Benchmark for MLLMs Hallucination Evaluation

Junyang Wang, Yuhang Wang, Guohai Xu et al.
arXiv 预印本（元数据未标注录用）

提出低成本、无需大模型打分的多维基准，同时覆盖生成与判别任务下的存在、属性、关系三类幻觉。

展开中英文摘要与表格信息

中文摘要（原表译文）

尽管在多模态任务上取得了显著进展，当前的多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）仍面临幻觉这一重大挑战，可能带来有害后果。因此，评测 MLLMs 的幻觉在模型改进与实际应用部署中正变得日益重要。已有工作在评测成本高（如依赖人工或先进 LLMs）以及评测维度不足（如任务类型与幻觉类型）方面存在局限。本文提出无需 LLM 的多维基准 AMBER，可用于评测生成式任务与判别式任务，涵盖存在性、属性与关系三类幻觉。基于 AMBER，我们设计了一套低成本高效的评测流水线。此外，我们对包括 GPT-4V(ision) 在内的主流 MLLMs 进行了全面评测与细致分析，并给出了缓解幻觉的指导性建议。AMBER 的数据与代码见 https://github.com/junyangwang0410/AMBER 。

English abstract

Despite making significant progress in multi-modal tasks, current Multi-modal Large Language Models (MLLMs) encounter the significant challenge of hallucinations, which may lead to harmful consequences. Therefore, evaluating MLLMs' hallucinations is becoming increasingly important in model improvement and practical application deployment. Previous works are limited in high evaluation costs (e.g., relying on humans or advanced LLMs) and insufficient evaluation dimensions (e.g., types of tasks and hallucinations). In this paper, we propose an LLM-free multi-dimensional benchmark AMBER, which can be used to evaluate both generative task and discriminative task including existence, attribute and relation hallucination. Based on AMBER, we design a low-cost and efficient evaluation pipeline. Additionally, we conduct a comprehensive evaluation and detailed analysis of mainstream MLLMs including GPT-4V(ision), and also give guideline suggestions for mitigating hallucinations. The data and code of AMBER are available at https://github.com/junyangwang0410/AMBER.

完整作者
Junyang Wang, Yuhang Wang, Guohai Xu, Jing Zhang, Yukai Gu, Haitao Jia, Jiaqi Wang, Haiyang Xu, Ming Yan, Ji Zhang, Jitao Sang

47 · 2024-05 · 原表 #17 图像幻觉基准与评测

THRONE：面向自由生成的物体幻觉基准

THRONE: An Object-based Hallucination Benchmark for the Free-form Generations of Large Vision-Language Models

Prannay Kaul, Zhizhong Li, Hao Yang et al.
CVPR 2024

聚焦开放式自由生成中的幻觉（Type I），指出其与选择题式幻觉（Type II）往往呈反相关，选择题降幻觉不等于自由生成降幻觉。

展开中英文摘要与表格信息

中文摘要（原表译文）

缓解大型视觉语言模型（LVLMs）的幻觉仍是一个未解问题。近期的基准并未处理开放式自由形式回答中的幻觉，我们称之为"Type I 幻觉"；它们关注的是针对非常特定问题格式的回答中的幻觉——通常是关于某个特定物体或属性的选择题回答——我们称之为"Type II 幻觉"。此外，这类基准往往需要调用外部模型 API，而这些 API 可能发生变化。在实践中我们观察到，Type II 幻觉的减少并不会带来 Type I 幻觉的减少，相反这两类幻觉常常呈反相关。为此，我们提出 THRONE，一个新颖的、基于物体的自动化框架，用于定量评测 LVLM 自由形式输出中的 Type I 幻觉。我们使用公开的语言模型来识别 LVLM 回答中的幻觉并计算有信息量的指标。通过在公开数据集上评测大量近期 LVLMs，我们表明现有指标的改善并不会带来 Type I 幻觉的减少，而且用于衡量 Type I 幻觉的既有基准并不完整。最后，我们提供一种简单有效的数据增强方法作为强基线，可同时减少 Type I 与 Type II 幻觉。代码见 https://github.com/amazon-science/THRONE 。

English abstract

Mitigating hallucinations in large vision-language models (LVLMs) remains an open problem. Recent benchmarks do not address hallucinations in open-ended free-form responses, which we term "Type I hallucinations". Instead, they focus on hallucinations responding to very specific question formats -- typically a multiple-choice response regarding a particular object or attribute -- which we term "Type II hallucinations". Additionally, such benchmarks often require external API calls to models which are subject to change. In practice, we observe that a reduction in Type II hallucinations does not lead to a reduction in Type I hallucinations but rather that the two forms of hallucinations are often anti-correlated. To address this, we propose THRONE, a novel object-based automatic framework for quantitatively evaluating Type I hallucinations in LVLM free-form outputs. We use public language models (LMs) to identify hallucinations in LVLM responses and compute informative metrics. By evaluating a large selection of recent LVLMs using public datasets, we show that an improvement in existing metrics do not lead to a reduction in Type I hallucinations, and that established benchmarks for measuring Type I hallucinations are incomplete. Finally, we provide a simple and effective data augmentation method to reduce Type I and Type II hallucinations as a strong baseline. Code is now available at https://github.com/amazon-science/THRONE .

完整作者
Prannay Kaul, Zhizhong Li, Hao Yang, Yonatan Dukler, Ashwin Swaminathan, C. J. Taylor, Stefano Soatto

48 · 2024-06 · 原表 #18 图像幻觉基准与评测

R-Bench：LVLM 关系幻觉的评测与分析

Evaluating and Analyzing Relationship Hallucinations in Large Vision-Language Models

Mingrui Wu, Jiayi Ji, Oucheng Huang et al.
ICML 2024

提出关系幻觉基准 R-Bench，揭示「关系-关系／主体-关系／关系-客体」三类共现是关系幻觉主因，并据此改进指令微调数据。

展开中英文摘要与表格信息

中文摘要（原表译文）

幻觉问题在现有大型视觉语言模型（LVLMs）中是普遍关切。此前的工作主要集中于物体幻觉，而物体幻觉可通过引入物体检测器较容易地缓解。然而这些工作忽视了物体间关系上的幻觉，而关系对视觉理解至关重要。本文提出 R-Bench，一个用于评测视觉关系幻觉的新基准。R-Bench 包含关注关系是否存在的图像级问题，以及评估局部视觉理解的实例级问题。我们识别出导致幻觉的三类关系共现：关系-关系、主体-关系、关系-客体。视觉指令微调数据的长尾分布显著影响 LVLMs 对视觉关系的理解。此外，我们的分析揭示，当前 LVLMs 倾向于忽视视觉内容而过度依赖大型语言模型的常识知识；它们在基于上下文信息推理空间关系方面也表现不佳。

English abstract

The issue of hallucinations is a prevalent concern in existing Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs). Previous efforts have primarily focused on investigating object hallucinations, which can be easily alleviated by introducing object detectors. However, these efforts neglect hallucinations in inter-object relationships, which is essential for visual comprehension. In this work, we introduce R-Bench, a novel benchmark for evaluating Vision Relationship Hallucination. R-Bench features image-level questions that focus on the existence of relationships and instance-level questions that assess local visual comprehension. We identify three types of relationship co-occurrences that lead to hallucinations: relationship-relationship, subject-relationship, and relationship-object. The visual instruction tuning dataset's long-tail distribution significantly impacts LVLMs' understanding of visual relationships. Furthermore, our analysis reveals that current LVLMs tend to disregard visual content and overly rely on the common sense knowledge of Large Language Models. They also struggle with reasoning about spatial relationships based on contextual information.

完整作者
Mingrui Wu, Jiayi Ji, Oucheng Huang, Jiale Li, Yuhang Wu, Xiaoshuai Sun, Rongrong Ji

49 · 2024-07 · 原表 #19 图像幻觉基准与评测

视觉语言模型中的多物体幻觉

Multi-Object Hallucination in Vision-Language Models

Xuweiyi Chen, Ziqiao Ma, Xuejun Zhang et al.
NeurIPS 2024

提出 ROPE 自动化评测协议，系统研究模型同时关注多个物体时的错认（虚构不存在的物体）与分心现象。

展开中英文摘要与表格信息

中文摘要（原表译文）

大型视觉语言模型（LVLMs）常受物体幻觉困扰，即生成给定图像中并不存在的物体。当前物体幻觉基准主要关注单一物体类别的存在性而非单个实例；本工作系统研究了多物体幻觉，考察模型在被要求同时关注多个物体时如何误感知（例如虚构不存在的物体或注意力被分散）。我们提出基于识别的物体探测评测（ROPE），这一自动化评测协议在测试时考虑单张图像内物体类别的分布，并使用视觉指代提示以消除歧义。通过全面的实证研究以及对多物体幻觉潜在成因因素的分析，我们发现：(1) 与关注单个物体相比，LVLMs 在关注多个物体时会出现更多幻觉；(2) 被测物体类别的分布会影响幻觉行为，说明 LVLMs 可能依赖捷径与虚假相关；(3) 幻觉行为受数据特定因素（显著性与频率）以及模型内在行为的影响。我们希望使 LVLMs 能够识别并推理现实视觉场景中常见的多个物体，提供洞见，并量化我们在缓解该问题上的进展。

English abstract

Large vision language models (LVLMs) often suffer from object hallucination, producing objects not present in the given images. While current benchmarks for object hallucination primarily concentrate on the presence of a single object class rather than individual entities, this work systematically investigates multi-object hallucination, examining how models misperceive (e.g., invent nonexistent objects or become distracted) when tasked with focusing on multiple objects simultaneously. We introduce Recognition-based Object Probing Evaluation (ROPE), an automated evaluation protocol that considers the distribution of object classes within a single image during testing and uses visual referring prompts to eliminate ambiguity. With comprehensive empirical studies and analysis of potential factors leading to multi-object hallucination, we found that (1). LVLMs suffer more hallucinations when focusing on multiple objects compared to a single object. (2). The tested object class distribution affects hallucination behaviors, indicating that LVLMs may follow shortcuts and spurious correlations. (3). Hallucinatory behaviors are influenced by data-specific factors, salience and frequency, and model intrinsic behaviors. We hope to enable LVLMs to recognize and reason about multiple objects that often occur in realistic visual scenes, provide insights, and quantify our progress towards mitigating the issues.

完整作者
Xuweiyi Chen, Ziqiao Ma, Xuejun Zhang, Sihan Xu, Shengyi Qian, Jianing Yang, David F. Fouhey, Joyce Chai

50 · 2024-07 · 原表 #20 图像幻觉基准与评测

HaloQuest：面向多模态推理的视觉幻觉数据集

HaloQuest: A Visual Hallucination Dataset for Advancing Multimodal Reasoning

Zhecan Wang, Garrett Bingham, Adams Yu et al.
ECCV 2024

借助合成图像构建 7.7K 规模 VQA 数据，覆盖虚假前提、上下文不足等幻觉形态，兼具评测与训练价值。

展开中英文摘要与表格信息

中文摘要（原表译文）

幻觉一直是大型语言模型的主要问题，在多模态场景下仍是关键挑战——此时视觉语言模型（VLMs）不仅要处理文本输入，还要处理视觉输入。尽管 VLMs 进展迅速，但用于评测与应对多模态幻觉的资源有限，且大多聚焦于评测。本文提出 HaloQuest，一个新的视觉问答数据集，覆盖虚假前提、上下文不足、视觉挑战等多模态幻觉的多个方面。HaloQuest 的一个新颖之处在于除真实图像外还利用合成图像，从而实现数据集的规模化构建。HaloQuest 包含超过 7.7K 个样本，覆盖多种类别，既被设计为对 VLMs 具有挑战性的基准，也被设计为可提升多模态推理的微调数据集。我们的实验表明，当前模型在 HaloQuest 上表现吃力，所有开源 VLM 的准确率均低于 36%；另一方面，在 HaloQuest 上微调可显著降低幻觉率，同时保持标准推理任务上的性能。结果还表明，用生成图像做基准评测与用真实图像高度相关（r=0.97）。最后，我们提出一种新颖的自动评测（Auto-Eval）机制，其与人类评分者高度相关（r=0.99），可用于评测 VLMs。总之，本工作在对 VLMs 幻觉的理解、评测与缓解方面取得了实质性进展，是迈向未来更可靠多模态 AI 系统的重要一步。

English abstract

Hallucination has been a major problem for large language models and remains a critical challenge when it comes to multimodality in which vision-language models (VLMs) have to deal with not just textual but also visual inputs. Despite rapid progress in VLMs, resources for evaluating and addressing multimodal hallucination are limited and mostly focused on evaluation. This work introduces HaloQuest, a novel visual question answering dataset that captures various aspects of multimodal hallucination such as false premises, insufficient contexts, and visual challenges. A novel idea from HaloQuest is to leverage synthetic images, apart from real ones, to enable dataset creation at scale. With over 7.7K examples spanning across a wide variety of categories, HaloQuest was designed to be both a challenging benchmark for VLMs and a fine-tuning dataset for advancing multimodal reasoning. Our experiments reveal that current models struggle with HaloQuest, with all open-source VLMs achieving below 36% accuracy. On the other hand, fine-tuning on HaloQuest significantly reduces hallucination rates while preserving performance on standard reasoning tasks. Our results discover that benchmarking with generated images is highly correlated (r=0.97) with real images. Last but not least, we propose a novel Auto-Eval mechanism that is highly correlated with human raters (r=0.99) for evaluating VLMs. In sum, this work makes concrete strides towards understanding, evaluating, and mitigating hallucination in VLMs, serving as an important step towards more reliable multimodal AI systems in the future.

完整作者
Zhecan Wang, Garrett Bingham, Adams Yu, Quoc Le, Thang Luong, Golnaz Ghiasi

51 · 2024-09 · 原表 #21 图像幻觉基准与评测

ODE：多模态大模型幻觉的开放集动态评测

ODE: Open-Set Evaluation of Hallucinations in Multimodal Large Language Models

Yahan Tu, Rui Hu, Jitao Sang
arXiv 预印本（元数据未标注录用）

用图结构表示物体概念、属性及其分布关联，动态生成新样本以规避数据污染，评测存在级与属性级幻觉。

展开中英文摘要与表格信息

中文摘要（原表译文）

幻觉是多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）面临的持续挑战。然而，现有的幻觉评测基准通常是静态的，可能忽视数据污染的潜在风险。为解决此问题，我们提出 ODE，一种开放集、动态的评测协议，用于在存在性层面与属性层面评测 MLLMs 的物体幻觉。ODE 采用基于图的结构来表示现实世界的物体概念、其属性以及它们之间的分布关联。该结构便于依据多样的分布准则抽取概念组合，生成多样化样本以进行结构化查询，从而在生成式任务与判别式任务中评测幻觉。通过生成新样本、动态的概念组合以及多样的分布频次，ODE 降低了数据污染风险并拓宽了评测范围。该协议适用于通用场景与特定场景，包括数据受限的场景。实验结果表明了该协议的有效性，并揭示 MLLMs 在由 ODE 生成的样本上表现出更高的幻觉率，说明存在潜在的数据污染。此外，这些生成的样本有助于分析幻觉模式并对模型进行微调，为缓解 MLLMs 幻觉提供了有效途径。

English abstract

Hallucination poses a persistent challenge for multimodal large language models (MLLMs). However, existing benchmarks for evaluating hallucinations are generally static, which may overlook the potential risk of data contamination. To address this issue, we propose ODE, an open-set, dynamic protocol designed to evaluate object hallucinations in MLLMs at both the existence and attribute levels. ODE employs a graph-based structure to represent real-world object concepts, their attributes, and the distributional associations between them. This structure facilitates the extraction of concept combinations based on diverse distributional criteria, generating varied samples for structured queries that evaluate hallucinations in both generative and discriminative tasks. Through the generation of new samples, dynamic concept combinations, and varied distribution frequencies, ODE mitigates the risk of data contamination and broadens the scope of evaluation. This protocol is applicable to both general and specialized scenarios, including those with limited data. Experimental results demonstrate the effectiveness of our protocol, revealing that MLLMs exhibit higher hallucination rates when evaluated with ODE-generated samples, which indicates potential data contamination. Furthermore, these generated samples aid in analyzing hallucination patterns and fine-tuning models, offering an effective approach to mitigating hallucinations in MLLMs.

完整作者
Yahan Tu, Rui Hu, Jitao Sang

52 · 2024-10 · 原表 #22 图像幻觉基准与评测

统一三元组级幻觉评测框架（Tri-HE）

Unified Triplet-Level Hallucination Evaluation for Large Vision-Language Models

Junjie Wu, Tsz Ting Chung, Kai Chen et al.
TMLR (Transactions on Machine Learning Research)

以（物体,关系,物体）三元组统一度量物体幻觉与关系幻觉，可泛化到多种视觉语言任务。

展开中英文摘要与表格信息

中文摘要（原表译文）

尽管在视觉语言推理上表现出色，大型视觉语言模型（LVLMs）仍可能生成给定图像中并不存在的幻觉内容。多数现有 LVLM 幻觉基准局限于评测与物体相关的幻觉。然而，两个物体之间关系上的潜在幻觉，即关系幻觉，仍缺乏研究。为弥补这一空白，我们设计了一个统一框架，以同时度量 LVLMs 的物体幻觉与关系幻觉。该框架的核心思想是通过从 LVLMs 回答中抽取的（物体, 关系, 物体）三元组来评测幻觉，因而易于泛化到不同的视觉语言任务。基于该框架，我们进一步提出 Tri-HE，一个新的三元组级幻觉评测基准，可同时研究物体幻觉与关系幻觉。通过在 Tri-HE 上的全面评测，我们观察到在现有 LVLMs 中关系幻觉问题甚至比物体幻觉更严重，凸显出一个此前被忽视、关乎 LVLMs 可靠性的问题。此外，基于我们的发现，我们设计了一种简单且免训练的方法，可有效缓解 LVLMs 的幻觉。数据集与复现代码公开于 https://github.com/wujunjie1998/Tri-HE 。

English abstract

Despite the outstanding performance in vision-language reasoning, Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs) might generate hallucinated contents that do not exist in the given image. Most existing LVLM hallucination benchmarks are constrained to evaluate the object-related hallucinations. However, the potential hallucination on the relations between two objects, i.e., relation hallucination, still lacks investigation. To remedy that, we design a unified framework to measure the object and relation hallucination in LVLMs simultaneously. The core idea of our framework is to evaluate hallucinations via (object, relation, object) triplets extracted from LVLMs' responses, making it easily generalizable to different vision-language tasks. Based on our framework, we further introduce Tri-HE, a novel Triplet-level Hallucination Evaluation benchmark which can be used to study both object and relation hallucination at the same time. With comprehensive evaluations on Tri-HE, we observe that the relation hallucination issue is even more serious than object hallucination among existing LVLMs, highlighting a previously neglected problem towards reliable LVLMs. Moreover, based on our findings, we design a simple training-free approach that effectively mitigates hallucinations for LVLMs. Our dataset and code for the reproduction of our experiments are available publicly at https://github.com/wujunjie1998/Tri-HE.

完整作者
Junjie Wu, Tsz Ting Chung, Kai Chen, Dit-Yan Yeung

53 · 2024-06 · 原表 #23 视频幻觉基准与评测

申博清单 #10 · 视频侧评测基准

VideoHallucer：视频语言模型内在与外在幻觉评测

VideoHallucer: Evaluating Intrinsic and Extrinsic Hallucinations in Large Video-Language Models

Yuxuan Wang, Yueqian Wang, Dongyan Zhao et al.
arXiv 预印本（元数据未标注录用）

首个视频幻觉综合基准，用对抗式二元 VideoQA 把幻觉分为内在（物体-关系／时序／语义细节）与外在（事实类）两类。

展开中英文摘要与表格信息

中文摘要（原表译文）

多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）的近期进展已将其能力扩展到视频理解。然而这些模型常受"幻觉"困扰，即生成偏离实际视频内容的无关或无意义内容。本工作提出 VideoHallucer，首个用于检测大型视频语言模型（LVLMs）幻觉的综合基准。VideoHallucer 将幻觉分为内在与外在两大类，并进一步细分子类以便详细分析，包括物体-关系、时序、语义细节、外在事实性与外在非事实性幻觉。我们采用对抗式二元 VideoQA 方法进行全面评测，策略性地构造基础问题与幻觉问题的配对。通过在 VideoHallucer 上评测 11 个 LVLMs，我们揭示：i) 当前大多数模型在幻觉方面存在显著问题；ii) 扩大数据集与参数量虽提升了模型检测基本视觉线索与反事实的能力，但对检测外在事实性幻觉帮助有限；iii) 现有模型更擅长检测事实而非识别幻觉。作为副产品，这些分析进一步指导了我们 self-PEP 框架的设计，在所有模型架构上平均提升 5.38% 的抗幻觉能力。

English abstract

Recent advancements in Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have extended their capabilities to video understanding. Yet, these models are often plagued by "hallucinations", where irrelevant or nonsensical content is generated, deviating from the actual video context. This work introduces VideoHallucer, the first comprehensive benchmark for hallucination detection in large video-language models (LVLMs). VideoHallucer categorizes hallucinations into two main types: intrinsic and extrinsic, offering further subcategories for detailed analysis, including object-relation, temporal, semantic detail, extrinsic factual, and extrinsic non-factual hallucinations. We adopt an adversarial binary VideoQA method for comprehensive evaluation, where pairs of basic and hallucinated questions are crafted strategically. By evaluating eleven LVLMs on VideoHallucer, we reveal that i) the majority of current models exhibit significant issues with hallucinations; ii) while scaling datasets and parameters improves models' ability to detect basic visual cues and counterfactuals, it provides limited benefit for detecting extrinsic factual hallucinations; iii) existing models are more adept at detecting facts than identifying hallucinations. As a byproduct, these analyses further instruct the development of our self-PEP framework, achieving an average of 5.38% improvement in hallucination resistance across all model architectures.

完整作者
Yuxuan Wang, Yueqian Wang, Dongyan Zhao, Cihang Xie, Zilong Zheng

54 · 2024-09 · 原表 #24 视频幻觉基准与评测

EventHallusion：诊断视频 LLM 的事件幻觉

EventHallusion: Diagnosing Event Hallucinations in Video LLMs

Jiacheng Zhang, Yang Jiao, Shaoxiang Chen et al.
arXiv 预印本（元数据未标注录用）

从幻觉归因角度评测视频模型对「事件」这一核心对象的幻觉，并同步提出简洁有效的时序对比解码（TCD）。

展开中英文摘要与表格信息

中文摘要（原表译文）

近来多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）在视频理解领域取得显著进展。尽管展现出出色的内容推理与指令遵循能力，这些视频大模型的幻觉问题相比图像领域的对应研究仍探索不足。为缩小这一差距，我们提出 EventHallusion，一个聚焦评估 VideoLLMs 对"事件"（视频分析的关键所在）之幻觉的新基准。从幻觉归因的视角，EventHallusion 旨在评估 VideoLLM 对语言先验与视觉-语言偏差的易感性。另一方面，我们还提出一种简单而有效的方法——时序对比解码（TCD），以应对 VideoLLMs 的幻觉问题。TCD 在解码阶段通过将原始视频与一个时序线索被打乱的修改版本进行比较，纠正模型对自身先验的偏向。通过在 EventHallusion 上对 8 个开源与 2 个闭源 VideoLLMs 进行全面评测，我们观察到开源模型受幻觉问题影响显著，而闭源模型表现明显更好。进一步为开源 VideoLLMs 配备 TCD 后，在 EventHallusion 的多数指标上取得了明显的性能提升。代码与基准数据见 https://github.com/Stevetich/EventHallusion 。

English abstract

Recently, Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have made significant progress in the video comprehension field. Despite remarkable content reasoning and instruction following capabilities they demonstrated, the hallucination problem of these VideoLLMs is less explored compared with its counterpart in the image domain. To mitigate this gap, we propose EventHallusion, a novel benchmark that focuses on assessing the VideoLLMs' hallucination toward event, the crux of video analysis. From a hallucination attribution perspective, our EventHallusion benchmark is curated to assess a VideoLLM's susceptibility toward language priors and vision-language biases. On the other hand, we also propose a simple yet effective method, called Temporal Contrastive Decoding (TCD), to tackle the hallucination problems of VideoLLMs. The proposed TCD method rectifies the model's bias toward its priors during the decoding stage by comparing the original video with a modified version, in which temporal cues are disrupted. Through comprehensive evaluation of eight open-source and two closed-source VideoLLMs on the proposed EventHallusion benchmark, we observe that the open-source models suffer significantly from hallucination problems, whereas the closed-source ones perform markedly better. By further equipping open-source VideoLLMs with the proposed TCD approach, evident performance improvements are achieved across most metrics in the EventHallusion benchmark. Our codes and benchmark data are available at https://github.com/Stevetich/EventHallusion.

完整作者
Jiacheng Zhang, Yang Jiao, Shaoxiang Chen, Na Zhao, Zhiyu Tan, Hao Li, Xingjun Ma, Jingjing Chen

55 · 2024-11 · 原表 #25 视频幻觉基准与评测

申博清单 #12 · 视频侧评测基准

VidHal：视觉大模型的时序幻觉基准

VidHal: Benchmarking Temporal Hallucinations in Vision LLMs

Wey Yeh Choong, Yangyang Guo, Mohan Kankanhalli
TMLR (Transactions on Machine Learning Research)

针对视频时序层面构建带幻觉等级描述的视频基准，突破以往只做图像幻觉评测且难以捕捉细粒度错误的局限。

展开中英文摘要与表格信息

中文摘要（原表译文）

视觉大语言模型（VLLMs）被普遍认为易产生幻觉。针对该问题的现有研究主要局限于图像输入，对基于视频的幻觉探索有限。此外，当前的评测方法无法捕捉生成回答中的细微错误，而视频丰富的时空动态往往加剧了这些错误。为此，我们提出 VidHal，一个专为评测 VLLMs 视频幻觉而设计的基准。VidHal 通过在广泛常见的时序层面上引导生成视频实例来构建。该基准的一个显著特点是精心构造了代表每段视频不同程度幻觉的描述文本。为实现细粒度评测，我们提出一种新的描述排序任务，要求 VLLMs 按幻觉程度对描述排序。我们在 VidHal 上开展了大量实验，全面评测了众多模型。结果揭示了现有 VLLMs 在幻觉生成方面的显著局限。我们希望该基准能推动两方面研究：1) 对 VLLM 能力的整体理解，尤其是幻觉方面；2) 广泛开发更先进的 VLLMs 以缓解该问题。

English abstract

Vision Large Language Models (VLLMs) are widely acknowledged to be prone to hallucinations. Existing research addressing this problem has primarily been confined to image inputs, with limited exploration of video-based hallucinations. Furthermore, current evaluation methods fail to capture nuanced errors in generated responses, which are often exacerbated by the rich spatiotemporal dynamics of videos. To address this, we introduce VidHal, a benchmark specially designed to evaluate video-based hallucinations in VLLMs. VidHal is constructed by bootstrapping video instances across a wide range of common temporal aspects. A defining feature of our benchmark lies in the careful creation of captions which represent varying levels of hallucination associated with each video. To enable fine-grained evaluation, we propose a novel caption ordering task requiring VLLMs to rank captions by hallucinatory extent. We conduct extensive experiments on VidHal and comprehensively evaluate a broad selection of models. Our results uncover significant limitations in existing VLLMs regarding hallucination generation. Through our benchmark, we aim to inspire further research on 1) holistic understanding of VLLM capabilities, particularly regarding hallucination, and 2) extensive development of advanced VLLMs to alleviate this problem.

完整作者
Wey Yeh Choong, Yangyang Guo, Mohan Kankanhalli

56 · 2024-12 · 原表 #26 视频幻觉基准与评测

申博清单 #11 · 视频侧评测基准

VidHalluc：视频理解中的时序幻觉评测

VidHalluc: Evaluating Temporal Hallucinations in Multimodal Large Language Models for Video Understanding

Chaoyu Li, Eun Woo Im, Pooyan Fazli
CVPR 2025

用 5002 个易混淆配对视频评测动作、时序顺序、场景转换三类幻觉，发现主流 MLLM 在视频理解上普遍脆弱。

展开中英文摘要与表格信息

中文摘要（原表译文）

多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）近期在视频理解上取得显著进展，在内容推理与指令遵循任务上表现出色。然而，模型生成不准确或误导性内容的幻觉问题在视频领域仍研究不足。基于"MLLM 视觉编码器常常无法区分视觉不同但语义相似的视频对"这一观察，我们提出 VidHalluc——目前最大的、用于考察 MLLMs 视频理解幻觉的基准。它由 5002 段视频构成，以配对方式突出易于发生幻觉的情形。VidHalluc 从三个关键维度评估幻觉：(1) 动作，(2) 时序顺序，(3) 场景转换。全面测试表明，多数 MLLMs 在这些维度上都易受幻觉影响。此外，我们提出 DINO-HEAL，一种免训练方法，通过在推理时引入 DINOv2 的空间显著性对视觉特征重新加权来减少幻觉。结果显示 DINO-HEAL 在 VidHalluc 上稳定提升，在所有任务上平均提升 3.02%。VidHalluc 基准与 DINO-HEAL 代码见 https://people-robots.github.io/vidhalluc 。

English abstract

Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have recently shown significant advancements in video understanding, excelling in content reasoning and instruction-following tasks. However, hallucination, where models generate inaccurate or misleading content, remains underexplored in the video domain. Building on the observation that MLLM visual encoders often fail to distinguish visually different yet semantically similar video pairs, we introduce VidHalluc, the largest benchmark designed to examine hallucinations in MLLMs for video understanding. It consists of 5,002 videos, paired to highlight cases prone to hallucinations. VidHalluc assesses hallucinations across three critical dimensions: (1) action, (2) temporal sequence, and (3) scene transition. Comprehensive testing shows that most MLLMs are vulnerable to hallucinations across these dimensions. Furthermore, we propose DINO-HEAL, a training-free method that reduces hallucinations by incorporating spatial saliency from DINOv2 to reweight visual features during inference. Our results show that DINO-HEAL consistently improves performance on VidHalluc, achieving an average improvement of 3.02% in mitigating hallucinations across all tasks. Both the VidHalluc benchmark and DINO-HEAL code are available at https://people-robots.github.io/vidhalluc.

完整作者
Chaoyu Li, Eun Woo Im, Pooyan Fazli

57 · 2025-03 · 原表 #27 视频幻觉基准与评测

视频理解中的大模型幻觉：基准、分析与缓解（HAVEN）

Exploring Hallucination of Large Multimodal Models in Video Understanding: Benchmark, Analysis and Mitigation

Hongcheng Gao, Jiashu Qu, Jingyi Tang et al.
arXiv 预印本（元数据未标注录用）

构建 6K 问题的 HAVEN 基准（幻觉成因×幻觉方面×题型），量化视频时长、模型规模等 7 类影响因素并给出缓解方案。

展开中英文摘要与表格信息

中文摘要（原表译文）

大型多模态模型（LMMs）的幻觉——给出看似正确实则错误的回答——限制了其可靠性与适用性。本文旨在研究 LMMs 在视频模态下的幻觉问题；相较图像与文本等静态模态，视频是动态且更具挑战的。基于此动机，我们首先提出一个综合基准 HAVEN，用于评测 LMMs 在视频理解任务中的幻觉。它建立在三个维度（幻觉成因、幻觉方面、问题形式）之上，共含 6K 个问题。随后，我们通过在 HAVEN 上对 16 个 LMMs 的实验，定量研究了 7 个对幻觉有影响的因素，例如视频时长、模型规模与模型推理。此外，受 OpenAI o1 等近期思考型模型的启发，我们提出一个视频思考模型，通过监督推理微调（SRFT）与直接偏好优化（TDPO）来缓解 LMMs 的幻觉——其中 SRFT 增强推理能力，TDPO 减少思考过程中的幻觉。大量实验与分析证明了其有效性：在幻觉评测上准确率较基线提升 7.65%，偏差分数降低 4.5%。代码与数据公开于 https://github.com/Hongcheng-Gao/HAVEN 。

English abstract

The hallucination of large multimodal models (LMMs), providing responses that appear correct but are actually incorrect, limits their reliability and applicability. This paper aims to study the hallucination problem of LMMs in video modality, which is dynamic and more challenging compared to static modalities like images and text. From this motivation, we first present a comprehensive benchmark termed HAVEN for evaluating hallucinations of LMMs in video understanding tasks. It is built upon three dimensions, i.e., hallucination causes, hallucination aspects, and question formats, resulting in 6K questions. Then, we quantitatively study 7 influential factors on hallucinations, e.g., duration time of videos, model sizes, and model reasoning, via experiments of 16 LMMs on the presented benchmark. In addition, inspired by recent thinking models like OpenAI o1, we propose a video-thinking model to mitigate the hallucinations of LMMs via supervised reasoning fine-tuning (SRFT) and direct preference optimization (TDPO)-- where SRFT enhances reasoning capabilities while TDPO reduces hallucinations in the thinking process. Extensive experiments and analyses demonstrate the effectiveness. Remarkably, it improves the baseline by 7.65% in accuracy on hallucination evaluation and reduces the bias score by 4.5%. The code and data are public at https://github.com/Hongcheng-Gao/HAVEN.

完整作者
Hongcheng Gao, Jiashu Qu, Jingyi Tang, Baolong Bi, Yue Liu, Hongyu Chen, Li Liang, Li Su, Qingming Huang

58 · 2025-06 · 原表 #28 视频幻觉基准与评测

ARGUS：视频 LLM 的幻觉与遗漏评测

ARGUS: Hallucination and Omission Evaluation in Video-LLMs

Ruchit Rawal, Reza Shirkavand, Heng Huang et al.
arXiv 预印本（元数据未标注录用）

用自由形式视频描述任务同时度量幻觉率与关键细节遗漏率，弥补选择题式评测严重低估视频幻觉的问题。

展开中英文摘要与表格信息

中文摘要（原表译文）

视频大语言模型迄今尚未被广泛部署，很大程度上源于其幻觉倾向。典型的 Video-LLM 基准仅依赖选择题。遗憾的是，VideoLLMs 在视频描述这类自由形式文本生成任务上的幻觉要比在选择题验证任务上严重得多。为弥补这一缺陷，我们提出 ARGUS，一个衡量自由形式视频描述性能的 VideoLLM 基准。通过将 VideoLLM 输出与人类真值描述进行比较，ARGUS 量化两项指标：第一，度量以关于视频内容或时序关系的错误陈述形式出现的幻觉率；第二，度量模型遗漏重要描述细节的比率。这两项指标共同构成对视频描述性能的完整视角。

English abstract

Video large language models have not yet been widely deployed, largely due to their tendency to hallucinate. Typical benchmarks for Video-LLMs rely simply on multiple-choice questions. Unfortunately, VideoLLMs hallucinate far more aggressively on freeform text generation tasks like video captioning than they do on multiple choice verification tasks. To address this weakness, we propose ARGUS, a VideoLLM benchmark that measures freeform video captioning performance. By comparing VideoLLM outputs to human ground truth captions, ARGUS quantifies dual metrics. First, we measure the rate of hallucinations in the form of incorrect statements about video content or temporal relationships. Second, we measure the rate at which the model omits important descriptive details. Together, these dual metrics form a comprehensive view of video captioning performance.

完整作者
Ruchit Rawal, Reza Shirkavand, Heng Huang, Gowthami Somepalli, Tom Goldstein

59 · 2025-08 · 原表 #29 视频幻觉基准与评测

EgoIllusion：第一视角视频理解幻觉基准

EGOILLUSION: Benchmarking Hallucinations in Egocentric Video Understanding

Ashish Seth, Utkarsh Tyagi, Ramaneswaran Selvakumar et al.
arXiv 预印本（元数据未标注录用）

首个针对第一视角视频的幻觉基准（1400 视频／8000 问），GPT-4o、Gemini 等强模型准确率仅约 59%。

展开中英文摘要与表格信息

中文摘要（原表译文）

多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）在复杂多模态任务上表现出色。尽管 MLLMs 在第三人称与第一视角视频的视觉感知和推理方面表现优异，它们仍易产生幻觉，给出连贯但不准确的回答。我们提出 EgoIllusion，首个评测第一视角视频中 MLLM 幻觉的基准。EgoIllusion 包含 1400 段视频与 8000 个人工标注的开放式与封闭式问题，旨在触发第一视角视频中视觉与听觉线索上的幻觉。对十个 MLLMs 的评测揭示了重大挑战，包括 GPT-4o 与 Gemini 等强模型也仅取得 59% 的准确率。EgoIllusion 为开发稳健的基准以评测 MLLMs 的有效性奠定基础，并推动研发幻觉率更低的更好的第一视角 MLLMs。我们的基准将开源以支持可复现性。

English abstract

Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have demonstrated remarkable performance in complex multimodal tasks. While MLLMs excel at visual perception and reasoning in third-person and egocentric videos, they are prone to hallucinations, generating coherent yet inaccurate responses. We present EgoIllusion, a first benchmark to evaluate MLLM hallucinations in egocentric videos. EgoIllusion comprises 1,400 videos paired with 8,000 human-annotated open and closed-ended questions designed to trigger hallucinations in both visual and auditory cues in egocentric videos. Evaluations across ten MLLMs reveal significant challenges, including powerful models like GPT-4o and Gemini, achieving only 59% accuracy. EgoIllusion lays the foundation in developing robust benchmarks to evaluate the effectiveness of MLLMs and spurs the development of better egocentric MLLMs with reduced hallucination rates. Our benchmark will be open-sourced for reproducibility.

完整作者
Ashish Seth, Utkarsh Tyagi, Ramaneswaran Selvakumar, Nishit Anand, Sonal Kumar, Sreyan Ghosh, Ramani Duraiswami, Chirag Agarwal, Dinesh Manocha

60 · 2025-08 · 原表 #30 视频幻觉基准与评测

ELV-Halluc：长视频理解中的语义聚合幻觉基准

ELV-Halluc: Benchmarking Semantic Aggregation Hallucinations in Long Video Understanding

Hao Lu, Jiahao Wang, Yaolun Zhang et al.
arXiv 预印本（元数据未标注录用）

提出「语义聚合幻觉」——帧级语义正确但聚合到事件级出错，并构建长视频基准专门评测此类幻觉。

展开中英文摘要与表格信息

中文摘要（原表译文）

视频多模态大语言模型（Video-MLLMs）在视频理解上取得了显著进展。然而它们仍易产生与视频输入不一致或无关的幻觉内容。此前的视频幻觉基准主要关注短视频，并将幻觉归因于强语言先验、帧缺失或视觉编码器引入的视觉-语言偏差等因素。这些原因确实解释了短视频中的大多数幻觉，但仍过度简化了幻觉的成因。有时模型输出了错误结果，但其帧级语义却是正确的。我们把这类幻觉称为语义聚合幻觉（SAH），它出现在将帧级语义聚合为事件级语义组的过程中。鉴于长视频中因跨多个事件的语义复杂度上升而使 SAH 尤为关键，有必要将这类幻觉的成因分离出来并深入研究。为解决上述问题，我们提出 ELV-Halluc，首个专门面向长视频幻觉的基准，可对 SAH 进行系统研究。我们的实验确认了 SAH 的存在，并表明它随语义复杂度上升而增加。此外我们发现，模型在语义快速变化时更易出现 SAH。我们还讨论了缓解 SAH 的可能途径，证明位置编码策略有助于缓解 SAH，并进一步采用 DPO 策略增强模型区分事件内与跨事件语义的能力。为此我们构建了 8K 对抗数据对，在 ELV-Halluc 与 Video-MME 上均取得提升，其中 SAH 比率大幅降低 27.7%。

English abstract

Video multimodal large language models (Video-MLLMs) have achieved remarkable progress in video understanding. However, they remain vulnerable to hallucination-producing content inconsistent with or unrelated to video inputs. Previous video hallucination benchmarks primarily focus on short-videos. They attribute hallucinations to factors such as strong language priors, missing frames, or vision-language biases introduced by the visual encoder. While these causes indeed account for most hallucinations in short videos, they still oversimplify the cause of hallucinations. Sometimes, models generate incorrect outputs but with correct frame-level semantics. We refer to this type of hallucination as Semantic Aggregation Hallucination (SAH), which arises during the process of aggregating frame-level semantics into event-level semantic groups. Given that SAH becomes particularly critical in long videos due to increased semantic complexity across multiple events, it is essential to separate and thoroughly investigate the causes of this type of hallucination. To address the above issues, we introduce ELV-Halluc, the first benchmark dedicated to long-video hallucination, enabling a systematic investigation of SAH. Our experiments confirm the existence of SAH and show that it increases with semantic complexity. Additionally, we find that models are more prone to SAH on rapidly changing semantics. Moreover, we discuss potential approaches to mitigate SAH. We demonstrate that positional encoding strategy contributes to alleviating SAH, and further adopt DPO strategy to enhance the model's ability to distinguish semantics within and across events. To support this, we curate a dataset of 8K adversarial data pairs and achieve improvements on both ELV-Halluc and Video-MME, including a substantial 27.7% reduction in SAH ratio.

完整作者
Hao Lu, Jiahao Wang, Yaolun Zhang, Ruohui Wang, Xuanyu Zheng, Yepeng Tang, Dahua Lin, Lewei Lu

61 · 2025-09 · 原表 #31 视频幻觉基准与评测

申博清单 #15 · 视频侧评测基准

MESH：像人一样理解视频——大视频模型幻觉度量

MESH -- Understanding Videos Like Human: Measuring Hallucinations in Large Video Models

Garry Yang, Zizhe Chen, Man Hon Wong et al.
arXiv 预印本（元数据未标注录用）

按人类感知过程自底向上（基础物体→由粗到细主体特征→主体-动作对）设计目标/陷阱成对实例来评测视频幻觉。

展开中英文摘要与表格信息

中文摘要（原表译文）

大型视频模型（LVMs）在大型语言模型（LLMs）与视觉模块的语义能力之上，通过整合时序信息来更好地理解动态视频内容。尽管取得进展，LVMs 仍易产生幻觉，输出不准确或无关的描述。当前的视频幻觉基准过于依赖对视频内容的人工分类，忽视了人类自然解读视频时所依赖的感知过程。我们提出 MESH，一个用于系统评测 LVMs 幻觉的基准。MESH 采用包含二选一与多选形式的问答框架，并纳入目标实例与陷阱实例。它遵循自底向上的思路，评测基础物体、由粗到细的主体特征以及主体-动作对，与人类的视频理解过程相契合。我们证明 MESH 为识别视频中的幻觉提供了有效而全面的方法。评测显示，LVMs 虽擅长识别基础物体与特征，但在处理细节或对齐长视频中涉及多主体的多个动作时，其幻觉易感性会明显上升。

English abstract

Large Video Models (LVMs) build on the semantic capabilities of Large Language Models (LLMs) and vision modules by integrating temporal information to better understand dynamic video content. Despite their progress, LVMs are prone to hallucinations-producing inaccurate or irrelevant descriptions. Current benchmarks for video hallucination depend heavily on manual categorization of video content, neglecting the perception-based processes through which humans naturally interpret videos. We introduce MESH, a benchmark designed to evaluate hallucinations in LVMs systematically. MESH uses a Question-Answering framework with binary and multi-choice formats incorporating target and trap instances. It follows a bottom-up approach, evaluating basic objects, coarse-to-fine subject features, and subject-action pairs, aligning with human video understanding. We demonstrate that MESH offers an effective and comprehensive approach for identifying hallucinations in videos. Our evaluations show that while LVMs excel at recognizing basic objects and features, their susceptibility to hallucinations increases markedly when handling fine details or aligning multiple actions involving various subjects in longer videos.

完整作者
Garry Yang, Zizhe Chen, Man Hon Wong, Haoyu Lei, Yongqiang Chen, Zhenguo Li, Kaiwen Zhou, James Cheng

62 · 2026-01 · 原表 #32 视频幻觉基准与评测

VERHallu：视频 LLM 事件关系幻觉的评测与缓解

VERHallu: Evaluating and Mitigating Event Relation Hallucination in Video Large Language Models

Zefan Zhang, Kehua Zhu, Shijie Jiang et al.
arXiv 预印本（元数据未标注录用）

聚焦事件之间的因果、时序、子事件关系幻觉，构建含反事实问答的基准覆盖三类任务，并提出缓解方法。

展开中英文摘要与表格信息

中文摘要（原表译文）

视频大语言模型（VideoLLMs）表现出多种类型的幻觉。现有研究主要关注涉及视频中事件、物体与场景是否存在的幻觉，而在很大程度上忽视了事件关系幻觉。本文提出一个新的基准 VERHallu，用于评测视频事件关系幻觉。该基准聚焦事件之间的因果、时序与子事件关系，涵盖关系分类、问答与反事实问答三类任务，以全面评测事件关系幻觉。此外，它包含偏离典型预训练分布的反直觉视频场景，每个样本都配有覆盖视觉-语言偏差与纯语言偏差的人工标注候选答案。我们的分析表明，当前最先进的 VideoLLMs 在密集事件关系推理上表现不佳，由于对帧级线索利用不足而常常依赖先验知识。尽管这些模型对关键事件表现出较强的接地能力，但往往忽视周围的子事件，导致对事件关系的理解不完整、不准确。为此我们提出关键帧传播（KFP）策略，在中间层重新分配帧级注意力以增强多事件理解。实验表明，它能在不影响推理速度的前提下有效缓解事件关系幻觉。

English abstract

Video Large Language Models (VideoLLMs) exhibit various types of hallucinations. Existing research has primarily focused on hallucinations involving the presence of events, objects, and scenes in videos, while largely neglecting event relation hallucination. In this paper, we introduce a novel benchmark for evaluating the Video Event Relation Hallucination, named VERHallu. This benchmark focuses on causal, temporal, and subevent relations between events, encompassing three types of tasks: relation classification, question answering, and counterfactual question answering, for a comprehensive evaluation of event relation hallucination. Additionally, it features counterintuitive video scenarios that deviate from typical pretraining distributions, with each sample accompanied by human-annotated candidates covering both vision-language and pure language biases. Our analysis reveals that current state-of-the-art VideoLLMs struggle with dense-event relation reasoning, often relying on prior knowledge due to insufficient use of frame-level cues. Although these models demonstrate strong grounding capabilities for key events, they often overlook the surrounding subevents, leading to an incomplete and inaccurate understanding of event relations. To tackle this, we propose a Key-Frame Propagating (KFP) strategy, which reallocates frame-level attention within intermediate layers to enhance multi-event understanding. Experiments show it effectively mitigates the event relation hallucination without affecting inference speed.

完整作者
Zefan Zhang, Kehua Zhu, Shijie Jiang, Hongyuan Lu, Shengkai Sun, Tian Bai

63 · 2026-07 · 原表 #33 视频幻觉基准与评测

MoHallBench：视频 LLM 动作幻觉基准

MoHallBench: A Benchmark for Motion Hallucination in Video Large Language Models

Sihan Chen, Jiale Li, Jianghang Lin et al.
arXiv 预印本（元数据未标注录用）

针对「凭空推断视频中未发生的人体动作」这一视频特有幻觉，构建 11306 视频／40493 问答的大规模诊断基准。

展开中英文摘要与表格信息

中文摘要（原表译文）

视频大语言模型（VideoLLMs）在视频理解上取得显著进展，但仍受与视觉证据不符的幻觉困扰。现有基准主要关注物体幻觉或粗粒度动作感知，而一个关键的视频特有问题仍未得到充分探索：动作幻觉，即模型推断出视频中并未出现的人体动作。我们提出 MoHallBench，一个用于诊断 VideoLLMs 动作幻觉的基准。MoHallBench 系统评测三类主要幻觉来源：共现先验、顺序推断与相似混淆。它包含 11306 个视频片段与 40493 个问答对，覆盖二选一、多选与生成式设置。我们进一步引入带偏见感知指标的双向提问协议，以减少二元评测中的肯定倾向。对十个近期开源 VideoLLMs 的实验揭示，动作识别与抗幻觉能力之间存在明显的解耦：在正向动作识别上表现良好的模型，往往在对抗性负样本上失败。在所有设置中，顺序推断幻觉最为严重，表明当前模型倾向于从局部动作线索过度推断预期结果。分析进一步确认，更强的先验与更细粒度的相似性会显著放大幻觉。我们希望 MoHallBench 能促进未来对 VideoLLMs 动作幻觉的评测与缓解。

English abstract

Video Large Language Models (VideoLLMs) have shown strong progress in video understanding, yet they still suffer from hallucinations that are inconsistent with visual evidence. Existing benchmarks mainly focus on object hallucination or coarse action perception, leaving a key video-specific problem underexplored: motion hallucination, in which models infer human motions that are absent from the video. We present MoHallBench, a benchmark for diagnosing motion hallucination in VideoLLMs. MoHallBench systematically evaluates three major sources of hallucination: co-occurrence priors, sequential inference, and similarity confusion. It contains 11,306 video clips and 40,493 question-answer pairs, covering binary-choice, multiple-choice, and generative settings. We further introduce a bi-directional questioning protocol with bias-aware metrics to reduce affirmation bias in binary evaluation. Experiments on ten recent open-source VideoLLMs reveal a clear decoupling between action recognition and hallucination resistance, as models that perform well on positive action recognition often fail on adversarial negatives. Among all settings, sequential inference hallucination is the most severe, showing that current models tend to over-infer expected outcomes from partial motion cues. Our analyses further confirm that stronger priors and finer-grained similarity substantially amplify hallucination. We hope MoHallBench can facilitate future evaluation and mitigation of motion hallucination in VideoLLMs.

完整作者
Sihan Chen, Jiale Li, Jianghang Lin, Mengyuan Liu

64 · 2023-11 · 原表 #34 偏好对齐与训练缓解

HalluciDoctor：消除视觉指令数据中的幻觉毒性

HalluciDoctor: Mitigating Hallucinatory Toxicity in Visual Instruction Data

Qifan Yu, Juncheng Li, Longhui Wei et al.
CVPR 2024

用交叉检验范式检测并剔除机器生成视觉指令数据中的物体、关系、属性幻觉，从根本上减少幻觉传播。

展开中英文摘要与表格信息

中文摘要（原表译文）

在机器生成的指令遵循数据上微调的多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）在各类多模态理解与生成任务上表现优异。然而机器生成数据中固有的幻觉——它可能导致 MLLMs 产生幻觉输出——仍研究不足。本工作旨在研究各类幻觉（即物体、关系、属性幻觉），并缓解大规模机器生成视觉指令数据集中的这些幻觉毒性。借鉴人类识别事实性错误的能力，我们提出一个基于交叉检验范式的新颖幻觉检测与消除框架 HalluciDoctor。我们用该框架自动识别并消除训练数据中的幻觉。有趣的是，HalluciDoctor 还表明，由长尾物体共现产生的虚假相关会促成幻觉。据此我们执行反事实视觉指令扩展以平衡数据分布，从而增强 MLLMs 的抗幻觉能力。在幻觉评测基准上的全面实验表明，我们的方法成功相对缓解了 44.6% 的幻觉，并相比 LLaVA 保持有竞争力的性能。数据与代码公开于 https://github.com/Yuqifan1117/HalluciDoctor 。

English abstract

Multi-modal Large Language Models (MLLMs) tuned on machine-generated instruction-following data have demonstrated remarkable performance in various multi-modal understanding and generation tasks. However, the hallucinations inherent in machine-generated data, which could lead to hallucinatory outputs in MLLMs, remain under-explored. This work aims to investigate various hallucinations (i.e., object, relation, attribute hallucinations) and mitigate those hallucinatory toxicities in large-scale machine-generated visual instruction datasets. Drawing on the human ability to identify factual errors, we present a novel hallucination detection and elimination framework, HalluciDoctor, based on the cross-checking paradigm. We use our framework to identify and eliminate hallucinations in the training data automatically. Interestingly, HalluciDoctor also indicates that spurious correlations arising from long-tail object co-occurrences contribute to hallucinations. Based on that, we execute counterfactual visual instruction expansion to balance data distribution, thereby enhancing MLLMs' resistance to hallucinations. Comprehensive experiments on hallucination evaluation benchmarks show that our method successfully mitigates 44.6% hallucinations relatively and maintains competitive performance compared to LLaVA. The data and code for this paper are publicly available. \url{https://github.com/Yuqifan1117/HalluciDoctor}.

完整作者
Qifan Yu, Juncheng Li, Longhui Wei, Liang Pang, Wentao Ye, Bosheng Qin, Siliang Tang, Qi Tian, Yueting Zhuang

65 · 2023-11 · 原表 #35 偏好对齐与训练缓解

超越幻觉：幻觉感知的直接偏好优化（HA-DPO）

Beyond Hallucinations: Enhancing LVLMs through Hallucination-Aware Direct Preference Optimization

Zhiyuan Zhao, Bin Wang, Linke Ouyang et al.
ICME 2025

把幻觉问题重构为偏好选择任务，并设计高效流水线构造高质量正（无幻觉）/负（有幻觉）样本对做 DPO。

展开中英文摘要与表格信息

中文摘要（原表译文）

多模态大语言模型近年取得显著进展，但仍受一个常见问题困扰——"幻觉问题"，即模型生成的文本描述不准确地描绘甚至完全捏造了所关联图像的内容。本文提出一种新的解决方案：幻觉感知直接偏好优化（HA-DPO），将幻觉问题重新表述为偏好选择任务。当给定同一图像的两个回答（一个准确、一个含幻觉）时，训练模型倾向于无幻觉的回答。此外，本文提出一条高效流水线来构造正（无幻觉）/负（含幻觉）样本对，确保得到高质量、风格一致的数据集以支撑稳健的偏好学习。应用于三个主流多模态模型时，HA-DPO 显著减少了幻觉问题并增强了模型的泛化能力。值得注意的是，MiniGPT-4 在加入 HA-DPO 后提升显著：POPE 准确率从 51.13% 升至 86.13%（绝对提升 35%），MME 分数从 932.00 跃升至 1326.46（相对提升 42.32%）。代码、模型与数据集见 https://opendatalab.github.io/HA-DPO 。

English abstract

Multimodal large language models have made significant advancements in recent years, yet they still suffer from a common issue known as the "hallucination problem", in which the models generate textual descriptions that inaccurately depict or entirely fabricate content from associated images. This paper introduces a novel solution, Hallucination-Aware Direct Preference Optimization (HA-DPO), which reframes the hallucination problem as a preference selection task. The model is trained to favor the non-hallucinating response when presented with two responses of the same image (one accurate and one hallucinatory). Furthermore, this paper proposes an efficient pipeline for constructing positive~(non-hallucinatory) and negative~(hallucinatory) sample pairs, ensuring a high-quality, style-consistent dataset for robust preference learning. When applied to three mainstream multimodal models, HA-DPO significantly reduced hallucination issues and amplified the models' generalization capabilities. Notably, the MiniGPT-4 model, when enhanced with HA-DPO, demonstrated a substantial improvement: POPE accuracy rose from 51.13% to 86.13% (an absolute improvement of 35%), and the MME score surged from 932.00 to 1326.46 (a relative improvement of 42.32%). The codes, models, and datasets are made accessible at https://opendatalab.github.io/HA-DPO.

完整作者
Zhiyuan Zhao, Bin Wang, Linke Ouyang, Xiaoyi Dong, Jiaqi Wang, Conghui He

66 · 2023-12 · 原表 #36 偏好对齐与训练缓解

申博清单 #21 · 缓解方法 · 偏好对齐

RLHF-V：基于细粒度纠正反馈的行为对齐

RLHF-V: Towards Trustworthy MLLMs via Behavior Alignment from Fine-grained Correctional Human Feedback

Tianyu Yu, Yuan Yao, Haoye Zhang et al.
CVPR 2024

收集片段级人工纠正反馈并做稠密直接偏好优化，显著提升 MLLM 输出的视觉事实可靠性。

展开中英文摘要与表格信息

中文摘要（原表译文）

多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）近期在多模态理解、推理与交互方面展现出令人印象深刻的能力。然而现有 MLLMs 普遍存在严重的幻觉问题，生成的文本在事实上与所关联图像不符。该问题使现有 MLLMs 不可信，从而难以在真实世界（尤其是高风险）应用中落地。为应对这一挑战，我们提出 RLHF-V，通过基于细粒度纠正性人类反馈的行为对齐来提升 MLLM 的可信度。具体地，RLHF-V 以针对幻觉的片段级纠正形式收集人类偏好，并基于这些人类反馈进行稠密直接偏好优化。在五个基准上的全面实验（涵盖自动评测与人工评测）表明，RLHF-V 能以出色的数据与计算效率使 MLLM 行为显著更可信。值得注意的是，仅用 1.4k 条标注数据，RLHF-V 就将基础 MLLM 的幻觉率降低了 34.8%，优于使用 10k 条标注数据训练的同期工作 LLaVA-RLHF。最终模型在开源 MLLMs 中取得了可信度方面的最先进性能，并在防止由过度泛化引发的幻觉方面表现出比 GPT-4V 更好的鲁棒性。我们在 https://github.com/RLHF-V/RLHF-V 开源了代码、模型与数据。

English abstract

Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have recently demonstrated impressive capabilities in multimodal understanding, reasoning, and interaction. However, existing MLLMs prevalently suffer from serious hallucination problems, generating text that is not factually grounded in associated images. The problem makes existing MLLMs untrustworthy and thus impractical in real-world (especially high-stakes) applications. To address the challenge, we present RLHF-V, which enhances MLLM trustworthiness via behavior alignment from fine-grained correctional human feedback. Specifically, RLHF-V collects human preference in the form of segment-level corrections on hallucinations, and performs dense direct preference optimization over the human feedback. Comprehensive experiments on five benchmarks in both automatic and human evaluation show that, RLHF-V can enable substantially more trustworthy MLLM behaviors with promising data and computation efficiency. Remarkably, using 1.4k annotated data samples, RLHF-V significantly reduces the hallucination rate of the base MLLM by 34.8%, outperforming the concurrent LLaVA-RLHF trained on 10k annotated data. The final model achieves state-of-the-art performance in trustworthiness among open-source MLLMs, and shows better robustness than GPT-4V in preventing hallucinations aroused from over-generalization. We open-source our code, model, and data at https://github.com/RLHF-V/RLHF-V.

完整作者
Tianyu Yu, Yuan Yao, Haoye Zhang, Taiwen He, Yifeng Han, Ganqu Cui, Jinyi Hu, Zhiyuan Liu, Hai-Tao Zheng, Maosong Sun, Tat-Seng Chua

原表标题

67 · 2024-05 · 原表 #37 偏好对齐与训练缓解

通过幻觉诱导优化缓解 LVLM 幻觉（HIO）

Alleviating Hallucinations in Large Vision-Language Models through Hallucination-Induced Optimization

Xinyu Lyu, Beitao Chen, Lianli Gao et al.
NeurIPS 2024

针对全局视觉不确定性不可控导致幻觉 token 难以诱导的问题，主动构造幻觉诱导参考以获得更精确的对比优化目标。

展开中英文摘要与表格信息

中文摘要（原表译文）

尽管大型视觉语言模型（LVLMs）在理解多模态数据方面展现出非凡能力，它们仍不可避免地产生幻觉，导致生成文本与对应图像之间脱节。当前几乎所有视觉对比解码方法都试图通过引入视觉不确定性信息来缓解幻觉，以适当拉大幻觉 token 与目标 token 之间的对比 logits 差距。然而由于全局视觉不确定性不可控，它们难以精确诱导出幻觉 token，这严重限制了其缓解幻觉的效果，甚至可能诱发非预期的幻觉。为解决该问题，我们进行了理论分析以提升对比解码的有效性。基于该洞见，我们提出一种新的优化策略——幻觉诱导优化（HIO）。该策略基于经微调的理论偏好模型（即反向 Bradley-Terry 模型）放大幻觉 token 与目标 token 之间的对比，从而促成高效的对比解码以缓解 LVLMs 幻觉。大量实验研究表明，我们的 HIO 策略能有效减少 LVLMs 幻觉，在多个基准上优于最先进方法。

English abstract

Although Large Visual Language Models (LVLMs) have demonstrated exceptional abilities in understanding multimodal data, they invariably suffer from hallucinations, leading to a disconnect between the generated text and the corresponding images. Almost all current visual contrastive decoding methods attempt to mitigate these hallucinations by introducing visual uncertainty information that appropriately widens the contrastive logits gap between hallucinatory and targeted ones. However, due to uncontrollable nature of the global visual uncertainty, they struggle to precisely induce the hallucinatory tokens, which severely limits their effectiveness in mitigating hallucinations and may even lead to the generation of undesired hallucinations. To tackle this issue, we conducted the theoretical analysis to promote the effectiveness of contrast decoding. Building on this insight, we introduce a novel optimization strategy named Hallucination-Induced Optimization (HIO). This strategy seeks to amplify the contrast between hallucinatory and targeted tokens relying on a fine-tuned theoretical preference model (i.e., Contrary Bradley-Terry Model), thereby facilitating efficient contrast decoding to alleviate hallucinations in LVLMs. Extensive experimental research demonstrates that our HIO strategy can effectively reduce hallucinations in LVLMs, outperforming state-of-the-art methods across various benchmarks.

完整作者
Xinyu Lyu, Beitao Chen, Lianli Gao, Jingkuan Song, Heng Tao Shen
