68 · 2024-05 · 原表 #38 偏好对齐与训练缓解

数据增强短语级对齐缓解 MLLM 物体幻觉（DPA）

Mitigating Object Hallucination in MLLMs via Data-augmented Phrase-level Alignment

Pritam Sarkar, Sayna Ebrahimi, Ali Etemad et al.
ICLR 2025

在短语级构造「幻觉/正确」响应对并设计 DPA 损失，在保持通用视觉语言能力的同时降低物体幻觉。

展开中英文摘要与表格信息

中文摘要（原表译文）

尽管取得了显著进展，多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）仍常生成事实不准确的信息，即幻觉。本工作针对 MLLMs 的物体幻觉，即生成关于输入图像中并不存在的物体的信息。我们提出数据增强短语级对齐（DPA），一种可应用于已指令微调的现成 MLLMs 的新颖损失函数，在缓解幻觉的同时保持其通用视觉语言能力。为用 DPA 微调 MLLMs，我们首先在短语级选择性地改动正确回答的真值信息，通过生成式数据增强构造一组"含幻觉"与"正确"的回答对。随后用 DPA 损失训练 MLLMs，以降低含幻觉短语相对于正确短语的似然。我们在多个基准上的详尽评测证实，DPA 在缓解幻觉的同时保留了 MLLMs 在通用任务上的开箱即用性能。例如，用 DPA 微调后的模型（我们称之为幻觉衰减的语言与视觉助手 HALVA）在幻觉视觉问答上 F1 最多提升 13.4%，在图像描述任务上幻觉率最多降低 4.2%。

English abstract

Despite their significant advancements, Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) often generate factually inaccurate information, referred to as hallucination. In this work, we address object hallucinations in MLLMs, where information is generated about an object not present in the input image. We introduce Data-augmented Phrase-level Alignment (DPA), a novel loss which can be applied to instruction-tuned off-the-shelf MLLMs to mitigate hallucinations, while preserving their general vision-language capabilities. To fine-tune MLLMs with DPA, we first generate a set of `hallucinated' and `correct' response pairs through generative data augmentation by selectively altering the ground-truth information of the correct responses at a phrase level. The DPA loss is then used to train MLLMs to reduce the likelihood of hallucinated phrases compared to the correct ones. Our thorough evaluation on various benchmarks confirms the effectiveness of DPA in mitigating hallucination while retaining the out-of-the-box performance of the MLLMs on general tasks. For instance, MLLMs finetuned with DPA, which we refer to as Hallucination Attenuated Language and Vision Assistant (HALVA), improve F1 by up to 13.4% on hallucination visual question-answering and reduce the hallucination rate by up to 4.2% on image description tasks.

完整作者
Pritam Sarkar, Sayna Ebrahimi, Ali Etemad, Ahmad Beirami, Sercan Ö. Arık, Tomas Pfister

69 · 2024-11 · 原表 #39 偏好对齐与训练缓解

V-DPO：视觉引导的直接偏好优化

V-DPO: Mitigating Hallucination in Large Vision Language Models via Vision-Guided Direct Preference Optimization

Yuxi Xie, Guanzhen Li, Xiao Xu et al.
EMNLP 2024 (Findings)

指出幻觉源于对 LLM 主干的过度依赖造成视觉注意不足，用视觉引导偏好学习在训练时强化视觉上下文学习。

展开中英文摘要与表格信息

中文摘要（原表译文）

大型视觉语言模型（LVLMs）存在幻觉，导致输出的文本回答与输入视觉内容失配。近期研究表明，对大型语言模型（LLM）主干的过度依赖是 LVLM 幻觉的成因之一，它内在地引入语言先验偏差，导致对视觉输入的上下文注意不足。我们通过偏好学习缓解这种过度依赖，从而应对幻觉问题。我们提出视觉引导直接偏好优化（V-DPO），在训练时增强视觉上下文学习。为阐释 V-DPO 在不同类型训练数据上的有效性与泛化性，我们构建了一个同时包含"回答对比"与"图像对比"偏好对的合成数据集，并与现有的人类标注幻觉样本作对比。我们的方法在多个幻觉基准上相较基线方法取得显著提升。分析表明，V-DPO 更擅长从图像对比偏好数据中学习，显示出其在唤起并理解视觉上下文细微差别方面的优越能力。代码公开于 https://github.com/YuxiXie/V-DPO 。

English abstract

Large vision-language models (LVLMs) suffer from hallucination, resulting in misalignment between the output textual response and the input visual content. Recent research indicates that the over-reliance on the Large Language Model (LLM) backbone, as one cause of the LVLM hallucination, inherently introduces bias from language priors, leading to insufficient context attention to the visual inputs. We tackle this issue of hallucination by mitigating such over-reliance through preference learning. We propose Vision-guided Direct Preference Optimization (V-DPO) to enhance visual context learning at training time. To interpret the effectiveness and generalizability of V-DPO on different types of training data, we construct a synthetic dataset containing both response- and image-contrast preference pairs, compared against existing human-annotated hallucination samples. Our approach achieves significant improvements compared with baseline methods across various hallucination benchmarks. Our analysis indicates that V-DPO excels in learning from image-contrast preference data, demonstrating its superior ability to elicit and understand nuances of visual context. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/YuxiXie/V-DPO.

完整作者
Yuxi Xie, Guanzhen Li, Xiao Xu, Min-Yen Kan

70 · 2024-11 · 原表 #40 偏好对齐与训练缓解

面向幻觉的直接偏好优化（HDPO）

Mitigating Hallucination in Multimodal Large Language Model via Hallucination-targeted Direct Preference Optimization

Yuhan Fu, Ruobing Xie, Xingwu Sun et al.
arXiv 预印本（元数据未标注录用）

针对视觉能力不足、长文本生成、多模态冲突三类成因分别构造偏好数据，比通用 DPO 更有效地降低幻觉。

展开中英文摘要与表格信息

中文摘要（原表译文）

多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）已知会产生幻觉，这限制了其实际应用。近期工作尝试应用直接偏好优化（DPO）提升 MLLMs 性能，但在缓解幻觉方面的提升并不一致。为更有效地解决该问题，我们提出面向幻觉的直接偏好优化（HDPO）以减少 MLLMs 的幻觉。与以往方法不同，我们的方法从幻觉的多样形式与成因入手。具体地，我们针对以下三类 MLLM 幻觉成因分别构造了三类偏好对数据：(1) 视觉能力不足、(2) 长文本生成、(3) 多模态冲突。实验结果表明，我们的方法在多个幻觉评测数据集上取得优越性能，超越了大多数最先进（SOTA）方法，凸显了该方法的潜力。消融研究与深入分析进一步证实了方法的有效性，并表明通过扩大规模还有进一步提升的空间。

English abstract

Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) are known to hallucinate, which limits their practical applications. Recent works have attempted to apply Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) to enhance the performance of MLLMs, but have shown inconsistent improvements in mitigating hallucinations. To address this issue more effectively, we introduce Hallucination-targeted Direct Preference Optimization (HDPO) to reduce hallucinations in MLLMs. Unlike previous approaches, our method tackles hallucinations from their diverse forms and causes. Specifically, we develop three types of preference pair data targeting the following causes of MLLM hallucinations: (1) insufficient visual capabilities, (2) long context generation, and (3) multimodal conflicts. Experimental results demonstrate that our method achieves superior performance across multiple hallucination evaluation datasets, surpassing most state-of-the-art (SOTA) methods and highlighting the potential of our approach. Ablation studies and in-depth analyses further confirm the effectiveness of our method and suggest the potential for further improvements through scaling up.

完整作者
Yuhan Fu, Ruobing Xie, Xingwu Sun, Zhanhui Kang, Xirong Li

71 · 2024-12 · 原表 #41 偏好对齐与训练缓解

带自校准视觉锚定奖励的 token 级偏好优化（TPO）

Token Preference Optimization with Self-Calibrated Visual-Anchored Rewards for Hallucination Mitigation

Jihao Gu, Yingyao Wang, Meng Cao et al.
arXiv 预印本（元数据未标注录用）

用原图与扰动图条件下 token 分布之差构造 token 级视觉锚定奖励，实现免细粒度标注的偏好优化。

展开中英文摘要与表格信息

中文摘要（原表译文）

直接偏好优化（DPO）已被证明能通过使输出更贴近人类偏好，有效缓解大型视觉语言模型（LVLMs）的幻觉。尽管近期取得进展，现有方法仍有两个不足：1) 缺乏可扩展的 token 级奖励；2) 忽视了视觉锚定的 token。为此，我们提出一种带自校准奖励的新颖 token 偏好优化模型（简称 TPO），它无需细粒度标注即可自适应地关注与视觉相关的 token。具体地，我们引入 token 级"视觉锚定奖励"，定义为在原始图像与受损图像条件下生成 token 的逻辑分布之差。此外，为突出信息量大的视觉锚定 token，我们提出一个视觉感知训练目标，以实现更准确的 token 级优化。大量实验结果表明，所提出的 TPO 达到了最先进性能。例如，在 LLAVA-1.5-7B 之上构建的 TPO 在幻觉基准上带来了绝对的性能提升。

English abstract

Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) has been demonstrated to be highly effective in mitigating hallucinations in Large Vision Language Models (LVLMs) by aligning their outputs more closely with human preferences. Despite the recent progress, existing methods suffer from two drawbacks: 1) Lack of scalable token-level rewards; and 2) Neglect of visual-anchored tokens. To this end, we propose a novel Token Preference Optimization model with self-calibrated rewards (dubbed as TPO), which adaptively attends to visual-correlated tokens without fine-grained annotations. Specifically, we introduce a token-level \emph{visual-anchored} \emph{reward} as the difference of the logistic distributions of generated tokens conditioned on the raw image and the corrupted one. In addition, to highlight the informative visual-anchored tokens, a visual-aware training objective is proposed to enhance more accurate token-level optimization. Extensive experimental results have manifested the state-of-the-art performance of the proposed TPO. For example, by building on top of LLAVA-1.5-7B, our TPO boosts the performance absolute improvement for hallucination benchmarks.

完整作者
Jihao Gu, Yingyao Wang, Meng Cao, Pi Bu, Jun Song, Yancheng He, Shilong Li, Bo Zheng

72 · 2025-04 · 原表 #42 偏好对齐与训练缓解

PaMi-VDPO：提示感知的多实例视频偏好学习

PaMi-VDPO: Mitigating Video Hallucinations by Prompt-Aware Multi-Instance Video Preference Learning

Xinpeng Ding, Kui Zhang, Jianhua Han et al.
arXiv 预印本（元数据未标注录用）

用视频增强在线生成「被拒样本」以免标注，并按提示筛选有效增强，解决离线偏好数据适应性差与误拒问题。

展开中英文摘要与表格信息

中文摘要（原表译文）

直接偏好优化（DPO）有助于减少视频多模态大语言模型（VLLMs）的幻觉，但其对离线偏好数据的依赖限制了适应性，也无法捕捉真实的视频-回答失配。我们提出视频直接偏好优化（VDPO），一个在线偏好学习框架，它利用视频增强生成被拒样本、同时保持回答不变，从而免除偏好标注之需。然而，选择有效的增强并非易事：在某些提示下，部分片段可能与原视频在语义上完全相同，从而导致误拒并破坏对齐。为此，我们提出提示感知多实例学习 VDPO（PaMi-VDPO），它依据提示上下文来选择增强。我们不再只构造单个被拒样本，而是构建增强片段候选集并采用"由近及远"的选择策略：先确保所有片段语义相关，再优先选出最符合提示且最具区分度的片段。这使模型能更好地捕捉有意义的视觉差异、缓解幻觉，同时避免误拒并改善对齐。PaMi-VDPO 可无缝集成进现有 VLLMs，无需额外参数、也无需 GPT-4 或人工监督。仅用 10k 条 SFT 数据，它就在 VideoHallucer 上较基础模型提升 5.3%，超越 GPT-4o，同时在通用视频基准上保持稳定性能。

English abstract

Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) helps reduce hallucinations in Video Multimodal Large Language Models (VLLMs), but its reliance on offline preference data limits adaptability and fails to capture true video-response misalignment. We propose Video Direct Preference Optimization (VDPO), an online preference learning framework that eliminates the need for preference annotation by leveraging video augmentations to generate rejected samples while keeping responses fixed. However, selecting effective augmentations is non-trivial, as some clips may be semantically identical to the original under specific prompts, leading to false rejections and disrupting alignment. To address this, we introduce Prompt-aware Multi-instance Learning VDPO (PaMi-VDPO), which selects augmentations based on prompt context. Instead of a single rejection, we construct a candidate set of augmented clips and apply a close-to-far selection strategy, initially ensuring all clips are semantically relevant while then prioritizing the most prompt-aware distinct clip. This allows the model to better capture meaningful visual differences, mitigating hallucinations, while avoiding false rejections, and improving alignment. PaMi-VDPOseamlessly integrates into existing VLLMs without additional parameters, GPT-4/human supervision. With only 10k SFT data, it improves the base model by 5.3% on VideoHallucer, surpassing GPT-4o, while maintaining stable performance on general video benchmarks.

完整作者
Xinpeng Ding, Kui Zhang, Jianhua Han, Lanqing Hong, Hang Xu, Xiaomeng Li

73 · 2025-06 · 原表 #43 偏好对齐与训练缓解

理论一致对称多模态偏好优化（SymMPO）

Mitigating Hallucination Through Theory-Consistent Symmetric Multimodal Preference Optimization

Wenqi Liu, Xuemeng Song, Jiaxi Li et al.
NeurIPS 2025

提出与标准 DPO 理论严格一致的对称偏好学习，用直接的响应对监督强化视觉理解以降低幻觉。

展开中英文摘要与表格信息

中文摘要（原表译文）

直接偏好优化（DPO）已成为缓解多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）幻觉的有效途径。尽管现有方法通过引入面向视觉的对比目标增强了 MLLMs 对视觉输入的关注，从而减少了幻觉并取得显著进展，但它们存在优化目标函数不严谨与偏好监督间接的问题。为解决这些局限，我们提出对称多模态偏好优化（SymMPO），它以直接的偏好监督（即回答对）进行对称偏好学习以增强视觉理解，同时与标准 DPO 保持严谨的理论一致性。除常规的序数偏好学习外，SymMPO 还引入偏好间隔一致性损失，以定量调节对称偏好对之间的偏好差距。在五个基准上的全面评测表明 SymMPO 性能优越，验证了其在缓解 MLLMs 幻觉方面的有效性。

English abstract

Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) has emerged as an effective approach for mitigating hallucination in Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs). Although existing methods have achieved significant progress by utilizing vision-oriented contrastive objectives for enhancing MLLMs' attention to visual inputs and hence reducing hallucination, they suffer from non-rigorous optimization objective function and indirect preference supervision. To address these limitations, we propose a Symmetric Multimodal Preference Optimization (SymMPO), which conducts symmetric preference learning with direct preference supervision (i.e., response pairs) for visual understanding enhancement, while maintaining rigorous theoretical alignment with standard DPO. In addition to conventional ordinal preference learning, SymMPO introduces a preference margin consistency loss to quantitatively regulate the preference gap between symmetric preference pairs. Comprehensive evaluation across five benchmarks demonstrate SymMPO's superior performance, validating its effectiveness in hallucination mitigation of MLLMs.

完整作者
Wenqi Liu, Xuemeng Song, Jiaxi Li, Yinwei Wei, Na Zheng, Jianhua Yin, Liqiang Nie

74 · 2023-10 · 原表 #44 解码与推理时干预

申博清单 #20 · 缓解方法 · 免训练流水线

Woodpecker：面向 MLLM 的幻觉纠正

Woodpecker: Hallucination Correction for Multimodal Large Language Models

Shukang Yin, Chaoyou Fu, Sirui Zhao et al.
Science China Information Sciences, 2024

免训练的事后修补方案，经关键概念抽取、提问生成、视觉知识验证、视觉断言生成、幻觉纠正五阶段修正描述。

展开中英文摘要与表格信息

中文摘要（原表译文）

幻觉是笼罩在快速发展的多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）上的一道浓重阴影，指生成文本与图像内容不一致的现象。为缓解幻觉，现有研究主要采用指令微调方式，需要用特定数据重新训练模型。本文开辟了另一条路径，提出一种免训练方法 Woodpecker。正如啄木鸟医治树木，它从生成文本中挑出并纠正幻觉。具体而言，Woodpecker 包含五个阶段：关键概念抽取、问题构造、视觉知识验证、视觉断言生成与幻觉纠正。它以事后修补的方式实现，可方便地服务不同的 MLLMs，并可通过访问五个阶段的中间输出而具备可解释性。我们从定量与定性两方面评测 Woodpecker，展示了这一新范式的巨大潜力。在 POPE 基准上，我们的方法相较基线 MiniGPT-4/mPLUG-Owl 分别取得 30.66%/24.33% 的准确率提升。源代码发布于 https://github.com/BradyFU/Woodpecker 。

English abstract

Hallucination is a big shadow hanging over the rapidly evolving Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs), referring to the phenomenon that the generated text is inconsistent with the image content. In order to mitigate hallucinations, existing studies mainly resort to an instruction-tuning manner that requires retraining the models with specific data. In this paper, we pave a different way, introducing a training-free method named Woodpecker. Like a woodpecker heals trees, it picks out and corrects hallucinations from the generated text. Concretely, Woodpecker consists of five stages: key concept extraction, question formulation, visual knowledge validation, visual claim generation, and hallucination correction. Implemented in a post-remedy manner, Woodpecker can easily serve different MLLMs, while being interpretable by accessing intermediate outputs of the five stages. We evaluate Woodpecker both quantitatively and qualitatively and show the huge potential of this new paradigm. On the POPE benchmark, our method obtains a 30.66%/24.33% improvement in accuracy over the baseline MiniGPT-4/mPLUG-Owl. The source code is released at https://github.com/BradyFU/Woodpecker.

完整作者
Shukang Yin, Chaoyou Fu, Sirui Zhao, Tong Xu, Hao Wang, Dianbo Sui, Yunhang Shen, Ke Li, Xing Sun, Enhong Chen

免训练的多阶段纠正流水线（生成—验证—修正），为“推理期证据接地”路线提供可直接对比的基线。

75 · 2023-11 · 原表 #45 解码与推理时干预

申博清单 #16 · 缓解方法 · 解码期

视觉对比解码缓解 LVLM 物体幻觉（VCD）

Mitigating Object Hallucinations in Large Vision-Language Models through Visual Contrastive Decoding

Sicong Leng, Hang Zhang, Guanzheng Chen et al.
CVPR 2024

免训练方法：对比「原图」与「失真图」两路输出分布，压制统计偏差与单模态先验造成的物体幻觉。

展开中英文摘要与表格信息

中文摘要（原表译文）

大型视觉语言模型（LVLMs）已取得长足进步，将视觉识别与语言理解交织在一起，生成不仅连贯而且贴合语境的内容。尽管取得成功，LVLMs 仍受物体幻觉问题困扰，即模型生成看似合理却错误的输出，其中包含图像中并不存在的物体。为缓解该问题，我们提出视觉对比解码（VCD），一种简单且免训练的方法，它对比由原始视觉输入与失真视觉输入导出的输出分布。所提出的 VCD 有效减少了对统计偏差与单模态先验的过度依赖——这两者是物体幻觉的根本成因。这一调整确保生成内容与视觉输入紧密接地，从而得到语境上准确的输出。实验表明，VCD 无需额外训练或使用外部工具，即可在不同 LVLM 系列上显著缓解物体幻觉问题。除缓解物体幻觉外，VCD 在通用 LVLM 基准上同样表现出色，凸显了其广泛的适用性。

English abstract

Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs) have advanced considerably, intertwining visual recognition and language understanding to generate content that is not only coherent but also contextually attuned. Despite their success, LVLMs still suffer from the issue of object hallucinations, where models generate plausible yet incorrect outputs that include objects that do not exist in the images. To mitigate this issue, we introduce Visual Contrastive Decoding (VCD), a simple and training-free method that contrasts output distributions derived from original and distorted visual inputs. The proposed VCD effectively reduces the over-reliance on statistical bias and unimodal priors, two essential causes of object hallucinations. This adjustment ensures the generated content is closely grounded to visual inputs, resulting in contextually accurate outputs. Our experiments show that VCD, without either additional training or the usage of external tools, significantly mitigates the object hallucination issue across different LVLM families. Beyond mitigating object hallucinations, VCD also excels in general LVLM benchmarks, highlighting its wide-ranging applicability.

完整作者
Sicong Leng, Hang Zhang, Guanzheng Chen, Xin Li, Shijian Lu, Chunyan Miao, Lidong Bing

76 · 2023-11 · 原表 #46 解码与推理时干预

申博清单 #17 · 缓解方法 · 解码期

OPERA：过度信任惩罚与回溯重分配

OPERA: Alleviating Hallucination in Multi-Modal Large Language Models via Over-Trust Penalty and Retrospection-Allocation

Qidong Huang, Xiaoyi Dong, Pan Zhang et al.
CVPR 2024

发现幻觉与自注意力「知识聚合模式」密切相关，用过度信任惩罚加回溯重分配在束搜索中抑制幻觉，无需额外训练。

展开中英文摘要与表格信息

中文摘要（原表译文）

幻觉是多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）普遍存在的挑战，严重阻碍了其需要精确判断的真实场景应用。现有方法或以专门设计的数据进行训练，或借助来自其他来源的外部知识进行推理，都会带来不可避免的额外成本。本文提出 OPERA，一种基于过度信任惩罚与回溯重分配策略的新颖 MLLM 解码方法，可谓"近乎免费的午餐"——无需额外数据、知识或训练即可缓解幻觉问题。我们的方法始于一个有趣的观察：大多数幻觉与自注意力矩阵中体现的知识聚合模式密切相关，即 MLLMs 倾向于只关注少数汇总 token 而非全部先前 token 来生成新 token。这种局部性过度信任倾向导致忽视图像 token，从而带幻觉地描述图像内容。基于该观察，OPERA 在束搜索解码过程中对模型 logits 引入惩罚项以缓解过度信任，并配合一种回滚策略：回溯先前已生成 token 中是否存在汇总 token，必要时重新分配 token 选择。大量实验表明，OPERA 在不同 MLLMs 与指标上均表现出显著的幻觉缓解效果，证明了其有效性与通用性。代码见：https://github.com/shikiw/OPERA 。

English abstract

Hallucination, posed as a pervasive challenge of multi-modal large language models (MLLMs), has significantly impeded their real-world usage that demands precise judgment. Existing methods mitigate this issue with either training with specific designed data or inferencing with external knowledge from other sources, incurring inevitable additional costs. In this paper, we present OPERA, a novel MLLM decoding method grounded in an Over-trust Penalty and a Retrospection-Allocation strategy, serving as a nearly free lunch to alleviate the hallucination issue without additional data, knowledge, or training. Our approach begins with an interesting observation that, most hallucinations are closely tied to the knowledge aggregation patterns manifested in the self-attention matrix, i.e., MLLMs tend to generate new tokens by focusing on a few summary tokens, but not all the previous tokens. Such partial over-trust inclination results in the neglecting of image tokens and describes the image content with hallucination. Based on the observation, OPERA introduces a penalty term on the model logits during the beam-search decoding to mitigate the over-trust issue, along with a rollback strategy that retrospects the presence of summary tokens in the previously generated tokens, and re-allocate the token selection if necessary. With extensive experiments, OPERA shows significant hallucination-mitigating performance on different MLLMs and metrics, proving its effectiveness and generality. Our code is available at: https://github.com/shikiw/OPERA.

完整作者
Qidong Huang, Xiaoyi Dong, Pan Zhang, Bin Wang, Conghui He, Jiaqi Wang, Dahua Lin, Weiming Zhang, Nenghai Yu

揭示“过度信任少数视觉 token”是幻觉的结构性成因，并给出惩罚+回滚机制，是“从注意力信号检测幻觉”的关键依据。

原表标题

77 · 2024-03 · 原表 #47 解码与推理时干预

HALC：自适应焦点对比解码

HALC: Object Hallucination Reduction via Adaptive Focal-Contrast Decoding

Zhaorun Chen, Zhuokai Zhao, Hongyin Luo et al.
ICML 2024

结合自适应焦点定位（局部）与专用束搜索（全局）在线修正幻觉 token，可即插即用且无需额外训练。

展开中英文摘要与表格信息

中文摘要（原表译文）

尽管大型视觉语言模型（LVLMs）在解读多模态上下文方面展现出令人印象深刻的能力，它们仍不可避免地受物体幻觉（OH）困扰。我们提出 HALC，一种旨在缓解 LVLMs 物体幻觉的新颖解码算法。HALC 利用视觉语言任务中独特的细粒度最优视觉信息，同时作用于局部与全局上下文。具体地，HALC 在局部集成一个稳健的自动焦点接地机制以即时纠正含幻觉的 token，在全局采用专门的束搜索算法，在保持文本生成质量的同时显著减少物体幻觉。此外，HALC 可作为即插即用模块集成到任意 LVLMs 中，无需额外训练。大量实验研究证明了 HALC 在减少物体幻觉方面的有效性，在四个基准上优于最先进方法。

English abstract

While large vision-language models (LVLMs) have demonstrated impressive capabilities in interpreting multi-modal contexts, they invariably suffer from object hallucinations (OH). We introduce HALC, a novel decoding algorithm designed to mitigate OH in LVLMs. HALC leverages distinct fine-grained optimal visual information in vision-language tasks and operates on both local and global contexts simultaneously. Specifically, HALC integrates a robust auto-focal grounding mechanism (locally) to correct hallucinated tokens on the fly, and a specialized beam search algorithm (globally) to significantly reduce OH while preserving text generation quality. Additionally, HALC can be integrated into any LVLMs as a plug-and-play module without extra training. Extensive experimental studies demonstrate the effectiveness of HALC in reducing OH, outperforming state-of-the-arts across four benchmarks.

完整作者
Zhaorun Chen, Zhuokai Zhao, Hongyin Luo, Huaxiu Yao, Bo Li, Jiawei Zhou

78 · 2024-03 · 原表 #48 解码与推理时干预

用视觉信息接地控制多模态幻觉（M3ID）

Multi-Modal Hallucination Control by Visual Information Grounding

Alessandro Favero, Luca Zancato, Matthew Trager et al.
CVPR 2024

揭示随生成 token 增多模型对视觉提示的依赖下降正是幻觉诱因，提出 M3ID 放大图像影响、偏向与视觉高互信息的 token。

展开中英文摘要与表格信息

中文摘要（原表译文）

生成式视觉语言模型（VLMs）容易生成听起来合理、却并不总是基于输入图像的文本回答。我们研究这一通常被称为"幻觉"的现象，并表明它源于对语言先验的过度依赖。特别地，我们表明随着生成的 token 增多，对视觉提示的依赖会下降，而这一行为与幻觉的出现强相关。为减少幻觉，我们提出多模态互信息解码（M3ID），一种用于提示放大的新采样方法。M3ID 放大参考图像相对于语言先验的影响，从而偏向生成与视觉提示互信息更高的 token。M3ID 可在推理时应用于任意预训练自回归 VLM，无需进一步训练，计算开销极小。如果能训练，我们还表明 M3ID 可与直接偏好优化（DPO）配合，在不需要任何标签的情况下提升模型对提示图像的依赖。实证结果表明，我们的算法在减少视觉未接地回答从而降低幻觉的同时，保持了预训练 VLMs 的流畅性与语言能力。具体地，对 LLaVA 13B 模型，M3ID 与 M3ID+DPO 分别将描述任务中含幻觉物体的比例降低 25% 与 28%，并将 POPE 等 VQA 基准的准确率提升 21% 与 24%。

English abstract

Generative Vision-Language Models (VLMs) are prone to generate plausible-sounding textual answers that, however, are not always grounded in the input image. We investigate this phenomenon, usually referred to as "hallucination" and show that it stems from an excessive reliance on the language prior. In particular, we show that as more tokens are generated, the reliance on the visual prompt decreases, and this behavior strongly correlates with the emergence of hallucinations. To reduce hallucinations, we introduce Multi-Modal Mutual-Information Decoding (M3ID), a new sampling method for prompt amplification. M3ID amplifies the influence of the reference image over the language prior, hence favoring the generation of tokens with higher mutual information with the visual prompt. M3ID can be applied to any pre-trained autoregressive VLM at inference time without necessitating further training and with minimal computational overhead. If training is an option, we show that M3ID can be paired with Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) to improve the model's reliance on the prompt image without requiring any labels. Our empirical findings show that our algorithms maintain the fluency and linguistic capabilities of pre-trained VLMs while reducing hallucinations by mitigating visually ungrounded answers. Specifically, for the LLaVA 13B model, M3ID and M3ID+DPO reduce the percentage of hallucinated objects in captioning tasks by 25% and 28%, respectively, and improve the accuracy on VQA benchmarks such as POPE by 21% and 24%.

完整作者
Alessandro Favero, Luca Zancato, Matthew Trager, Siddharth Choudhary, Pramuditha Perera, Alessandro Achille, Ashwin Swaminathan, Stefano Soatto

79 · 2024-05 · 原表 #49 解码与推理时干预

RITUAL：随机图像变换作为通用反幻觉手段

RITUAL: Random Image Transformations as a Universal Anti-hallucination Lever in Large Vision Language Models

Sangmin Woo, Jaehyuk Jang, Donguk Kim et al.
arXiv 预印本（元数据未标注录用）

用随机变换后的图像作为互补输入调整输出概率分布，借多样视觉视角纠正导致幻觉的误读。

展开中英文摘要与表格信息

中文摘要（原表译文）

大型视觉语言模型（LVLMs）的近期进展彻底改变了机器基于视觉输入理解并生成文本回答的方式，但它们常产生误读视觉信息的"幻觉"输出，给可靠性与可信度带来挑战。我们提出 RITUAL，一种简单的解码方法，在解码过程中利用随机变换后的图像作为互补输入来调整输出概率分布，从而减少幻觉，无需额外训练或外部模型。我们的关键洞见是：随机变换让模型接触到多样的视觉视角，从而能够纠正导致幻觉的误读。具体地，当模型基于原始图像产生幻觉时，变换后的图像——在朝向、尺度或颜色等方面被改变——提供了替代视角，帮助重新校准模型的预测。通过融合来自原始图像与变换图像的概率分布，RITUAL 有效减少了幻觉。为进一步提升可靠性并应对任意变换可能带来的不稳定性，我们提出扩展版 RITUAL+，它依据 LVLM 的自反馈来选择图像变换：RITUAL+ 不再随机施加变换，而是用 LVLM 评估并挑选在给定上下文中对减少幻觉最有帮助的变换。这种自适应方式缓解了某些变换对特定任务可能的负面影响，确保在不同场景下性能更一致。实验表明，RITUAL 与 RITUAL+ 在多个物体幻觉基准上显著减少了幻觉。

English abstract

Recent advancements in Large Vision Language Models (LVLMs) have revolutionized how machines understand and generate textual responses based on visual inputs, yet they often produce "hallucinatory" outputs that misinterpret visual information, posing challenges in reliability and trustworthiness. We propose RITUAL, a simple decoding method that reduces hallucinations by leveraging randomly transformed images as complementary inputs during decoding, adjusting the output probability distribution without additional training or external models. Our key insight is that random transformations expose the model to diverse visual perspectives, enabling it to correct misinterpretations that lead to hallucinations. Specifically, when a model hallucinates based on the original image, the transformed images -- altered in aspects such as orientation, scale, or color -- provide alternative viewpoints that help recalibrate the model's predictions. By integrating the probability distributions from both the original and transformed images, RITUAL effectively reduces hallucinations. To further improve reliability and address potential instability from arbitrary transformations, we introduce RITUAL+, an extension that selects image transformations based on self-feedback from the LVLM. Instead of applying transformations randomly, RITUAL+ uses the LVLM to evaluate and choose transformations that are most beneficial for reducing hallucinations in a given context. This self-adaptive approach mitigates the potential negative impact of certain transformations on specific tasks, ensuring more consistent performance across different scenarios. Experiments demonstrate that RITUAL and RITUAL+ significantly reduce hallucinations across several object hallucination benchmarks.

完整作者
Sangmin Woo, Jaehyuk Jang, Donguk Kim, Yubin Choi, Changick Kim

80 · 2024-05 · 原表 #50 解码与推理时干预

NoiseBoost：用噪声扰动缓解 MLLM 幻觉

NoiseBoost: Alleviating Hallucination with Noise Perturbation for Multimodal Large Language Models

Kai Wu, Boyuan Jiang, Zhengkai Jiang et al.
arXiv 预印本（元数据未标注录用）

指出长篇描述幻觉源于 LLM 的摘要机制导致过度依赖语言 token，用噪声特征扰动平衡视觉与语言注意力权重。

展开中英文摘要与表格信息

中文摘要（原表译文）

多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）在大型语言模型基础上提供了理解视觉信息的强大机制。然而 MLLMs 因幻觉而声名不佳，在为图像生成长而细致的描述时尤其如此。我们的分析揭示，幻觉源于大型语言模型固有的摘要机制，导致过度依赖语言 token 而忽视视觉信息。本文提出 NoiseBoost，一种可广泛适用且简单的、通过引入噪声特征扰动来缓解 MLLMs 幻觉的方法。噪声扰动起到正则化作用，促使注意力权重在视觉 token 与语言 token 之间均衡分布。尽管简单，NoiseBoost 在包括监督微调与强化学习在内的常见训练策略下都能稳定提升 MLLMs 性能。此外，NoiseBoost 还开创性地使 MLLMs 的半监督学习成为可能，释放了无标注数据的潜力。综合实验表明，NoiseBoost 在人工评测下将密集描述准确率提升 8.1%，并能通过挖掘无标注数据以 50% 的数据量取得相当的结果。代码与模型见 https://kaiwu5.github.io/noiseboost 。

English abstract

Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) contribute a powerful mechanism to understanding visual information building on large language models. However, MLLMs are notorious for suffering from hallucinations, especially when generating lengthy, detailed descriptions for images. Our analysis reveals that hallucinations stem from the inherent summarization mechanism of large language models, leading to excessive dependence on linguistic tokens while neglecting vision information. In this paper, we propose NoiseBoost, a broadly applicable and simple method for alleviating hallucinations for MLLMs through the integration of noise feature perturbations. Noise perturbation acts as a regularizer, facilitating a balanced distribution of attention weights among visual and linguistic tokens. Despite its simplicity, NoiseBoost consistently enhances the performance of MLLMs across common training strategies, including supervised fine-tuning and reinforcement learning. Further, NoiseBoost pioneerly enables semi-supervised learning for MLLMs, unleashing the power of unlabeled data. Comprehensive experiments demonstrate that NoiseBoost improves dense caption accuracy by 8.1% with human evaluation and achieves comparable results with 50% of the data by mining unlabeled data. Code and models are available at https://kaiwu5.github.io/noiseboost.

完整作者
Kai Wu, Boyuan Jiang, Zhengkai Jiang, Qingdong He, Donghao Luo, Shengzhi Wang, Qingwen Liu, Chengjie Wang
发表时间
2024-05
原表序号
50
研究方向
解码与推理时干预
关键词
噪声扰动；注意力正则；长描述幻觉；即插即用
发表载体类型
预印本

81 · 2024-06 · 原表 #51 解码与推理时干预

CODE：以自生成描述为对比参考抑制幻觉

CODE: Contrasting Self-generated Description to Combat Hallucination in Large Multi-modal Models

Junho Kim, Hyunjun Kim, Yeonju Kim et al.
arXiv 预印本（元数据未标注录用）

用模型自身生成的图像描述作为对比参考，在解码期动态调整信息以纠正与真实视觉内容不符的响应。

展开中英文摘要与表格信息

中文摘要（原表译文）

大型多模态模型（LMMs）近期在视觉上下文理解与连贯回答生成方面展现出卓越能力。然而在这些进展之外，幻觉问题已成为重大挑战，产生与视觉内容无关的错误回答。本文提出一种新的基于对比的解码方法——对立描述对比解码（CODE），它在 LMMs 的解码阶段利用自生成的描述作为对比参考来应对幻觉问题。CODE 将模型自身给出的全面描述作为视觉对照物，以纠正并改善回答与实际视觉内容的对齐。通过动态调整信息流以及 LMM 词表中下一 token 预测的分布，CODE 提升了所生成回答的连贯性与信息量。大量实验表明，我们的方法在多种基准与前沿 LMMs 上显著减少幻觉并改善跨模态一致性。该方法提供了一种简单而有效的解码策略，无需额外训练即可集成到现有 LMM 框架中。

English abstract

Large Multi-modal Models (LMMs) have recently demonstrated remarkable abilities in visual context understanding and coherent response generation. However, alongside these advancements, the issue of hallucinations has emerged as a significant challenge, producing erroneous responses that are unrelated to the visual contents. In this paper, we introduce a novel contrastive-based decoding method, COuntering DEscription Contrastive Decoding (CODE), which leverages self-generated descriptions as contrasting references during the decoding phase of LMMs to address hallucination issues. CODE utilizes the comprehensive descriptions from model itself as visual counterpart to correct and improve response alignment with actual visual content. By dynamically adjusting the information flow and distribution of next-token predictions in the LMM's vocabulary, CODE enhances the coherence and informativeness of generated responses. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method significantly reduces hallucinations and improves cross-modal consistency across various benchmarks and cutting-edge LMMs. Our method provides a simple yet effective decoding strategy that can be integrated to existing LMM frameworks without additional training.

完整作者
Junho Kim, Hyunjun Kim, Yeonju Kim, Yong Man Ro

82 · 2024-10 · 原表 #52 解码与推理时干预

MLLM 真的看见了吗？动态纠正解码（DeCo）

MLLM can see? Dynamic Correction Decoding for Hallucination Mitigation

Chenxi Wang, Xiang Chen, Ningyu Zhang et al.
ICLR 2025

发现模型在浅层其实「看得见」物体、幻觉源于语言先验对视觉信息的压制，提出动态纠正解码融合前层知识校正最终 logits。

展开中英文摘要与表格信息

中文摘要（原表译文）

多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）频繁出现幻觉现象，但其深层原因仍了解不足。本文通过实证分析发现：尽管 MLLMs 在最终输出中错误生成了物体，它们实际上能够在前面的层中识别出视觉物体。我们推测，这可能是由于语言模型强大的知识先验压制了视觉信息，从而导致幻觉。受此启发，我们提出一种新的 MLLM 动态纠正解码方法 DeCo，它自适应地选择合适的先前层，并按比例将知识整合到最后一层以调整输出 logits。需要指出，DeCo 与模型无关，可无缝结合各类经典解码策略并应用于不同的 MLLMs。我们在广泛使用的基准上评测 DeCo，结果表明相较基线它能大幅降低幻觉率，凸显了缓解幻觉的潜力。代码见 https://github.com/zjunlp/DeCo 。

English abstract

Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) frequently exhibit hallucination phenomena, but the underlying reasons remain poorly understood. In this paper, we present an empirical analysis and find that, although MLLMs incorrectly generate the objects in the final output, they are actually able to recognize visual objects in the preceding layers. We speculate that this may be due to the strong knowledge priors of the language model suppressing the visual information, leading to hallucinations. Motivated by this, we propose a novel dynamic correction decoding method for MLLMs DeCo, which adaptively selects the appropriate preceding layers and proportionally integrates knowledge into the final layer to adjust the output logits. Note that DeCo is model agnostic and can be seamlessly incorporated with various classic decoding strategies and applied to different MLLMs. We evaluate DeCo on widely-used benchmarks, demonstrating that it can reduce hallucination rates by a large margin compared to baselines, highlighting its potential to mitigate hallucinations. Code is available at https://github.com/zjunlp/DeCo.

完整作者
Chenxi Wang, Xiang Chen, Ningyu Zhang, Bozhong Tian, Haoming Xu, Shumin Deng, Huajun Chen

83 · 2024-12 · 原表 #53 解码与推理时干预

深入探究视觉对比解码

Delve into Visual Contrastive Decoding for Hallucination Mitigation of Large Vision-Language Models

Yi-Lun Lee, Yi-Hsuan Tsai, Wei-Chen Chiu
arXiv 预印本（元数据未标注录用）

系统比较不同视觉信息改变方式对对比解码效果的影响，深化对该免训练范式工作机理的理解。

展开中英文摘要与表格信息

中文摘要（原表译文）

尽管大型视觉语言模型（LVLMs）在生成与输入视觉内容相关的合理回答方面表现出色，它们仍受幻觉困扰，即生成的文本不准确地反映视觉内容。为此，近期方法采用对比解码，通过对比原始样本与视觉失真样本的输出分布来校准模型回答，以免训练方式展现出可观的幻觉缓解效果。然而，改变视觉输入中信息的潜力尚未被充分探索，因此更深入地研究视觉对比解码的行为具有重要意义。本文首先探索了多种用于对比解码的视觉内容改变方式，包括图像下采样与图像编辑。下采样减少图像的细节文本信息，而编辑则在图像中产生新内容，二者提供了不同方面的视觉对比样本。为进一步研究使用不同对比样本所带来的收益，我们分析了概率层面的指标，包括熵与分布距离。有趣的是，这些样本在缓解幻觉上的效果在不同 LVLMs 与基准之间差异很大。基于我们的分析，我们提出一种简单而有效的对比样本融合方法，为在不同场景下应用对比解码提供了实用方案。我们开展了大量实验以验证所提出的融合方法在不同基准上的表现。

English abstract

While large vision-language models (LVLMs) have shown impressive capabilities in generating plausible responses correlated with input visual contents, they still suffer from hallucinations, where the generated text inaccurately reflects visual contents. To address this, recent approaches apply contrastive decoding to calibrate the model's response via contrasting output distributions with original and visually distorted samples, demonstrating promising hallucination mitigation in a training-free manner. However, the potential of changing information in visual inputs is not well-explored, so a deeper investigation into the behaviors of visual contrastive decoding is of great interest. In this paper, we first explore various methods for contrastive decoding to change visual contents, including image downsampling and editing. Downsampling images reduces the detailed textual information while editing yields new contents in images, providing new aspects as visual contrastive samples. To further study benefits by using different contrastive samples, we analyze probability-level metrics, including entropy and distribution distance. Interestingly, the effect of these samples in mitigating hallucinations varies a lot across LVLMs and benchmarks. Based on our analysis, we propose a simple yet effective method to combine contrastive samples, offering a practical solution for applying contrastive decoding across various scenarios. Extensive experiments are conducted to validate the proposed fusion method among different benchmarks.

完整作者
Yi-Lun Lee, Yi-Hsuan Tsai, Wei-Chen Chiu
发表时间
2024-12

84 · 2025-03 · 原表 #54 解码与推理时干预

Octopus：动态对比解码缓解幻觉

Octopus: Alleviating Hallucination via Dynamic Contrastive Decoding

Wei Suo, Lijun Zhang, Mengyang Sun et al.
arXiv 预印本（元数据未标注录用）

指出幻觉成因是混合的、每个生成步面临不同挑战，进而提出按生成步自适应的动态对比解码。

展开中英文摘要与表格信息

中文摘要（原表译文）

大型视觉语言模型（LVLMs）在视觉内容理解与多模态推理上取得了令人印象深刻的性能。遗憾的是，这些大模型受严重幻觉问题困扰，倾向于生成捏造的回答。近来已有若干对比解码（CD）策略通过引入受扰输入来缓解幻觉。尽管取得很大进展，这些 CD 策略大多对所有输入条件采用"一刀切"的做法。本文通过大量实验重新审视这一过程。相关结果表明，幻觉成因是混合的，且每个生成步都面临独特的幻觉挑战。基于这些有意义的洞见，我们提出一个简单而有效的 Octopus 式框架，使模型能够自适应地识别幻觉类型并构建动态的 CD 工作流。我们的 Octopus 框架不仅在四个基准上优于现有方法，还展现出出色的可部署性与可扩展性。代码见 https://github.com/LijunZhang01/Octopus 。

English abstract

Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs) have obtained impressive performance in visual content understanding and multi-modal reasoning. Unfortunately, these large models suffer from serious hallucination problems and tend to generate fabricated responses. Recently, several Contrastive Decoding (CD) strategies have been proposed to alleviate hallucination by introducing disturbed inputs. Although great progress has been made, these CD strategies mostly apply a one-size-fits-all approach for all input conditions. In this paper, we revisit this process through extensive experiments. Related results show that hallucination causes are hybrid and each generative step faces a unique hallucination challenge. Leveraging these meaningful insights, we introduce a simple yet effective Octopus-like framework that enables the model to adaptively identify hallucination types and create a dynamic CD workflow. Our Octopus framework not only outperforms existing methods across four benchmarks but also demonstrates excellent deployability and expansibility. Code is available at https://github.com/LijunZhang01/Octopus.

完整作者
Wei Suo, Lijun Zhang, Mengyang Sun, Lin Yuanbo Wu, Peng Wang, Yanning Zhang
发表时间
2025-03

85 · 2025-04 · 原表 #55 解码与推理时干预

生成后验证：回溯重采样降低 VLM 幻觉（REVERSE）

Generate, but Verify: Reducing Hallucination in Vision-Language Models with Retrospective Resampling

Tsung-Han Wu, Heekyung Lee, Jiaxin Ge et al.
NeurIPS 2025

统一「生成调整」与「事后验证」两条路线，用可插拔幻觉检测器驱动回溯重采样，对输出做细化而非直接否决。

展开中英文摘要与表格信息

中文摘要（原表译文）

视觉语言模型（VLMs）擅长视觉理解，但常受视觉幻觉困扰，即生成并不存在的物体、动作或概念的描述，这在安全攸关的应用中带来重大风险。现有幻觉缓解方法通常遵循两种范式之一：生成调整——修改解码行为以让文本对齐视觉输入；事后验证——由外部模型评估并纠正输出。两者虽有效，但生成调整方法往往依赖启发式规则且缺乏纠正机制，而事后验证则较为复杂，通常需要多个模型，且倾向于否决输出而非改进它。本工作提出 REVERSE，一个将幻觉感知训练与即时自验证相统一的框架。借助一个包含超过 1.3M（约 130 万）半合成样本的新幻觉验证数据集，以及一种新颖的推理时回溯重采样技术，我们的方法使 VLMs 能够在生成过程中检测幻觉并动态修正这些幻觉。评测显示，REVERSE 实现了最先进的幻觉削减效果，在 CHAIR-MSCOCO 上最多优于现有最佳方法 12%，在 HaloQuest 上优于 34%。数据集、模型与代码见：https://reverse-vlm.github.io 。

English abstract

Vision-Language Models (VLMs) excel at visual understanding but often suffer from visual hallucinations, where they generate descriptions of nonexistent objects, actions, or concepts, posing significant risks in safety-critical applications. Existing hallucination mitigation methods typically follow one of two paradigms: generation adjustment, which modifies decoding behavior to align text with visual inputs, and post-hoc verification, where external models assess and correct outputs. While effective, generation adjustment methods often rely on heuristics and lack correction mechanisms, while post-hoc verification is complicated, typically requiring multiple models and tending to reject outputs rather than refine them. In this work, we introduce REVERSE, a unified framework that integrates hallucination-aware training with on-the-fly self-verification. By leveraging a new hallucination-verification dataset containing over 1.3M semi-synthetic samples, along with a novel inference-time retrospective resampling technique, our approach enables VLMs to both detect hallucinations during generation and dynamically revise those hallucinations. Our evaluations show that REVERSE achieves state-of-the-art hallucination reduction, outperforming the best existing methods by up to 12% on CHAIR-MSCOCO and 34% on HaloQuest. Our dataset, model, and code are available at: https://reverse-vlm.github.io.

完整作者
Tsung-Han Wu, Heekyung Lee, Jiaxin Ge, Joseph E. Gonzalez, Trevor Darrell, David M. Chan

86 · 2024-10 · 原表 #56 注意力与表征干预

解释与编辑视觉语言表征以缓解幻觉

Interpreting and Editing Vision-Language Representations to Mitigate Hallucinations

Nick Jiang, Anish Kachinthaya, Suzie Petryk et al.
ICLR 2025

把图像内部表征投影到词表发现真实物体置信度更高，据此正交擦除幻觉物体特征，COCO2014 上最多降低 25.7% 幻觉。

展开中英文摘要与表格信息

中文摘要（原表译文）

我们研究视觉语言模型（VLMs）的内部表征以应对幻觉——尽管模型规模与训练不断进步，幻觉仍是一个持续挑战。我们将 VLMs 的内部图像表征投影到其语言词表，观察到真实物体上的输出概率比幻觉物体上更有信心。我们还利用这些输出概率对真实物体进行空间定位。基于该方法，我们提出一种知识擦除算法，通过将图像特征对幻觉物体特征做线性正交化来消除幻觉。我们表明，对模型潜在表征的有针对性编辑可在 COCO2014 数据集上将幻觉减少最多 25.7%，同时保持性能。我们的发现展示了更深入理解 VLMs 潜在表征如何能提升可靠性并带来零样本分割等新能力。

English abstract

We investigate the internal representations of vision-language models (VLMs) to address hallucinations, a persistent challenge despite advances in model size and training. We project VLMs' internal image representations to their language vocabulary and observe more confident output probabilities on real objects than hallucinated objects. We additionally use these output probabilities to spatially localize real objects. Building on this approach, we introduce a knowledge erasure algorithm that removes hallucinations by linearly orthogonalizing image features with respect to hallucinated object features. We show that targeted edits to a model's latent representations can reduce hallucinations by up to 25.7% on the COCO2014 dataset while preserving performance. Our findings demonstrate how a deeper understanding of VLMs' latent representations can enhance reliability and enable novel capabilities, such as zero-shot segmentation.

完整作者
Nick Jiang, Anish Kachinthaya, Suzie Petryk, Yossi Gandelsman

87 · 2024-10 · 原表 #57 注意力与表征干预

DAMRO：从 LVLM 注意力机制入手缓解物体幻觉

DAMRO: Dive into the Attention Mechanism of LVLM to Reduce Object Hallucination

Xuan Gong, Tianshi Ming, Xinpeng Wang et al.
EMNLP 2024

发现解码器对图像 token 的注意力分布与视觉编码器高度一致地偏向背景而非被指物体，据此设计注意力修正以减幻觉。

展开中英文摘要与表格信息

中文摘要（原表译文）

尽管大型视觉语言模型（LVLMs）取得了巨大成功，它们仍不可避免地受幻觉困扰。我们知道，LVLMs 中的视觉编码器与大型语言模型（LLM）解码器都基于 Transformer，使模型能够通过注意力机制抽取视觉信息并生成文本输出。我们发现，LLM 解码器对图像 token 的注意力分布与视觉编码器高度一致，且两种分布都倾向于关注某些特定的背景 token，而非图像中被指涉的物体。我们将这种意料之外的注意力分布归因于视觉编码器本身的固有缺陷，它误导 LLMs 过度强调冗余信息并产生物体幻觉。为解决该问题，我们提出 DAMRO，一种新颖的免训练策略，深入 LVLM 的注意力机制以减少物体幻觉。具体地，我们的方法利用 ViT 的分类 token（CLS）过滤掉散落在背景中的高注意力离群 token，然后在解码阶段消除它们的影响。我们在 LLaVA-1.5、LLaVA-NeXT 与 InstructBLIP 等 LVLMs 上，使用 POPE、CHAIR、MME 以及 GPT-4V 辅助评测等多种基准评测了我们的方法。结果表明，我们的方法显著降低了这些离群 token 的影响，从而有效缓解了 LVLMs 的幻觉。代码发布于 https://github.com/coder-gx/DAMRO 。

English abstract

Despite the great success of Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs), they inevitably suffer from hallucination. As we know, both the visual encoder and the Large Language Model (LLM) decoder in LVLMs are Transformer-based, allowing the model to extract visual information and generate text outputs via attention mechanisms. We find that the attention distribution of LLM decoder on image tokens is highly consistent with the visual encoder and both distributions tend to focus on particular background tokens rather than the referred objects in the image. We attribute to the unexpected attention distribution to an inherent flaw in the visual encoder itself, which misguides LLMs to over emphasize the redundant information and generate object hallucination. To address the issue, we propose DAMRO, a novel training-free strategy that $D$ive into $A$ttention $M$echanism of LVLM to $R$educe $O$bject Hallucination. Specifically, our approach employs classification token (CLS) of ViT to filter out high-attention outlier tokens scattered in the background and then eliminate their influence during decoding stage. We evaluate our method on LVLMs including LLaVA-1.5, LLaVA-NeXT and InstructBLIP, using various benchmarks such as POPE, CHAIR, MME and GPT-4V Aided Evaluation. The results demonstrate that our approach significantly reduces the impact of these outlier tokens, thus effectively alleviating the hallucination of LVLMs. The code is released at https://github.com/coder-gx/DAMRO.

完整作者
Xuan Gong, Tianshi Ming, Xinpeng Wang, Zhihua Wei

88 · 2024-10 · 原表 #58 注意力与表征干预

同心因果注意力缓解物体幻觉（CCA）

Mitigating Object Hallucination via Concentric Causal Attention

Yun Xing, Yiheng Li, Ivan Laptev et al.
NeurIPS 2024

揭示幻觉与 RoPE 长程衰减相关（视觉线索离指令 token 越远越易幻觉），提出同心因果注意力重排视觉 token 的依赖结构。

展开中英文摘要与表格信息

中文摘要（原表译文）

近期的大型视觉语言模型（LVLMs）在给定多模态查询时展现出出色的零样本对话与推理能力。然而它们受物体幻觉困扰，即 LVLMs 倾向于生成与图像输入在事实上不一致的文本回答。我们的先导研究发现，物体幻觉与旋转位置编码（RoPE）密切相关——RoPE 是现有 LVLMs 中广泛采用的位置依赖建模设计。由于 RoPE 的长程衰减，当相关视觉线索在多模态输入序列中距指令 token 较远时，LVLMs 往往更容易产生幻觉。此外，我们在多模态对齐过程中反转视觉 token 的顺序时也观察到类似效应。我们的测试表明，RoPE 的长程衰减使 LVLMs 在捕捉长距离的视觉-指令交互时面临挑战。我们提出同心因果注意力（CCA），一种简单而有效的位置对齐策略，通过自然地缩短视觉 token 与指令 token 之间的相对距离来缓解 RoPE 长程衰减的影响。有了 CCA，视觉 token 能更好地与指令 token 交互，从而增强模型的感知能力并缓解物体幻觉。无需任何额外技巧，我们的位置对齐方法在多个物体幻觉基准上大幅超越现有的幻觉缓解策略。

English abstract

Recent Large Vision Language Models (LVLMs) present remarkable zero-shot conversational and reasoning capabilities given multimodal queries. Nevertheless, they suffer from object hallucination, a phenomenon where LVLMs are prone to generate textual responses not factually aligned with image inputs. Our pilot study reveals that object hallucination is closely tied with Rotary Position Encoding (RoPE), a widely adopted positional dependency modeling design in existing LVLMs. Due to the long-term decay in RoPE, LVLMs tend to hallucinate more when relevant visual cues are distant from instruction tokens in the multimodal input sequence. Additionally, we observe a similar effect when reversing the sequential order of visual tokens during multimodal alignment. Our tests indicate that long-term decay in RoPE poses challenges to LVLMs while capturing visual-instruction interactions across long distances. We propose Concentric Causal Attention (CCA), a simple yet effective positional alignment strategy that mitigates the impact of RoPE long-term decay in LVLMs by naturally reducing relative distance between visual and instruction tokens. With CCA, visual tokens can better interact with instruction tokens, thereby enhancing model's perception capability and alleviating object hallucination. Without bells and whistles, our positional alignment method surpasses existing hallucination mitigation strategies by large margins on multiple object hallucination benchmarks.

完整作者
Yun Xing, Yiheng Li, Ivan Laptev, Shijian Lu

89 · 2024-12 · 原表 #59 注意力与表征干预

Nullu：用 HalluSpace 投影缓解物体幻觉

Nullu: Mitigating Object Hallucinations in Large Vision-Language Models via HalluSpace Projection

Le Yang, Ziwei Zheng, Boxu Chen et al.
CVPR 2025

用带视觉内容的真实/幻觉文本提示提取「HalluSpace」，正交化模型权重使输入特征投影到其零空间以减少物体幻觉。

展开中英文摘要与表格信息

中文摘要（原表译文）

近期研究表明，大型视觉语言模型（LVLMs）常受物体幻觉（OH）问题困扰。为缓解该问题，我们提出一种高效方法，基于一个不安全子空间来编辑模型权重，本文称之为 HalluSpace。以伴随视觉内容的真实文本提示与幻觉文本提示作为输入，可通过抽取幻觉嵌入特征并从 LVLMs 中剔除真实表征来识别 HalluSpace。通过对模型权重正交化，输入特征将被投影到 HalluSpace 的零空间以降低物体幻觉，我们据此将方法命名为 Nullu。我们揭示，HalluSpace 通常包含用于构建 LVLMs 的大型语言模型（LLMs）中的先验信息，而此前研究已表明这些先验是物体幻觉的重要成因。因此，零空间投影抑制 LLMs 的先验以滤除幻觉特征，从而得到语境上准确的输出。实验表明，我们的方法能在不同 LVLM 系列上有效缓解物体幻觉且不增加额外推理成本，在通用 LVLM 基准上也表现强劲。代码发布于 https://github.com/Ziwei-Zheng/Nullu 。

English abstract

Recent studies have shown that large vision-language models (LVLMs) often suffer from the issue of object hallucinations (OH). To mitigate this issue, we introduce an efficient method that edits the model weights based on an unsafe subspace, which we call HalluSpace in this paper. With truthful and hallucinated text prompts accompanying the visual content as inputs, the HalluSpace can be identified by extracting the hallucinated embedding features and removing the truthful representations in LVLMs. By orthogonalizing the model weights, input features will be projected into the Null space of the HalluSpace to reduce OH, based on which we name our method Nullu. We reveal that HalluSpaces generally contain prior information in the large language models (LLMs) applied to build LVLMs, which have been shown as essential causes of OH in previous studies. Therefore, null space projection suppresses the LLMs' priors to filter out the hallucinated features, resulting in contextually accurate outputs. Experiments show that our method can effectively mitigate OH across different LVLM families without extra inference costs and also show strong performance in general LVLM benchmarks. Code is released at https://github.com/Ziwei-Zheng/Nullu.

完整作者
Le Yang, Ziwei Zheng, Boxu Chen, Zhengyu Zhao, Chenhao Lin, Chao Shen

90 · 2025-05 · 原表 #60 注意力与表征干预

SPIN：图像引导的注意力头抑制

Mitigating Hallucinations in Vision-Language Models through Image-Guided Head Suppression

Sreetama Sarkar, Yue Che, Alex Gavin et al.
arXiv 预印本（元数据未标注录用）

把幻觉归因到每层中动态变化的一小部分注意力头，推理时定向抑制它们，几乎不增加计算与延迟开销。

展开中英文摘要与表格信息

中文摘要（原表译文）

尽管在多模态理解任务上取得显著进展，大型视觉语言模型（LVLMs）常受"幻觉"困扰，生成与视觉上下文失配的文本。现有通过推理时干预来减少幻觉的方法会导致延迟显著增加。为缓解这一点，我们提出 SPIN，一种与任务无关的注意力引导头抑制策略，可在推理过程中无缝集成，且不带来明显的计算或延迟开销。我们研究 LVLMs 中的幻觉是否可与特定模型组件相关联。我们的分析表明，幻觉可归因于每一层中一个动态变化的注意力头子集。借助这一洞见，对每个文本查询 token，我们选择性地抑制那些对图像 token 注意力低的注意力头，同时保持 top-K 注意力头不变。在视觉问答与图像描述任务上的广泛评测表明，SPIN 能将幻觉分数降低最多 2.7 倍，同时保持 F1，并相较现有替代方法将吞吐量提升 1.8 倍。代码见 https://github.com/YUECHE77/SPIN 。

English abstract

Despite their remarkable progress in multimodal understanding tasks, large vision language models (LVLMs) often suffer from "hallucinations", generating texts misaligned with the visual context. Existing methods aimed at reducing hallucinations through inference time intervention incur a significant increase in latency. To mitigate this, we present SPIN, a task-agnostic attention-guided head suppression strategy that can be seamlessly integrated during inference, without incurring any significant compute or latency overhead. We investigate whether hallucination in LVLMs can be linked to specific model components. Our analysis suggests that hallucinations can be attributed to a dynamic subset of attention heads in each layer. Leveraging this insight, for each text query token, we selectively suppress attention heads that exhibit low attention to image tokens, keeping the top-K attention heads intact. Extensive evaluations on visual question answering and image description tasks demonstrate the efficacy of SPIN in reducing hallucination scores up to 2.7x while maintaining F1, and improving throughput by 1.8x compared to existing alternatives. Code is available at https://github.com/YUECHE77/SPIN.

完整作者
Sreetama Sarkar, Yue Che, Alex Gavin, Peter A. Beerel, Souvik Kundu

91 · 2025-07 · 原表 #61 注意力与表征干预

ONLY：单层干预即可充分缓解幻觉

ONLY: One-Layer Intervention Sufficiently Mitigates Hallucinations in Large Vision-Language Models

Zifu Wan, Ce Zhang, Silong Yong et al.
ICCV 2025

无需两次以上前向查询，仅做单层干预即可达到对比解码的减幻觉效果，显著降低推理延迟以适配实时应用。

展开中英文摘要与表格信息

中文摘要（原表译文）

近期的大型视觉语言模型（LVLMs）开创了一种通过文本回答理解并推理图像输入的新范式。尽管它们在一系列多模态任务上取得了出色性能，却面临幻觉这一持续挑战，这带来实际缺陷，也引发人们对其在真实应用中可靠部署的担忧。现有工作探索了对比解码方法来缓解该问题，即将原始 LVLM 的输出与受扰版本的输出进行对比。然而这些方法需要两次或更多次查询，拖慢了 LVLM 的回答生成，使其不太适合实时应用。为克服这一局限，我们提出 ONLY，一种免训练的解码方法，只需单次查询并在解码过程中做单层干预，从而实现高效的实时部署。具体地，我们利用每个 token 的文本-视觉熵比为文本输出选择性地放大关键文本信息以加以增强。大量实验结果表明，所提出的 ONLY 在各种基准上持续优于最先进方法，同时所需实现代价与计算成本极小。代码见 https://github.com/zifuwan/ONLY 。

English abstract

Recent Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs) have introduced a new paradigm for understanding and reasoning about image input through textual responses. Although they have achieved remarkable performance across a range of multi-modal tasks, they face the persistent challenge of hallucination, which introduces practical weaknesses and raises concerns about their reliable deployment in real-world applications. Existing work has explored contrastive decoding approaches to mitigate this issue, where the output of the original LVLM is compared and contrasted with that of a perturbed version. However, these methods require two or more queries that slow down LVLM response generation, making them less suitable for real-time applications. To overcome this limitation, we propose ONLY, a training-free decoding approach that requires only a single query and a one-layer intervention during decoding, enabling efficient real-time deployment. Specifically, we enhance textual outputs by selectively amplifying crucial textual information using a text-to-visual entropy ratio for each token. Extensive experimental results demonstrate that our proposed ONLY consistently outperforms state-of-the-art methods across various benchmarks while requiring minimal implementation effort and computational cost. Code is available at https://github.com/zifuwan/ONLY.

完整作者
Zifu Wan, Ce Zhang, Silong Yong, Martin Q. Ma, Simon Stepputtis, Louis-Philippe Morency, Deva Ramanan, Katia Sycara, Yaqi Xie

92 · 2025-07 · 原表 #62 注意力与表征干预

从中间层提取视觉事实以缓解幻觉

Extracting Visual Facts from Intermediate Layers for Mitigating Hallucinations in Multimodal Large Language Models

Haoran Zhou, Zihan Zhang, Hao Chen
arXiv 预印本（元数据未标注录用）

观察到中间层蕴含视觉事实、深层被语言先验压制，据此提取中间层视觉事实做输出分布校正。

展开中英文摘要与表格信息

中文摘要（原表译文）

多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）通过结合视觉识别与语言理解来生成既连贯又贴合语境的内容，已取得显著进展。然而 MLLMs 仍受物体幻觉困扰，即模型产生看似合理却事实错误的输出，其中包含图像中并不存在的物体。近期工作揭示，MLLMs 中的先验知识在深层显著压制视觉信息，从而导致含幻觉的输出。然而，这些先验在 MLLMs 的中间层阶段如何压制视觉信息，仍不清楚。我们观察到，视觉事实知识与中间层先验/原始概率分布之间的差异在中间层中呈现相似的演化趋势。受此启发，我们提出抽取视觉事实的解码方法（EVA），一种简单且免训练的方法，它动态选择视觉事实信息最显著的中间层。通过对比由原始输入与纯文本输入在所选层导出的输出分布，EVA 抽取视觉事实知识并按比例将其并入最后一层以校正输出 logits。重要的是，EVA 与模型无关，可无缝结合各类经典解码策略，并适用于不同的 MLLMs。我们在广泛使用的基准上验证 EVA，结果表明相较基线方法它显著降低了幻觉率，凸显了其在缓解幻觉方面的有效性。

English abstract

Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have made significant strides by combining visual recognition and language understanding to generate content that is both coherent and contextually accurate. However, MLLMs continue to struggle with object hallucinations, where models produce seemingly plausible but factually incorrect outputs, including objects that do not exist in the image. Recent work has revealed that the prior knowledge in MLLMs significantly suppresses visual information in deep layers, causing hallucinatory outputs. However, how these priors suppress visual information at the intermediate layer stage in MLLMs remains unclear. We observe that visual factual knowledge and the differences between intermediate-layer prior/original probability distributions show similar evolutionary trends in intermediate layers. Motivated by this, we introduce Decoding by Extracting Visual Facts (EVA), a simple, training-free method that dynamically selects intermediate layers with the most significant visual factual information. By contrasting the output distributions of the selected layer derived from the original input and pure-text input, EVA extracts visual factual knowledge and proportionally incorporates it into the final layer to correct the output logits. Importantly, EVA is model-agnostic, seamlessly integrates with various classic decoding strategies, and is applicable across different MLLMs. We validate EVA on widely-used benchmarks, and the results show that it significantly reduces hallucination rates compared to baseline methods, underscoring its effectiveness in mitigating hallucinations.

完整作者
Haoran Zhou, Zihan Zhang, Hao Chen

93 · 2025-08 · 原表 #63 注意力与表征干预

LVLM 的模态偏置：用注意力透镜分析与缓解物体幻觉

Modality Bias in LVLMs: Analyzing and Mitigating Object Hallucination via Attention Lens

Haohan Zheng, Zhenguo Zhang
arXiv 预印本（元数据未标注录用）

揭示幻觉时模型可能既忽略视觉也忽略文本（模态偏置），并用注意力透镜定位问题以缓解物体幻觉。

展开中英文摘要与表格信息

中文摘要（原表译文）

大型视觉语言模型（LVLMs）展现出卓越的多模态理解与推理能力，但仍受严重的物体幻觉困扰。此前研究主要将该缺陷归因于 LVLMs 中视觉编码器与大型语言模型（LLMs）规模失配所造成的语言先验：由于当前 LVLMs 建立在 LLMs 之上，它们倾向于过度依赖文本提示与 LLMs 的内部知识，生成与视觉线索不一致的描述。然而，通过对幻觉机制的深入研究，我们从实证上揭示了一个此前被忽视的现象：LVLMs 在幻觉时不仅可能忽略视觉信息，也可能忽略文本模态——我们称之为模态偏置，它表明 LVLMs 难以同时关注视觉与文本两种模态，导致对用户指令的理解是碎片化的。基于这一观察，我们提出一种简单而有效的免训练方法来缓解物体幻觉。具体地，我们干预并调整文本 token 与视觉 token 的注意力权重，平衡跨模态兼容性以更好地贴合用户意图。此外，我们采用对比解码策略降低 LVLM 对其参数化知识的过度依赖，与注意力操纵协同增效。大量实验证实模态偏置在 LVLMs 中普遍存在。值得注意的是，我们的方法在多个开源 LVLMs 与基准上有效缓解了幻觉，凸显了其泛化性与有效性。

English abstract

Large vision-language models (LVLMs) have demonstrated remarkable multimodal comprehension and reasoning capabilities, but they still suffer from severe object hallucination. Previous studies primarily attribute the flaw to linguistic prior caused by the scale mismatch between visual encoders and large language models (LLMs) in LVLMs. Specifically, as current LVLMs are built upon LLMs, they tend to over-rely on textual prompts and internal knowledge of LLMs, generating descriptions inconsistent with visual cues. However, through an in-depth investigation of the hallucinated mechanisms, we empirically reveal a previously overlooked phenomenon: LVLMs may ignore not only visual information but also textual modality during hallucination, a behavior termed as modality bias, which indicates that LVLMs struggle to simultaneously attend to both visual and textual modalities, leading to fragmented understanding of user-provided instructions. Based on this observation, we propose a simple yet effective training-free method to mitigate object hallucination. Concretely, we intervene and adjust the attention weights of textual and visual tokens, balancing cross-modal compatibility for better alignment with user intentions. Furthermore, we adopt a contrastive decoding strategy to reduce the LVLM's overreliance on its parametric knowledge, synergistically enhancing our attention manipulation. Extensive experiments confirm the widespread presence of modality bias in LVLMs. Notably, our method effectively mitigates hallucination across multiple open-source LVLMs and benchmarks, highlighting its generalizability and efficacy.

完整作者
Haohan Zheng, Zhenguo Zhang

94 · 2023-12 · 原表 #64 视频理解增强

Vista-LLaMA：保持与视觉 token 等距以降低视频幻觉

Vista-LLaMA: Reducing Hallucination in Video Language Models via Equal Distance to Visual Tokens

Fan Ma, Xiaojie Jin, Heng Wang et al.
arXiv 预印本（元数据未标注录用）

发现文本越长视觉影响越弱是视频幻觉主因，去掉视觉-文本间相对位置编码以维持恒定的视觉影响力。

展开中英文摘要与表格信息

中文摘要（原表译文）

大型视频语言模型的近期进展在视频理解上展现出令人期待的结果。当前方法直接把视频转换为语言 token，并使用大型语言模型完成多模态任务。然而，随着文本变长、视频的影响减弱，该方法往往生成无关内容，即通常所说的"幻觉"。为解决该问题，我们提出 Vista-LLaMA，一个新框架：无论生成的文本长度如何，它都保持所有视觉 token 与任意语言 token 之间距离的一致。Vista-LLaMA 在确定视觉 token 与文本 token 之间的注意力权重时省略相对位置编码，同时保留文本与文本 token 的位置编码。这放大了视觉 token 对文本生成的影响，在视觉与文本 token 相对距离更长时尤为明显。所提出的注意力机制显著降低了生成与视频内容无关文本的可能性。此外，我们提出一种顺序视觉投影器，借助前一帧把当前视频帧投影为语言空间的 token。该方法不仅捕捉视频内的时序关系，还使更少的视觉 token 即可涵盖整个视频。我们的方法在四个具有挑战性的开放式视频问答基准上显著优于此前多种方法（如 Video-ChatGPT、MovieChat），在零样本 NExT-QA 上达到 60.7 的准确率，在零样本 MSRVTT-QA 上达到 60.5，创下新的最先进性能。项目见 https://jinxxian.github.io/Vista-LLaMA 。

English abstract

Recent advances in large video-language models have displayed promising outcomes in video comprehension. Current approaches straightforwardly convert video into language tokens and employ large language models for multi-modal tasks. However, this method often leads to the generation of irrelevant content, commonly known as "hallucination", as the length of the text increases and the impact of the video diminishes. To address this problem, we propose Vista-LLaMA, a novel framework that maintains the consistent distance between all visual tokens and any language tokens, irrespective of the generated text length. Vista-LLaMA omits relative position encoding when determining attention weights between visual and text tokens, retaining the position encoding for text and text tokens. This amplifies the effect of visual tokens on text generation, especially when the relative distance is longer between visual and text tokens. The proposed attention mechanism significantly reduces the chance of producing irrelevant text related to the video content. Furthermore, we present a sequential visual projector that projects the current video frame into tokens of language space with the assistance of the previous frame. This approach not only captures the temporal relationship within the video, but also allows less visual tokens to encompass the entire video. Our approach significantly outperforms various previous methods (e.g., Video-ChatGPT, MovieChat) on four challenging open-ended video question answering benchmarks. We reach an accuracy of 60.7 on the zero-shot NExT-QA and 60.5 on the zero-shot MSRVTT-QA, setting a new state-of-the-art performance. This project is available at https://jinxxian.github.io/Vista-LLaMA.

完整作者
Fan Ma, Xiaojie Jin, Heng Wang, Yuchen Xian, Jiashi Feng, Yi Yang

95 · 2024-01 · 原表 #65 视频理解增强

时序洞察增强：缓解 MLLM 的时序幻觉

Temporal Insight Enhancement: Mitigating Temporal Hallucination in Multimodal Large Language Models

Li Sun, Liuan Wang, Jun Sun et al.
arXiv 预印本（元数据未标注录用）

从「事件查询」与视频中抽取事件特定信息来细化回答，专门缓解视频中的事件级时序幻觉。

展开中英文摘要与表格信息

中文摘要（原表译文）

多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）的近期进展显著增强了对多媒体内容的了解，把文本、图像与视频等多样模态汇聚在一起。然而这些模型面临一个关键挑战，尤其是在处理视频输入时：幻觉的发生——即错误感知或解读，在事件层面尤为突出。本研究提出一种创新方法来应对 MLLMs 的事件级幻觉，聚焦于视频内容中的特定时序理解。我们的方法借助一个新框架，从事件查询与给定视频两方面抽取并利用事件特定信息来改进 MLLMs 的回答。我们提出一种独特机制，把按需提出的事件查询分解为标志性动作。随后我们采用 CLIP、BLIP2 等模型预测事件发生的具体时间戳。我们使用 Charades-STA 数据集进行的评测表明，时序幻觉显著减少，事件相关回答的质量有所提升。本研究不仅为解决 MLLMs 的一项关键局限提供了新视角，还贡献了一种可在时序相关问题语境下定量衡量 MLLMs 的方法。

English abstract

Recent advancements in Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have significantly enhanced the comprehension of multimedia content, bringing together diverse modalities such as text, images, and videos. However, a critical challenge faced by these models, especially when processing video inputs, is the occurrence of hallucinations - erroneous perceptions or interpretations, particularly at the event level. This study introduces an innovative method to address event-level hallucinations in MLLMs, focusing on specific temporal understanding in video content. Our approach leverages a novel framework that extracts and utilizes event-specific information from both the event query and the provided video to refine MLLMs' response. We propose a unique mechanism that decomposes on-demand event queries into iconic actions. Subsequently, we employ models like CLIP and BLIP2 to predict specific timestamps for event occurrences. Our evaluation, conducted using the Charades-STA dataset, demonstrates a significant reduction in temporal hallucinations and an improvement in the quality of event-related responses. This research not only provides a new perspective in addressing a critical limitation of MLLMs but also contributes a quantitatively measurable method for evaluating MLLMs in the context of temporal-related questions.

完整作者
Li Sun, Liuan Wang, Jun Sun, Takayuki Okatani

96 · 2024-06 · 原表 #66 视频理解增强

幻觉缓解提示推动长视频理解

Hallucination Mitigation Prompts Long-term Video Understanding

Yiwei Sun, Zhihang Liu, Chuanbin Liu et al.
arXiv 预印本（元数据未标注录用）

针对帧聚合引入的严重幻觉，用 CLIP 分数按问题选取关键帧、把问题注入图像 Q-Former 查询，从而提升长视频理解。

展开中英文摘要与表格信息

中文摘要（原表译文）

近来多模态大语言模型在视频理解任务上取得显著进展。然而它们理解未经处理的长视频的能力非常有限，主要原因在于难以支撑巨大的显存开销。尽管现有方法通过帧聚合在显存与信息之间取得平衡，却不可避免地引入了严重的幻觉问题。为解决该问题，本文基于现有 MLLMs 构建了一条完整的幻觉缓解流水线。具体地，我们用 CLIP 分数结合问题引导帧采样过程，选出与问题相关的关键帧；随后把问题信息注入图像 Q-former 的查询中以获得更重要的视觉特征；最后在答案生成阶段，我们利用链式思维与上下文学习技术显式控制答案的生成。值得一提的是，对于断点模式，我们发现图像理解模型的结果优于视频理解模型，因此我们使用比较机制对两类模型的答案进行聚合。最终，我们在 MovieChat 数据集上于全局模式与断点模式分别取得 84.2% 与 62.9% 的成绩，超过官方基线模型 29.1% 与 24.1%。此外，所提方法在 CVPR LOVEU 2024 长视频问答挑战赛中获得第三名。代码见 https://github.com/lntzm/CVPR24Track-LongVideo 。

English abstract

Recently, multimodal large language models have made significant advancements in video understanding tasks. However, their ability to understand unprocessed long videos is very limited, primarily due to the difficulty in supporting the enormous memory overhead. Although existing methods achieve a balance between memory and information by aggregating frames, they inevitably introduce the severe hallucination issue. To address this issue, this paper constructs a comprehensive hallucination mitigation pipeline based on existing MLLMs. Specifically, we use the CLIP Score to guide the frame sampling process with questions, selecting key frames relevant to the question. Then, We inject question information into the queries of the image Q-former to obtain more important visual features. Finally, during the answer generation stage, we utilize chain-of-thought and in-context learning techniques to explicitly control the generation of answers. It is worth mentioning that for the breakpoint mode, we found that image understanding models achieved better results than video understanding models. Therefore, we aggregated the answers from both types of models using a comparison mechanism. Ultimately, We achieved 84.2\% and 62.9\% for the global and breakpoint modes respectively on the MovieChat dataset, surpassing the official baseline model by 29.1\% and 24.1\%. Moreover the proposed method won the third place in the CVPR LOVEU 2024 Long-Term Video Question Answering Challenge. The code is avaiable at https://github.com/lntzm/CVPR24Track-LongVideo

完整作者
Yiwei Sun, Zhihang Liu, Chuanbin Liu, Bowei Pu, Zhihan Zhang, Hongtao Xie

97 · 2025-02 · 原表 #67 视频理解增强

幻觉纠正能改善视频-语言对齐吗？（HACA）

Can Hallucination Correction Improve Video-Language Alignment?

Lingjun Zhao, Mingyang Xie, Paola Cascante-Bonilla et al.
arXiv 预印本（元数据未标注录用）

把「纠正描述中与视频不符的幻觉」作为自训练目标（HACA），在视频描述绑定与文本到视频检索上稳定涨点。

展开中英文摘要与表格信息

中文摘要（原表译文）

大型视觉语言模型常生成并非基于其视觉输入的幻觉内容。此前工作侧重缓解幻觉，而我们另辟蹊径，探索把幻觉纠正作为一种训练目标来改善视频-语言对齐。我们提出 HACA，一个自训练框架，学习纠正描述中与视频内容不一致的幻觉。通过识别并纠正不一致之处，HACA 增强了模型对齐视频表征与文本表征的能力，以支撑时空推理。实验结果在视频描述绑定与文本到视频检索任务上均显示出一致的增益，证明受幻觉纠正启发的任务可作为改善视觉-语言对齐的有效策略。

English abstract

Large Vision-Language Models often generate hallucinated content that is not grounded in its visual inputs. While prior work focuses on mitigating hallucinations, we instead explore leveraging hallucination correction as a training objective to improve video-language alignment. We introduce HACA, a self-training framework learning to correct hallucinations in descriptions that do not align with the video content. By identifying and correcting inconsistencies, HACA enhances the model's ability to align video and textual representations for spatio-temporal reasoning. Our experimental results show consistent gains in video-caption binding and text-to-video retrieval tasks, demonstrating that hallucination correction-inspired tasks serve as an effective strategy for improving vision and language alignment.

98 · 2025-03 · 原表 #68 视频理解增强

MASH-VLM：解耦时空表征缓解动作-场景幻觉

MASH-VLM: Mitigating Action-Scene Hallucination in Video-LLMs through Disentangled Spatial-Temporal Representations

Kyungho Bae, Jinhyung Kim, Sihaeng Lee et al.
CVPR 2025

指出时空特征混叠与标准 RoPE 导致「由场景猜动作」，用解耦的时空表征缓解动作-场景幻觉。

展开中英文摘要与表格信息

中文摘要（原表译文）

本工作针对视频大语言模型（Video-LLMs）中的动作-场景幻觉，即模型依据场景上下文错误预测动作，或依据观察到的动作错误预测场景。我们观察到，现有 Video-LLMs 常因两个主要因素而受动作-场景幻觉困扰。第一，现有 Video-LLMs 通过对所有 token 施加注意力操作而将空间特征与时序特征混在一起。第二，它们使用标准的旋转位置编码（RoPE），这使文本 token 依据序列顺序而过度偏重某类 token。为解决这些问题，我们提出 MASH-VLM，通过解耦的时空表征来缓解 Video-LLMs 的动作-场景幻觉。我们的方法包含两项关键创新：(1) DST-attention，一种新的注意力机制，通过在 LLM 内使用掩码注意力限制空间 token 与时序 token 之间的直接交互，从而实现二者的解耦；(2) Harmonic-RoPE，它扩展位置 ID 的维度，使空间 token 与时序 token 相对文本 token 保持均衡的位置。为评测 Video-LLMs 的动作-场景幻觉，我们提出 UNSCENE 基准，包含 1320 段视频与 4078 个问答对。大量实验表明，MASH-VLM 在 UNSCENE 基准以及现有视频理解基准上均取得最先进结果。

English abstract

In this work, we tackle action-scene hallucination in Video Large Language Models (Video-LLMs), where models incorrectly predict actions based on the scene context or scenes based on observed actions. We observe that existing Video-LLMs often suffer from action-scene hallucination due to two main factors. First, existing Video-LLMs intermingle spatial and temporal features by applying an attention operation across all tokens. Second, they use the standard Rotary Position Embedding (RoPE), which causes the text tokens to overemphasize certain types of tokens depending on their sequential orders. To address these issues, we introduce MASH-VLM, Mitigating Action-Scene Hallucination in Video-LLMs through disentangled spatial-temporal representations. Our approach includes two key innovations: (1) DST-attention, a novel attention mechanism that disentangles the spatial and temporal tokens within the LLM by using masked attention to restrict direct interactions between the spatial and temporal tokens; (2) Harmonic-RoPE, which extends the dimensionality of the positional IDs, allowing the spatial and temporal tokens to maintain balanced positions relative to the text tokens. To evaluate the action-scene hallucination in Video-LLMs, we introduce the UNSCENE benchmark with 1,320 videos and 4,078 QA pairs. Extensive experiments demonstrate that MASH-VLM achieves state-of-the-art results on the UNSCENE benchmark, as well as on existing video understanding benchmarks.

完整作者
Kyungho Bae, Jinhyung Kim, Sihaeng Lee, Soonyoung Lee, Gunhee Lee, Jinwoo Choi

99 · 2025-05 · 原表 #69 视频理解增强

VideoHallu：合成视频理解中的多模态幻觉评测与缓解

VideoHallu: Evaluating and Mitigating Multi-modal Hallucinations on Synthetic Video Understanding

Zongxia Li, Xiyang Wu, Guangyao Shi et al.
NeurIPS 2025

用物理或逻辑上不可能的合成视频做负对照，检验 VLM 究竟是真正理解视频还是只学到浅层视文相关性。

展开中英文摘要与表格信息

中文摘要（原表译文）

视觉语言模型（VLMs）在视频理解上取得了强劲结果，但一个关键问题仍然存在：它们究竟真正理解了视觉内容，还是只学到了视觉与语言之间的浅层相关？真正的视觉理解——尤其是对物理与常识的理解——对于与物理世界交互的 AI 系统至关重要。当前评测大多使用与训练数据相似的现实世界视频，因此高基准分数可能并不反映真实的推理能力。为此，我们提出负对照测试，使用描绘物理上不可能或逻辑上不一致事件的视频。我们提出 VideoHallu，一个用 Veo2、Sora 与 Kling 生成的、违背物理与常识场景的合成数据集，包含专家标注的问答对，覆盖四类违背情形。对领先 VLMs（Qwen-2.5-VL、Video-R1、VideoChat-R1）的测试表明，尽管它们在 MVBench、MMVU 等基准上成绩优异，却常常漏掉这些违背情形，暴露出视觉推理上的缺口。在 VideoHallu 上做强化学习微调可提升对此类违背的识别能力，而不会降低标准基准性能。数据见 https://github.com/zli12321/VideoHallu.git 。

English abstract

Vision-Language Models (VLMs) have achieved strong results in video understanding, yet a key question remains: do they truly comprehend visual content or only learn shallow correlations between vision and language? Real visual understanding, especially of physics and common sense, is essential for AI systems that interact with the physical world. Current evaluations mostly use real-world videos similar to training data, so high benchmark scores may not reflect real reasoning ability. To address this, we propose negative-control tests using videos that depict physically impossible or logically inconsistent events. We introduce VideoHallu, a synthetic dataset of physics- and commonsense-violating scenes generated with Veo2, Sora, and Kling. It includes expert-annotated question-answer pairs across four categories of violations. Tests of leading VLMs (Qwen-2.5-VL, Video-R1, VideoChat-R1) show that, despite strong results on benchmarks such as MVBench and MMVU, they often miss these violations, exposing gaps in visual reasoning. Reinforcement learning fine-tuning on VideoHallu improves recognition of such violations without reducing standard benchmark performance. Our data is available at https://github.com/zli12321/VideoHallu.git.

完整作者
Zongxia Li, Xiyang Wu, Guangyao Shi, Yubin Qin, Hongyang Du, Fuxiao Liu, Tianyi Zhou, Dinesh Manocha, Jordan Lee Boyd-Graber

100 · 2025-05 · 原表 #70 视频理解增强

时序感知激活工程缓解 VideoLLM 幻觉

Mitigating Hallucination in VideoLLMs via Temporal-Aware Activation Engineering

Jianfeng Cai, Wengang Zhou, Zongmeng Zhang et al.
arXiv 预印本（元数据未标注录用）

首次系统研究激活工程在 VideoLLM 上的有效性与机理，分析关键影响因素并提出时序感知的激活干预。

展开中英文摘要与表格信息

中文摘要（原表译文）

多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）在视频理解上取得显著进展。然而幻觉——模型生成看似合理却错误的输出——在视频领域仍是一个重大且未得到充分应对的挑战。在现有解决方案中，激活工程已被证明能有效缓解 LLMs 与 ImageLLMs 的幻觉，但其在 VideoLLMs 上的适用性仍基本未被探索。本工作首次系统研究激活工程用于缓解 VideoLLMs 幻觉的有效性与内在机制。我们首先考察影响激活工程性能的关键因素，发现模型对幻觉的敏感性取决于时序变化而非任务类型。此外，为激活工程选择合适的内部模块与数据集对于减少幻觉至关重要。在这些发现的指导下，我们提出一个面向 VideoLLMs 的时序感知激活工程框架，它依据时序变化特征自适应地识别并操纵幻觉敏感模块，从而在无需额外 LLM 微调的情况下大幅缓解幻觉。跨多个模型与基准的实验表明，我们的方法显著减少了 VideoLLMs 的幻觉，从而验证了研究发现的稳健性。

English abstract

Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have achieved remarkable progress in video understanding.However, hallucination, where the model generates plausible yet incorrect outputs, persists as a significant and under-addressed challenge in the video domain. Among existing solutions, activation engineering has proven successful in mitigating hallucinations in LLMs and ImageLLMs, yet its applicability to VideoLLMs remains largely unexplored. In this work, we are the first to systematically investigate the effectiveness and underlying mechanisms of activation engineering for mitigating hallucinations in VideoLLMs. We initially conduct an investigation of the key factors affecting the performance of activation engineering and find that a model's sensitivity to hallucination depends on $\textbf{temporal variation}$ rather than task type. Moreover, selecting appropriate internal modules and dataset for activation engineering is critical for reducing hallucination. Guided by these findings, we propose a temporal-aware activation engineering framework for VideoLLMs, which adaptively identifies and manipulates hallucination-sensitive modules based on the temporal variation characteristic, substantially mitigating hallucinations without additional LLM fine-tuning. Experiments across multiple models and benchmarks demonstrate that our method markedly reduces hallucination in VideoLLMs, thereby validating the robustness of our findings.

完整作者
Jianfeng Cai, Wengang Zhou, Zongmeng Zhang, Jiale Hong, Nianji Zhan, Houqiang Li

101 · 2025-11 · 原表 #71 视频理解增强

解耦感知与推理的抗幻觉视频理解（DPL）

Decoupling Perception from Reasoning for Hallucination-Resistant Video Understanding

Bowei Pu, Chuanbin Liu, Yifan Ge et al.
arXiv 预印本（元数据未标注录用）

把感知证据（含时间戳与视觉描述的结构化单元）与推理文本显式分离以便独立监督验证，实现抗幻觉的视频理解。

展开中英文摘要与表格信息

中文摘要（原表译文）

视频大语言模型通过生成中间推理文本提升了对复杂视频的推理能力。然而可靠的推理取决于准确的视频感知。在现有方法中，感知证据与推理文本交织在一起，使得难以直接监督感知过程。我们认为，可靠的监督需要把感知证据与推理显式分离，以便对感知进行独立验证。为直接监督感知，我们提出解耦感知与逻辑（DPL），它将感知表示为包含时间戳与视觉描述的固定格式证据单元。这种结构化表示便于直接抽取感知内容，并简化视频片段与奖励评估之间的对齐。基于 DPL，我们引入感知奖励，同时鼓励抗幻觉与基于感知的推理。一个事实感知评估器（FAE）提供抗幻觉分数，其幻觉评测性能可与 GPT-4o 相媲美。此外，我们通过将感知结果与问题输入参考模型来验证推理一致性。实验表明，通过提供可靠的过程奖励，Video-DPL 在 3B 与 7B 规模上均持续提升后训练性能，同时具有更高的数据效率。

English abstract

Video Large Language Models improve reasoning over complex videos by generating intermediate reasoning text. However, reliable reasoning depends on accurate video perception. In existing approaches, perception evidence is intertwined with reasoning text, making it difficult to directly supervise the perception process. We argue that reliable supervision requires explicitly separating perception evidence from reasoning so that perception can be verified independently. To supervise perception directly, we propose Decoupled Perception and Logic (DPL), which represents perception as fixed-format evidence units containing timestamps and visual descriptions. This structured representation enables direct extraction of perception content and simplifies alignment between video segments and reward evaluation. Building on DPL, we introduce a perception reward that encourages both hallucination resistance and perception-based reasoning. An Factual-Aware Evaluator (FAE) provides anti-hallucination scores and achieves hallucination evaluation performance comparable to GPT-4o. In addition, we validate reasoning consistency by feeding perception results and questions into a reference model. Experiments show that, by providing reliable process rewards, Video-DPL consistently improves post-training performance at both 3B and 7B scales, while delivering higher data efficiency.

完整作者
Bowei Pu, Chuanbin Liu, Yifan Ge, Peicheng Zhou, Yiwei Sun, Zhiying Lu, Zhangchi Hu, Hongtao Xie

102 · 2025-12 · 原表 #72 视频理解增强

SmartSight：以时序注意力塌缩在不牺牲视频理解的前提下减幻觉

SmartSight: Mitigating Hallucination in Video-LLMs Without Compromising Video Understanding via Temporal Attention Collapse

Yiming Sun, Mi Zhang, Feifei Li et al.
AAAI 2026

生成多个候选响应，用「时序注意力塌缩」分数挑出被贪心解码遮蔽的低幻觉输出，兼顾减幻觉与不损失理解推理能力。

展开中英文摘要与表格信息

中文摘要（原表译文）

尽管视频大语言模型近年快速进步，感知幻觉构成重大安全风险，严重限制了其真实场景的适用性。虽然已提出若干幻觉缓解方法，但它们往往牺牲模型的视频理解与推理能力。本工作提出 SmartSight，以免训练方式利用模型自身的自省能力，朝解决该问题迈出开创性一步。具体地，SmartSight 生成多个候选回答，以发掘那些常被标准贪心解码所遮蔽的低幻觉输出。它使用时序注意力塌缩分数评估每个回答的幻觉程度——该分数衡量模型在生成回答时是否过度聚焦于输入视频中琐碎的时序区域。为提升效率，SmartSight 定位视觉注意力消失点，从而实现更准确的幻觉估计并提前终止含幻觉的回答，大幅降低解码成本。实验表明，SmartSight 在 VRIPT-HAL 上使 Qwen2.5-VL-7B 的幻觉大幅降低 10.59%，同时增强视频理解与推理，在 VideoMMMU 上最多提升 8.86%。这些结果凸显了 SmartSight 在提升开源 Video-LLMs 可靠性方面的有效性。

English abstract

Despite Video Large Language Models having rapidly advanced in recent years, perceptual hallucinations pose a substantial safety risk, which severely restricts their real-world applicability. While several methods for hallucination mitigation have been proposed, they often compromise the model's capacity for video understanding and reasoning. In this work, we propose SmartSight, a pioneering step to address this issue in a training-free manner by leveraging the model's own introspective capabilities. Specifically, SmartSight generates multiple candidate responses to uncover low-hallucinated outputs that are often obscured by standard greedy decoding. It assesses the hallucination of each response using the Temporal Attention Collapse score, which measures whether the model over-focuses on trivial temporal regions of the input video when generating the response. To improve efficiency, SmartSight identifies the Visual Attention Vanishing point, enabling more accurate hallucination estimation and early termination of hallucinated responses, leading to a substantial reduction in decoding cost. Experiments show that SmartSight substantially lowers hallucinations for Qwen2.5-VL-7B by 10.59% on VRIPT-HAL, while simultaneously enhancing video understanding and reasoning, boosting performance on VideoMMMU by up to 8.86%. These results highlight SmartSight's effectiveness in improving the reliability of open-source Video-LLMs.

完整作者
Yiming Sun, Mi Zhang, Feifei Li, Geng Hong, Min Yang

103 · 2025-12 · 原表 #73 视频理解增强

驯化幻觉：用反事实视频生成提升 MLLM 视频理解（DualityForge）

Taming Hallucinations: Boosting MLLMs' Video Understanding via Counterfactual Video Generation

Zhe Huang, Hao Wen, Aiming Hao et al.
arXiv 预印本（元数据未标注录用）

用可控扩散编辑把真实视频改写为反事实场景以低成本合成训练数据，削弱语言先验依赖从而提升视频理解。

展开中英文摘要与表格信息

中文摘要（原表译文）

多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）在视频理解上取得显著进展。然而它们存在一个关键脆弱性：过度依赖语言先验，这会导致视觉未接地的幻觉，在处理违背常识的反事实视频时尤为突出。这一局限源于文本与视频之间固有的数据不平衡，而由于收集并标注反事实数据成本高昂，该问题难以解决。为此，我们提出 DualityForge，一个新的反事实数据合成框架，它采用可控的、基于扩散的视频编辑，把真实世界视频转换为反事实场景。通过把结构化的上下文信息嵌入视频编辑与问答生成过程，该框架可自动产出高质量问答对以及原始-编辑视频对，用于对比训练。在此基础上，我们构建了 DualityVidQA，一个旨在大规模减少 MLLM 幻觉的视频数据集。此外，为充分利用成对数据的对比特性，我们提出双性归一化优势训练（DNA-Train），一种两阶段的 SFT-RL 训练范式：其 RL 阶段施加成对的 ℓ1 优势归一化，从而实现更稳定高效的策略优化。在 DualityVidQA-Test 上的实验表明，我们的方法大幅减少了模型在反事实视频上的幻觉，相较 Qwen2.5-VL-7B 基线取得 24.0% 的相对提升。此外，我们的方法在幻觉基准与通用基准上均有显著增益，表明其较强的泛化能力。我们将开源数据集与代码。

English abstract

Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have made remarkable progress in video understanding. However, they suffer from a critical vulnerability: an over-reliance on language priors, which can lead to visual ungrounded hallucinations, especially when processing counterfactual videos that defy common sense. This limitation, stemming from the intrinsic data imbalance between text and video, is challenging to address due to the substantial cost of collecting and annotating counterfactual data. To address this, we introduce DualityForge, a novel counterfactual data synthesis framework that employs controllable, diffusion-based video editing to transform real-world videos into counterfactual scenarios. By embedding structured contextual information into the video editing and QA generation processes, the framework automatically produces high-quality QA pairs together with original-edited video pairs for contrastive training. Based on this, we build DualityVidQA, a large-scale video dataset designed to reduce MLLM hallucinations. In addition, to fully exploit the contrastive nature of our paired data, we propose Duality-Normalized Advantage Training (DNA-Train), a two-stage SFT-RL training regime where the RL phase applies pair-wise $\ell_1$ advantage normalization, thereby enabling a more stable and efficient policy optimization. Experiments on DualityVidQA-Test demonstrate that our method substantially reduces model hallucinations on counterfactual videos, yielding a relative improvement of 24.0% over the Qwen2.5-VL-7B baseline. Moreover, our approach achieves significant gains across both hallucination and general-purpose benchmarks, indicating strong generalization capability. We will open-source our dataset and code.

完整作者
Zhe Huang, Hao Wen, Aiming Hao, Bingze Song, Meiqi Wu, Jiahong Wu, Xiangxiang Chu, Sheng Lu, Haoqian Wang

104 · 2024-10 · 原表 #74 多模态与垂域扩展

多模态的诅咒：跨语言、视觉与音频的幻觉评测

The Curse of Multi-Modalities: Evaluating Hallucinations of Large Multimodal Models across Language, Visual, and Audio

Sicong Leng, Yun Xing, Zesen Cheng et al.
arXiv 预印本（元数据未标注录用）

首个系统研究语言+视觉+音频三模态幻觉的工作，指出单模态先验与虚假跨模态相关是两大主因并给出基准。

展开中英文摘要与表格信息

中文摘要（原表译文）

大型多模态模型（LMMs）的近期进展显著提升了其在多样任务上的表现，并且还在持续整合视频、音频等更多模态。然而多数现有 LMMs 仍易产生幻觉，即事实性多模态输入与所生成文本输出之间的偏差，这限制了它们在各真实场景中的适用性。本文首次系统研究了涉及三种最常见模态——语言、视觉与音频——的 LMMs 幻觉。我们的研究揭示了幻觉的两个关键成因：对单模态先验的过度依赖，以及虚假的模态间相关。为应对这些挑战，我们提出基准"多模态的诅咒"（CMM），它全面评测 LMMs 的幻觉，并对其深层问题做了细致分析。我们的发现凸显了若干关键脆弱点，包括模态整合的不平衡与训练数据带来的偏差，强调需要有平衡的跨模态学习与更强的幻觉缓解策略。基于我们的观察与发现，我们提出了可能提升 LMMs 可靠性的研究方向。

English abstract

Recent advancements in large multimodal models (LMMs) have significantly enhanced performance across diverse tasks, with ongoing efforts to further integrate additional modalities such as video and audio. However, most existing LMMs remain vulnerable to hallucinations, the discrepancy between the factual multimodal input and the generated textual output, which has limited their applicability in various real-world scenarios. This paper presents the first systematic investigation of hallucinations in LMMs involving the three most common modalities: language, visual, and audio. Our study reveals two key contributors to hallucinations: overreliance on unimodal priors and spurious inter-modality correlations. To address these challenges, we introduce the benchmark The Curse of Multi-Modalities (CMM), which comprehensively evaluates hallucinations in LMMs, providing a detailed analysis of their underlying issues. Our findings highlight key vulnerabilities, including imbalances in modality integration and biases from training data, underscoring the need for balanced cross-modal learning and enhanced hallucination mitigation strategies. Based on our observations and findings, we suggest potential research directions that could enhance the reliability of LMMs.

完整作者
Sicong Leng, Yun Xing, Zesen Cheng, Yang Zhou, Hang Zhang, Xin Li, Deli Zhao, Shijian Lu, Chunyan Miao, Lidong Bing
