105 · 2025-05 · 原表 #75 多模态与垂域扩展

AVCD：面向音视频大模型的对比解码

AVCD: Mitigating Hallucinations in Audio-Visual Large Language Models through Contrastive Decoding

Chaeyoung Jung, Youngjoon Jang, Joon Son Chung
arXiv 预印本（元数据未标注录用）

指出现有视觉对比解码不适配音视频模型，提出建模三模态交互的免训练音频-视觉对比解码。

展开中英文摘要与表格信息

中文摘要（原表译文）

幻觉仍多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）的重大挑战。为此，已有多种对比解码（CD）方法被提出，将原始 logits 与由受扰输入生成的幻觉 logits 做对比。尽管 CD 在视觉语言模型（VLMs）中展现出前景，它并不太适合音视频大语言模型（AV-LLMs）——在这类模型中，幻觉常常源自涉及音频、视频与语言的单模态与跨模态组合。这些错综复杂的交互要求更具自适应性、更模态感知的解码策略。本文提出音视频对比解码（AVCD），一种新颖的免训练解码框架，旨在建模三模态交互并抑制 AV-LLMs 中由模态引发的幻觉。与 VLMs 中以往破坏固定模态的 CD 方法不同，AVCD 利用注意力分布动态识别主导性较弱的模态，并施加注意力掩码以生成受扰输出 logits。为在三模态设置下支撑 CD，我们还重构了原始 CD 框架，以联合处理音频、视觉与文本输入。最后，为提升效率，我们引入熵引导的自适应解码，依据模型对预测的信心选择性地跳过不必要的解码步。大量实验表明，AVCD 持续优于现有解码方法。特别地，在 AVHBench 数据集上，它使 VideoLLaMA2 的准确率提升 2%、video-SALMONN 提升 7%，展现出较强的鲁棒性与泛化性。代码见 https://github.com/kaistmm/AVCD 。

English abstract

Hallucination remains a major challenge in multimodal large language models (MLLMs). To address this, various contrastive decoding (CD) methods have been proposed that contrasts original logits with hallucinated logits generated from perturbed inputs. While CD has shown promise in vision-language models (VLMs), it is not well-suited for AV-LLMs, where hallucinations often emerge from both unimodal and cross-modal combinations involving audio, video, and language. These intricate interactions call for a more adaptive and modality-aware decoding strategy. In this paper, we propose Audio-Visual Contrastive Decoding (AVCD)-a novel, training-free decoding framework designed to model trimodal interactions and suppress modality-induced hallucinations in AV-LLMs. Unlike previous CD methods in VLMs that corrupt a fixed modality, AVCD leverages attention distributions to dynamically identify less dominant modalities and applies attentive masking to generate perturbed output logits. To support CD in a trimodal setting, we also reformulate the original CD framework to jointly handle audio, visual, and textual inputs. Finally, to improve efficiency, we introduce entropy-guided adaptive decoding, which selectively skips unnecessary decoding steps based on the model's confidence in its predictions. Extensive experiments demonstrate that AVCD consistently outperforms existing decoding methods. Especially, on the AVHBench dataset, it improves accuracy by 2% for VideoLLaMA2 and 7% for video-SALMONN, demonstrating strong robustness and generalizability. Our code is available at https://github.com/kaistmm/AVCD.

完整作者
Chaeyoung Jung, Youngjoon Jang, Joon Son Chung

106 · 2025-06 · 原表 #76 多模态与垂域扩展

眼见为实？缓解多模态大模型的 OCR 幻觉（KIE-HVQA）

Seeing is Believing? Mitigating OCR Hallucinations in Multimodal Large Language Models

Zhentao He, Can Zhang, Ziheng Wu et al.
NeurIPS 2025

提出首个 OCR 幻觉基准 KIE-HVQA，指出视觉退化时模型无法感知不确定性、过度依赖语言先验而幻觉。

展开中英文摘要与表格信息

中文摘要（原表译文）

多模态大语言模型的近期进展通过整合文本与视觉信息增强了文档理解。然而现有模型在真实场景下、尤其是在视觉退化条件下，其范式存在不完备之处。在此类情形中，当前的回答范式往往无法充分感知视觉退化与歧义，导致过度依赖语言先验或视觉-文本推理失配。这种识别不确定性的困难常常导致生成幻觉内容，在无法给出精确答案时尤为如此。为更好地展示并分析这一现象与问题，我们提出 KIE-HVQA，首个专门评测退化文档理解中 OCR 幻觉的基准。该数据集包含覆盖身份证与发票的测试样本，并模拟了影响 OCR 可靠性的真实退化。这一设置可评测模型在退化输入下区分可靠视觉信息并据此作答的能力，从而凸显在不确定数据上避免幻觉的挑战。为实现视觉忠实的推理从而避免上述问题，我们进一步提出一个基于 GRPO 的框架，并配以新的奖励机制。通过在监督微调与强化学习框架中引入对视觉不确定性的自我意识，以及一种通过主动拒答来提升任务难度的分析方法，我们成功缓解了歧义区域上的幻觉。在 Qwen2.5-VL 上的实验表明，我们的 7B 参数模型在 KIE-HVQA 上相较 GPT-4o 取得 22% 的无幻觉准确率绝对提升，且标准任务性能没有明显下降，凸显了有效性与鲁棒性。

English abstract

Recent advancements in multimodal large language models have enhanced document understanding by integrating textual and visual information. However, existing models exhibit incompleteness within their paradigm in real-world scenarios, particularly under visual degradation. In such conditions, the current response paradigm often fails to adequately perceive visual degradation and ambiguity, leading to overreliance on linguistic priors or misaligned visual-textual reasoning. This difficulty in recognizing uncertainty frequently results in the generation of hallucinatory content, especially when a precise answer is not feasible. To better demonstrate and analyze this phenomenon and problem, we propose KIE-HVQA, the first benchmark dedicated to evaluating OCR hallucination in degraded document understanding. This dataset includes test samples spanning identity cards and invoices, with simulated real-world degradations for OCR reliability. This setup allows for evaluating models' capacity, under degraded input, to distinguish reliable visual information and answer accordingly, thereby highlighting the challenge of avoiding hallucination on uncertain data. To achieve vision-faithful reasoning and thereby avoid the aforementioned issues, we further introduce a GRPO-based framework featuring a novel reward mechanism. By incorporating a self-awareness of visual uncertainty and an analysis method that initiates refusal to answer to increase task difficulty within our supervised fine-tuning and reinforcement learning framework, we successfully mitigated hallucinations in ambiguous regions. Experiments on Qwen2.5-VL demonstrate that our 7B-parameter model achieves a 22\% absolute improvement in hallucination-free accuracy over GPT-4o on KIE-HVQA and there is no significant performance drop in standard tasks, highlighting both effectiveness and robustness.

完整作者
Zhentao He, Can Zhang, Ziheng Wu, Zhenghao Chen, Yufei Zhan, Yifan Li, Zhao Zhang, Xian Wang, Minghui Qiu

107 · 2025-08 · 原表 #77 多模态与垂域扩展

OmniDPO：面向全模态幻觉的偏好优化框架

OmniDPO: A Preference Optimization Framework to Address Omni-Modal Hallucination

Junzhe Chen, Tianshu Zhang, Shiyu Huang et al.
arXiv 预印本（元数据未标注录用）

针对全模态模型中文本先验主导、且训练时忽略视频与对应音频内在关联的问题，设计偏好优化框架降幻觉。

展开中英文摘要与表格信息

中文摘要（原表译文）

近来全模态大语言模型（OLLMs）掀起新一轮研究热潮，在音视频理解与实时环境感知等任务上取得令人印象深刻的结果。然而幻觉问题依然存在。与双模态设置类似，来自文本模态的先验往往占据主导，使 OLLMs 更依赖文本线索而忽视视觉与音频信息。此外，完全多模态场景带来新的挑战：多数现有模型在训练时分别把视觉或听觉模态与文本对齐，却忽视了视频与其对应音频之间的内在关联。这一疏忽导致在推理需要解读视频内容中隐含的音频线索时产生幻觉。为应对这些挑战，我们提出 OmniDPO，一个旨在缓解 OLLMs 幻觉的偏好对齐框架。具体地，OmniDPO 包含两种策略：(1) 构造文本偏好样本对，以增强模型对音视频交互的理解；(2) 构造多模态偏好样本对，以强化模型对视觉与音频信息的关注。通过同时应对两项挑战，OmniDPO 有效改善了多模态接地并减少幻觉。在两个 OLLMs 上的实验表明，OmniDPO 不仅有效缓解了多模态幻觉，还显著增强了模型的跨模态推理能力。全部代码与数据集将在论文录用后发布。

English abstract

Recently, Omni-modal large language models (OLLMs) have sparked a new wave of research, achieving impressive results in tasks such as audio-video understanding and real-time environment perception. However, hallucination issues still persist. Similar to the bimodal setting, the priors from the text modality tend to dominate, leading OLLMs to rely more heavily on textual cues while neglecting visual and audio information. In addition, fully multimodal scenarios introduce new challenges. Most existing models align visual or auditory modalities with text independently during training, while ignoring the intrinsic correlations between video and its corresponding audio. This oversight results in hallucinations when reasoning requires interpreting hidden audio cues embedded in video content. To address these challenges, we propose OmniDPO, a preference-alignment framework designed to mitigate hallucinations in OLLMs. Specifically, OmniDPO incorporates two strategies: (1) constructing text-preference sample pairs to enhance the model's understanding of audio-video interactions; and (2) constructing multimodal-preference sample pairs to strengthen the model's attention to visual and auditory information. By tackling both challenges, OmniDPO effectively improves multimodal grounding and reduces hallucination. Experiments conducted on two OLLMs demonstrate that OmniDPO not only effectively mitigates multimodal hallucinations but also significantly enhances the models' reasoning capabilities across modalities. All code and datasets will be released upon paper acceptance.

完整作者
Junzhe Chen, Tianshu Zhang, Shiyu Huang, Yuwei Niu, Chao Sun, Rongzhou Zhang, Guanyu Zhou, Lijie Wen, Xuming Hu

108 · 2026-04 · 原表 #78 多模态与垂域扩展

面向具身视觉语言模型的渐进式训练以缓解时空幻觉

A Progressive Training Strategy for Embodied Vision-Language Models to Mitigate Spatio-Temporal Hallucinations

Xiaoda Yang, Shuai Yang, Can Wang et al.
arXiv 预印本（元数据未标注录用）

指出前向/反向时序查询间的巨大落差的「多图推理幻觉」，用时空链式思维数据加渐进式训练缓解。

展开中英文摘要与表格信息

中文摘要（原表译文）

视觉语言模型（VLMs）在静态图像理解上取得显著进步，但在时空推理上仍面临关键障碍。一个主要瓶颈是"多图推理幻觉"：前向与反向时序查询之间存在巨大性能落差，暴露出模型依赖表面捷径而非基于状态的理解。为缓解这一问题，我们首先构建了一个新的链式思维（CoT）数据集，将复杂推理分解为详细的时空步骤与明确判断。在此基础上，我们提出一个渐进式训练框架：先在我们的 CoT 数据集上做监督预训练以灌输逻辑结构，随后用可扩展的弱标注数据微调以获得更广泛的泛化能力。实验表明，该方法不仅提升了主干准确率，还将前向-反向性能差距从超过 70% 缩小到仅 6.53%。这表明该方法增强了动态推理能力，并减少了当前 VLMs 固有的时序偏差。

English abstract

Vision-Language Models (VLMs) have made significant strides in static image understanding but continue to face critical hurdles in spatiotemporal reasoning. A major bottleneck is "multi-image reasoning hallucination", where a large performance drop between forward and reverse temporal queries reveals a dependence on superficial shortcuts instead of state-based understanding. To mitigate this, we first develop a new Chain-of-Thought (CoT) dataset that decomposes intricate reasoning into detailed spatiotemporal steps and definitive judgments. Building on this, we present a progressive training framework: it initiates with supervised pre-training on our CoT dataset to instill logical structures, followed by fine-tuning with scalable weakly-labeled data for broader generalization. Our experiments demonstrate that this approach not only improves backbone accuracy but also reduces the forward-backward performance gap from over 70% to only 6.53%. This shows that the method strengthens dynamic reasoning and reduces the inherent temporal biases of current VLMs.

完整作者
Xiaoda Yang, Shuai Yang, Can Wang, Jingyang Xue, Menglan Tang, Checheng Yu, Xunzhe Zhou, Sashuai Zhou, Tao Jin, Lixin Yang, Xiangyu Yue, Zhou Zhao

109 · 2026-05 · 原表 #79 多模态与垂域扩展

HalluCXR：胸片解读中医学 VLM 的幻觉评测与缓解

HalluCXR: Benchmarking and Mitigating Hallucinations in Medical Vision-Language Models for Chest Radiograph Interpretation

Haoyu Wang, Zitong Li
arXiv 预印本（元数据未标注录用）

在 856 张 MIMIC-CXR 胸片上做 15408 次评测，发现 61.9%–82.3% 输出含幻觉、最高 80.2% 属临床危险错误。

展开中英文摘要与表格信息

中文摘要（原表译文）

视觉语言模型（VLMs）正越来越多地用于医学图像解读，但它们经常产生幻觉，给出临床上看似合理却事实错误的所见，直接威胁患者安全。我们提出 HalluCXR，一个基准，在 856 张分层抽样的 MIMIC-CXR 胸片与三类查询上评测六个架构各异的 VLMs，共产生 15408 次模型评测。一套含临床严重度评级的八类幻觉分类体系与一个两层检测流水线，经 250 条人工标注验证（自动检测 F1=0.959；LLM 评判 F1=0.907）。我们发现 61.9%–82.3% 的输出含幻觉，其中高达 80.2% 属临床危险错误。由此浮现三个关键规律：正常胸片反而招致最严重的幻觉；常见所见被系统性地过度捏造，而罕见所见则被系统性漏检；仅凭回答长度即可预测幻觉风险（AUC 高达 0.908）。六模型集成可将捏造最多降低 84.8%，代价是遗漏增加；一个三模型子集以一半成本保持了相当性能。这些结果确立：幻觉审计、基于冗长度的风险监测以及基于集成的安全层，是临床部署的前提条件。

English abstract

Vision-language models (VLMs) are increasingly used for medical image interpretation, yet they frequently hallucinate, generating clinically plausible but factually incorrect findings that pose direct patient safety risks. We introduce HalluCXR, a benchmark evaluating six architecturally diverse VLMs across 856 stratified MIMIC-CXR chest radiographs and three query types, yielding 15,408 model evaluations. An eight-category hallucination taxonomy with clinical severity ratings and a two-layer detection pipeline are validated against 250 human annotations (auto-detection F1=0.959; LLM judge F1=0.907). We find that 61.9--82.3% of outputs contain hallucinations, with clinically dangerous errors in up to 80.2%. Three key patterns emerge: normal radiographs paradoxically attract the most severe hallucinations, common findings are systematically over-fabricated while rare findings go under-detected, and response length alone predicts hallucination risk (AUC up to 0.908). A six-model ensemble reduces fabrication by up to 84.8% at the cost of increased omission; a three-model subset retains comparable performance at half the cost. These results establish that hallucination auditing, verbosity-based risk monitoring, and ensemble-based safety layers are prerequisites for clinical deployment.

完整作者
Haoyu Wang, Zitong Li

申博清单 #05 · 图像侧评测基准

Evaluating Object Hallucination in Large Vision-Language Models

申博清单 #18 · 缓解方法 · 解码期

ICD

Mitigating Hallucinations in Large Vision-Language Models with Instruction Contrastive Decoding

Wang, Xintong, Pan, Jingheng, Ding, Liang et al.
2024 · arXiv 2403.18715

原表研究关联：从指令扰动角度做对比解码，说明幻觉同时受视觉与指令先验驱动，可用于设计多扰动源的对比方案。

与申博研究计划的关联

从指令扰动角度做对比解码，说明幻觉同时受视觉与指令先验驱动，可用于设计多扰动源的对比方案。

原表标题
Mitigating Hallucinations in Large Vision-Language Models with Instruction Contrastive Decoding（ICD）
年份 / 来源（原表）
2024 · arXiv 2403.18715

安装沉浸式翻译扩展后，打开本篇 PDF，点击浏览器扩展图标 → “翻译 PDF”，在其阅读器中选择中文及翻译服务。
